### [1] [A counterexample to a conjecture of Thakur on Carlitz-Wieferich primes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15305)
*Niedbala Giraudin*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文发现了一个显式的五次c-Wieferich素元（在F_{19^3}上），推翻了此前认为c-Wieferich素元次数必被特征p整除的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Thakur于2015年基于有限数据和低次证明提出猜想：奇特征下每个c-Wieferich素元的次数被p整除。该问题在2024年被重新列为开放问题，Bamunoba和Bergstrom经过大量计算后也相信该结论成立。本文旨在检验这一猜想。

Method: 通过显式构造一个在F_{19^3}上不可约的五次c-Wieferich素元，其中19不整除5，从而给出反例。进一步给出了[5]和M_5的公共因子的闭式表达式（等于μ(T^q - T)，其中μ是F_19上的五次多项式，无平方因子，次数为5*19^3）。完备性证明及发现方法见姊妹论文。

Result: 1. 存在次数为5（不被19整除）的显式c-Wieferich素元，否定了原猜想。2. 5是此类反例的最小可能次数。3. 在素数域F_p的广泛特征和次数范围内，穷举计算未发现反例。

Conclusion: 原猜想在奇特征下不成立，反例存在于扩域而非素数域上。这修正了关于Carlitz模的c-Wieferich素元次数性质的认知。

Abstract: Let A = F_q[T] with q a power of an odd prime p, let [n] = T^(q^n) - T, and let rho be the Carlitz module. A monic prime P of A is a c-Wieferich prime (to base 1) if rho_P(1) = 1 mod P^2. Thakur suggested in 2015, on the basis of limited data and of proofs in degrees 2 and 3, that in odd characteristic every c-Wieferich prime has degree divisible by p; the question was restated as open in 2024, and Bamunoba and Bergstrom, after extensive computations, expressed the belief that the statement holds in odd characteristic. We show that it is false: an explicit irreducible c-Wieferich prime of degree 5 over F_{19^3} is exhibited, with 19 not dividing 5. We further give a closed form for the resulting common factor of [5] and M_5: it equals mu(T^q - T) for an explicit quintic mu with coefficients in the prime field F_19, squarefree of degree 5*19^3. Degree 5 is the least possible degree of such a counterexample, and exhaustive computations show that no counterexample exists over the prime fields F_p in a substantial range of degrees and characteristics. Proofs of the completeness statements, and the method by which the example was found, appear in a companion paper.

</details>


### [2] [The Second Term for Strongly 2-Primitive Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15306)
*Przemek Chojecki*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了集合A⊆[1,n]的最大可能大小F(n)，其中任意三个元素a,b,c满足：若a不在{b,c}中，则a不整除bc。证明了F(n)=π(n)+(27/2+o(1))n^{2/3}/(log n)^2，确定了Erdős猜想的二阶常数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定Erdős关于无整除三元组集合最大规模的猜想中的二阶常数，该问题源于对乘法基中前导常数的研究。

Method: 上界利用乘法基结构保持前导常数；下界通过适当边着色构造尺度分离的素数三元组。

Result: 证明了F(n)=π(n)+(27/2+o(1))n^{2/3}/(log n)^2，其中π(n)为素数计数函数。

Conclusion: 完整确定了Erdős猜想中的二阶常数，解决了该组合数论问题。

Abstract: Let $F(n)$ be the largest size of a set $A\subseteq[1,n]$ such that $a\nmid bc$ whenever $a,b,c\in A$ and $a\notin\{b,c\}$, with $b$ and $c$ allowed to coincide. We prove \[
  F(n)=π(n)+\left(\frac{27}{2}+o(1)\right)\frac{n^{2/3}}{(\log n)^2}. \] This determines the second-order constant conjectured by Erdős; the upper bound keeps the leading constants in his multiplicative basis, while the lower bound packs scale-separated prime triples by proper edge-colourings.

</details>


### [3] [A weighted entropy approach for the quadratic inverse large sieve conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15311)
*Ernie Croot,Chi Hoi Yip*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究二次逆大筛问题，将Hanson定理中A包含二次像中元素的下界从≫ log N大幅提升到exp(c√(log N)/log log N)，并证明了密度依赖变体及双集合版本，结合熵方法和筛估计证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逆大筛问题预测在平方根阈值处达到最优的例子本质上是二次的。Hanson首次证明了无条件结果，但下界较弱（≫ log N）。本文旨在显著改进该下界，并建立密度依赖的变体，包括与Green-Harper的稳健逆大筛猜想及逆Goldbach问题相关的双集合版本。

Method: 将加权熵论证与筛估计相结合，受Croot-Mao-Pohoata-Sheffer-Yip近期工作的启发。通过调整熵权重和筛参数，得到更强的下界估计。

Result: 将A中包含在单个二次像中元素的下界从≫ log N改进到exp(c√(log N)/log log N)。同时证明了密度依赖的变体，包括双集合版本。结合Elsholtz-Harper定理，表明任何素数的假设分解都会迫使两个被加数与二次像有大的交集。

Conclusion: 本文显著改进了二次逆大筛问题的下界，证明了更强的无条件结果，并为逆大筛猜想和逆Goldbach问题提供了新的理论支持。

Abstract: The quadratic inverse large sieve problem predicts that the examples sharp at the square-root threshold are essentially quadratic. Hanson proved the first unconditional result in this direction: if $A\subseteq[N]$, $|A|\gg\sqrt N$, and $|A_p|\le p/2+O(1)$ for every prime $p$, then $A$ contains $\gg\log N$ elements in the image of a single quadratic. We significantly improve this lower bound to \[
  \exp\left(c\frac{\sqrt{\log N}}{\log\log N}\right). \] We also prove density-dependent variants, including a two-set version motivated by Green--Harper's robust inverse large sieve conjectures and their connection with the inverse Goldbach problem. Combined with a theorem of Elsholtz--Harper on hypothetical decompositions of the primes, our results show that any such decomposition would force both summands to have large intersections with quadratic images. Our proof combines a weighted entropy argument with sieve estimates, inspired by the recent work of Croot--Mao--Pohoata--Sheffer--Yip.

</details>


### [4] [Finiteness of the Tate-Shafarevich group over function fields for groups of multiplicative type](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15372)
*Igor A. Rapinchuk,Avinash Roy*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了在函数域K（由特征0域k上的光滑几何整簇X得到，dim≥2）上，对于k-定义的多乘型群D，其Tate-Shafarevich群Sha(D,V)在两种情形下有限：(1) k是有限生成域且X(k)≠∅；(2) k是数域。该结果将Harari-Szamuely关于曲线情形的结论推广到了高维。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Harari和Szamuely关于曲线情形（dim X=1）的Tate-Shafarevich群有限性结果推广到高维簇（dim X≥2），研究多乘型群在函数域上的Galois上同调性质。

Method: 利用多乘型群的结构性质，结合域k的算术性质（有限生成域或数域），通过上同调方法分析H^1(K,D)到各完备化局部域上同调群的核的有限性。

Result: 得到了Tate-Shafarevich群Sha(D,V)在两种条件下（k有限生成且X(k)非空，或k为数域）为有限群。

Conclusion: 将Harari-Szamuely的曲线情形结果成功推广到了任意维数≥2的情形，完善了关于函数域上多乘型群Tate-Shafarevich群有限性的理论。

Abstract: Let $K = k(X)$ be the function field of a smooth geometrically integral variety $X$ of dimension $\geq 2$ over a field $k$ of characteristic 0 and $V$ be the set of discrete valuations of $K$ associated with the prime divisors on $X$. We show that if $D$ is a $k$-defined group of multiplicative type, then the corresponding Tate-Shafarevich group $Sha(D,V) = \ker \left(H^1(K,D) \to \prod_{v \in V} H^1(K_v, D) \right)$ is finite in the following situations: (1) $k$ is finitely generated and $X(k) \neq \emptyset$; (2) $k$ is a number field. This complements previous work of Harari and Szamuely, which considered the case where $X$ is a curve.

</details>


### [5] [Sets of unit fractions without two members whose average is a unit fraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15419)
*Will Sawin*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明存在常数c>0，使得对任意足够大的N，存在大小为>cN的子集A⊆{1,…,N}，其中任意两不同元素a,b的调和平均数1/(1/a+1/b)不是单位分数，否定回答了Erdős和Graham的一个问题，并给出了无非平凡三项等差数列的单位分数集合最大大小的最新下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 否定回答Erdős和Graham关于单位分数调和平均数性质的经典问题，并改进单位分数集合中无三项等差数列的下界。

Method: 通过构造大小为>cN的子集A⊆{1,…,N}，证明其中任意两不同元素a,b满足1/(1/a+1/b)不是单位分数。

Result: 存在常数c>0，使得对任意足够大N，存在大小为>cN的满足条件的子集A。

Conclusion: 否定回答了Erdős和Graham的问题，并给出了无三项等差数列的单位分数集合最大大小的最优下界。

Abstract: We show that there is a constant $c>0$ such that, for all sufficiently large $N$, there is a subset $A \subseteq \{1,\dots,N\}$ of size $>cN$ such that for any two distinct elements $a,b$ in $A$, the average of $\frac{1}{a}$ and $\frac{1}{b}$ is not a unit fraction, negatively answering a question of Erdős and Graham. This also gives the best known lower bounds on the maximum size of a set of unit fractions without non-trivial three-term arithmetic progressions.

</details>


### [6] [Indecomposable translates and degree-$d$ points](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15451)
*Alexander Galarraga*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究数域上曲线的度数-d点存在性条件，证明了$\mathbb{P}^1$-孤立且$AV$-参数化的点仅来自满足“不可分解”性质的阿贝尔平移，并将其应用于一族亏格-7曲线，否定了Viray和Vogt的一个问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在刻画数域上曲线具有无穷多度数-d点的充要条件，并解决Viray和Vogt提出的关于阿贝尔平移可分解性的问题。

Method: 利用$\mathbb{P}^1$-参数化与$AV$-参数化点的分类，引入“不可分解”性质刻画$\mathbb{P}^1$-孤立$AV$-参数化点的来源，并通过代数闭包下有效阿贝尔平移的可分解性分析应用于具体曲线族。

Result: 证明了$\mathbb{P}^1$-孤立$AV$-参数化点仅来自满足“不可分解”性质的阿贝尔平移；对一族亏格-7曲线，证明了度数5上的每个有效阿贝尔平移在代数闭包下都是可分解的。

Conclusion: 给出了度数-d点存在性的刻画，并对Viray和Vogt的问题给出了否定回答。

Abstract: A curve over a number field has infinitely many degree-$d$ points if and only if there exists a degree-$d$ $\mathbb{P}^1$- or $AV$-parametrized point. We show that a $\mathbb{P}^1$-isolated $AV$-parametrized point arises only from an abelian translate satisfying a certain property, which we term ``indecomposable". We apply our results to a family of genus-7 curves for which we can show that every effective abelian translate in degree 5 is decomposable over the algebraic closure, giving a negative answer to a question of Viray and Vogt.

</details>


### [7] [On the existence of Newman and Littlewood multiples for certain integer polynomials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15520)
*Musbahu Idris,Jean-Marc Sac-Épée*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了Newman多项式和Littlewood多项式的存在性问题。对于Newman倍数，通过筛选已知小Mahler测度多项式数据库，发现了14个不可约多项式（最小Mahler测度为1.263095875491...）没有Newman倍数，从而将Hare-Mossinghoff问题中sigma的上界限定为该值。对于Littlewood倍数，解决了Drungilas等人提出的三个Newman多项式的最小次数Littlewood倍数的存在性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究是否存在实数sigma>1，使得所有无非负实根且Mahler测度小于sigma的整数多项式都有Newman倍数（Hare-Mossinghoff问题），以及解决特定Newman多项式的最小次数Littlewood倍数的存在性问题。

Method: 利用已知小Mahler测度多项式数据库（最高次数200），对每个多项式p(x)及其符号变换p(-x)进行测试。先通过混合整数线性优化问题进行次数有界预过滤，再对剩余候选者应用Hare-Mossinghoff认证程序。对于Littlewood倍数问题，直接检验三个Newman多项式在最小可能次数下是否存在Littlewood倍数。

Result: 发现了14个不可约多项式，它们无非负实根且无Newman倍数，Mahler测度均小于1.3，最小值为1.263095875491...。对于Littlewood倍数，三个Newman多项式中有一个达到了最小次数，另外两个在头两个可能次数下均无Littlewood倍数。

Conclusion: 将Hare-Mossinghoff问题中sigma的上界降低到约1.2631，并确定了三个特定Newman多项式的Littlewood倍数存在性。

Abstract: Newman polynomials have coefficients in {0, 1} and constant term 1, whereas Littlewood polynomials have coefficients in {-1, 1}. We study two questions concerning the existence of Newman and Littlewood multiples for certain integer polynomials. For Newman multiples, we revisit a question of Hare and Mossinghoff [6]: whether there exists a real number sigma > 1 such that every P in Z[x] with no nonnegative real root and Mahler measure less than sigma has a Newman multiple. To bound any possible value of sigma from above, we seek polynomials with no nonnegative real root and no Newman multiple, and with Mahler measure as small as possible. Using the updated database of known small-Mahler-measure polynomials up to degree 200 [11], we test, for each listed polynomial p(x), both p(x) and its sign transform p(-x). These two polynomials have the same Mahler measure, but the existence of a Newman multiple is not invariant under the substitution x -> x. After a degree-bounded prefilter formulated as a mixed-integer linear optimization problem, we apply the Hare-Mossinghoff certification procedure to the remaining candidates. This yields 14 irreducible polynomials of Mahler measure less than 1.3, with no nonnegative real root and no Newman multiple. The smallest of their Mahler measures is 1.263095875491..., showing that any such sigma is at most this value. For Littlewood multiples, we return to the three Newman polynomials listed in Table 3 of Drungilas, Jankauskas, Junevicius, Klebonas and Siurys [3], for which the existence of a Littlewood multiple of smallest possible degree was left unresolved. We show that one of them attains this degree, whereas the other two have no Littlewood multiple at either of their first two possible degrees.

</details>


### [8] [Improved bounds for multiplicative functions in almost all short intervals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15574)
*Siddarth Menon*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文改进了Matomäki-Radziwiłł方法在短区间乘性函数平均值上的应用，通过对筛法误差的更精细处理（引入更宽分离的素数区间限制典型因子分解），对Liouville函数和平滑数支撑乘性函数给出了本质最优的衰减界，改进了平均Chowla猜想的界，并讨论了方法的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 进一步优化Matomäki-Radziwiłł方法对短区间乘性函数平均值的估计精度，特别是在Liouville函数和平滑数乘性函数上达到该方法的理论最优衰减界，并改进平均Chowla猜想的现有结果。

Method: 对Matomäki-Radziwiłł方法进行精细化：引入更宽分离的最终素数范围（a more widely separated final prime range）来限制整数具有典型因子分解，从而更尖锐地处理筛法误差项。

Result: 对于Liouville函数和平滑数支撑的乘性函数，证明了在Matomäki-Radziwiłł方法框架下本质最优的衰减界；对任意1-有界乘性函数给出了较弱但仍有改进的界；改进了Matomäki-Radziwiłł-Tao的平均Chowla猜想界，使其在该方法下接近最优。

Conclusion: 通过更精细的筛法误差处理，Matomäki-Radziwiłł方法在特定乘性函数（如Liouville函数）上已达到本质最优；但对于一般1-有界乘性函数改进有限，存在方法固有的局限性。

Abstract: We refine the Matomäki-Radziwiłł method for short averages of multiplicative functions. For the Liouville function and multiplicative functions supported on smooth numbers, we prove decay bounds that are essentially optimal with the Matomäki-Radziwiłł method. The key new ingredient is a sharper treatment of the sieve error, achieved by introducing a more widely separated final prime range when restricting to integers with typical factorizations. We additionally give a weaker but still improved bound for arbitrary 1-bounded multiplicative functions and discuss some limitations of the method. As an application, we give an improved bound for the averaged Chowla conjecture of Matomäki-Radziwiłł-Tao that seems essentially best possible via their method.

</details>


### [9] [Arithmetic unlikely intersections in powers of the multiplicative group](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15741)
*Francesco Campagna,Gabriel Andreas Dill,Robert Wilms*

TL;DR: 本文受Bugeaud-Corvaja-Zannier工作启发，提出了关于数域整数环上乘法群幂中非显然交集的猜想：若与子群概形的交集因维度原因非显然，则其“大小”不应超出子群概形“复杂度”太多。作者获得了非显然交集的一些结果（推广了Barroero-Capuano-Mérai-Ostafe-Sha的工作），证明了1维猜想成立（源于Corvaja-Zannier），得到了2维的部分结果，并提出了一些作为猜想特例的开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受到Bugeaud-Corvaja-Zannier关于不可约整交的工作启发，作者试图在数域整数环上乘法群幂的框架下，系统性地猜想并量化非显然交集（unlikely intersections）的“大小”与子群概形“复杂度”之间的制约关系。

Method: 作者首先在非显然交集情形（即维度足够大使得交集可能发生）下建立结果，作为后续猜想的基准；然后提出一般猜想，并分别在1维（利用Corvaja-Zannier定理）、2维（部分结果）及特例开放问题中加以验证或讨论。

Result: 证明了1维猜想成立，获得了2维的部分结果，并推广了Barroero-Capuano-Mérai-Ostafe-Sha在非显然交集方面的已有工作。

Conclusion: 该猜想为理解数域整数环上乘法群幂中的非显然交集提供了统一框架，1维完全解决，2维及更高维度仍有待深入，所提开放问题为进一步研究指明了方向。

Abstract: Inspired by work of Bugeaud-Corvaja-Zannier, we formulate a conjecture about unlikely intersections in powers of the multiplicative group over the ring of integers in a number field. Broadly speaking, if an intersection with a subgroup scheme is unlikely for dimension reasons, its ``size" should not be too big compared to the ``complexity" of the subgroup scheme. We first obtain some results on likely intersections that serve as a benchmark for the unlikely case and generalize work of Barroero-Capuano-Mérai-Ostafe-Sha. We then show that our conjecture in dimension $1$ follows from work of Corvaja-Zannier, we obtain some partial result in dimension $2$, and we present some open problems that are special cases of the conjecture.

</details>


### [10] [A comment on the number of $k$-th powers inside arithmetic progressions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15895)
*Saša Novaković*

Method: 观察到Bourgain-Demeter的论证在步长相对于级数长度增长不过快时仍然有效，由此进行直接推广。

Conclusion: 虽然这两个结果应是已知的，但本文仍将其补充到文献中。

Abstract: In \cite{BD} Bourgain and Demeter found sharp upper bounds for the number of $k$-th powers inside arbitrary arithmetic progressions whose step has $O(1)$ many divisors. We make the easy observation that the same arguments are still valid if the step does not grow too rapidly in relation to the length of the progression. Furthermore, we give sharp bounds for the number of $k$-th powers among the first $N$ terms for $N$ large enough. Both results should be known. Nevertheless, we add to the literature.

</details>


### [11] [Upper bounds for moments of analytic ranks of elliptic curves over number fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15998)

TL;DR: 本文证明了数域上椭圆曲线解析秩矩的条件上界，并给出了解析秩大于给定边界之椭圆曲线密度的条件界。

Abstract: Let $\mathcal O$ be a special nilpotent orbit in the Lie algebra $\mathfrak g$ of a simple algebraic group $G$. We give two proofs of the result that every special piece ${\mathcal P}(\mathcal O)$ in $\mathfrak g$ is the quotient of a smooth $G$-variety $X$ by the action of a certain finite group $H$. We first deduce the result from a similar result for transverse slices, established in earlier work of the first three authors and Fu. Then we give a more explicit construction of $X$, as a subvariety of the closure of a $G$-orbit in the direct sum of $\mathfrak g$ and some fundamental weight representations of $G$. Both methods apply to classical $\mathfrak g$, where we give new proofs of this result, which was first proved by Kraft and Procesi. The result in the exceptional groups was conjectured by Lusztig. Our first proof shows that there can be several $G$-varieties $X$ that satisfy the conjecture, related to a natural embedding of $H$ in the fundamental group of $\mathcal O$. In an appendix, we relate this natural embedding to Lusztig's definition of $H$ that arises from the family in the Weyl group of $G$ attached to $\mathcal O$ and from the Springer correspondence.

</details>

Abstract: We extend to abelian length categories of finite rank a characterisation of brick-finiteness known for finite-dimensional algebras. Result: 证明了双层整数线性规划在一般整数变量下仍是Σ^p_2-完全的；证明了当上下层变量总数固定时，无需额外假设即可在多项式时间内求解，强化了Köppe等人(2010)的结果。

Abstract: We investigate the computational complexity of bilevel integer linear programming. While Jeroslow~(1985) established that the decision version of this problem is $Σ^p_2$-complete when restricted to binary variables, we prove that this $Σ^p_2$-completeness persists even for general integer variables, settling a question that remained open for over 40 years. Furthermore, we analyze the impact of various structural assumptions on computational complexity. Notably, we strengthen the result of Köppe et al.~(2010) by proving polynomial-time solvability whenever the total number of upper- and lower-level variables is fixed, without any additional assumptions.

</details>

<details>

Motivation: 过程优化模型常包含复杂的互联单元操作、动力学和不确定性，产生大规模非线性规划问题，传统CPU求解器计算负担重。尽管新兴GPU求解器具计算优势，但缺乏GPU兼容的过程建模工具限制了其应用。

Method: 利用现有GPU兼容优化软件栈（ExaModels代数建模系统、MadNLP优化求解器、cuDSS线性求解器）构建GPU兼容的过程优化模型。ExaModels通过暴露重复代数结构以GPU兼容方式表述问题，MadNLP和cuDSS在GPU上求解非线性规划。

Abstract: This paper presents a proof-of-concept workflow for equation-oriented process optimization that runs entirely on a GPU. Process optimization models often incorporate complex interconnected unit operations, dynamics, and uncertainties, resulting in large nonlinear programs that can be computationally demanding for conventional CPU-based solvers. Although emerging GPU-based solvers offer substantial computational benefits, their application to process optimization has been limited by the lack of GPU-compatible process modeling tools. We address this gap by prototyping the GPU-compatible process optimization models using an existing GPU-capable optimization software stack, including ExaModels (algebraic modeling system), MadNLP (optimization solver), and cuDSS (linear solver). ExaModels formulates the process optimization problem in a GPU-compatible way by exposing its repeated algebraic structure, while MadNLP and cuDSS solve the resulting nonlinear program on the GPU. This workflow is demonstrated on a CO2 absorber design problem under feed uncertainty, in which a shared column diameter is minimized subject to equilibrium and hydraulic constraints in all scenarios. For the largest case with 5,000 scenarios and 1.5 million variables, the GPU workflow achieves a speedup of approximately 21\times over a single-threaded CPU baseline using JuMP, Ipopt, and MA57.

</details>

Abstract: We investigate optimal transport distances induced by general Lagrangian action functionals. Extending the classical Monge - Kantorovich and Benamou - Brenier theories, we derive a unified variational framework that connects several equivalent formulations of the induced transport distance, including Lagrangian, Eulerian, convex optimization, Hamilton - Jacobi dual, and Hamiltonian flow formulations. Under standard convexity assumptions on the Lagrangian, we establish the equivalence of these formulations through variational arguments and convex duality. The resulting optimality system reveals a natural Hamiltonian structure on the Wasserstein space, providing a direct link between optimal transport, Hamiltonian dynamics, and optimal control. The proposed framework extends the classical quadratic-cost theory to general Lagrangian costs and offers a unified perspective for the analysis of transport metrics generated by action functionals.

Abstract: Fractional gradient descent (FGD) incorporates long-range memory through Caputo-type operators and has been shown to improve stability in ill-conditioned and nonconvex optimization problems. Abstract: We consider the Burgers equation with a scalar control acting through a fixed spatial profile, in a regime where the linearized system is not controllable. Abstract: We address the problem of optimally placing a limited number of sensors to reconstruct high-dimensional signals without knowledge of the underlying dynamics. Abstract: We introduce a Monte-Carlo Tree Search solver for SAT and XSAT that pairs WalkSAT-style rollouts with a two-thread symmetry-breaking initialisation: one thread starts from all-true, the other from all-false, bounding each thread's initial Hamming distance to a satisfying assignment by $\lfloor n/2 \rfloor$. We formulate LCCP as a set partitioning model and solve it using an exact branch-price-and-cut approach. Abstract: Urban transit electrification requires balancing energy efficiency, schedule reliability, and ride comfort for electric buses (EBs), particularly when interacting with transit-priority adaptive signals in congested networks. A structured reward combines guidance and event-based reinforcement to align EB arrivals with green opportunities while considering energy, time, comfort, and safety. The framework uses centralized training and decentralized execution (CTDE) with parameter sharing, allowing a single DRL agent to learn from multiple buses and routes using local observations. Experiments on a real-world corridor show that PriEco-DRL reduces EB energy consumption while maintaining network efficiency and transit priority compared with fixed-time, actuated, and rule-based signal-vehicle coordination baselines. Energy- and trajectory-based analyses reveal that the improvements stem from fewer unscheduled stop-start events and smoother speed regulation under adaptive signals. The results highlight a tunable energy-time trade-off, allowing flexible operational choices through reward weighting.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 在二维欧氏空间 ℝ² 中，针对凸 Lipschitz 目标函数，采用有限视界 n 和常数步长 η=Θ(1/√n) 的次梯度下降法，分析最后一轮迭代的收敛性。

Result: 最后一轮迭代的优化误差为 O(1/√n)，即达到与平均迭代相同的收敛速率，无需额外的对数因子。

Conclusion: 在二维情形下，次梯度方法的最后迭代即可达到最优收敛速率，高维中出现的对数因子在平面上是不必要的。

Abstract: We study the last iterate of the subGradient Method (sGM) for convex Lipschitz objectives defined on $\mathbb R^2$. We prove that for a finite horizon $n$ and a constant stepsize $η= Θ(1/\sqrt{n})$, the last iterate achieves an optimization error of order $1/\sqrt{n}$, showing that the extra $\log{n}$ factor appearing in high dimensions is unnecessary in the plane. This makes progress on an open problem posed by Koren and Segal in 2020 on the optimal error of the last iterate of sGM in fixed dimension.

</details>


### [32] [Optimal control of symmetry-breaking dynamics near criticality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16188)
*Pearson W. Miller*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了动力系统在叉式分岔附近的最优控制问题，通过Pontryagin最大值原理的渐近展开，分析了三种控制强度标度下的最优控制律，推导了弱控制和中等控制区间的广义振幅方程，并在双稳态生化开关模型中验证了结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受外部信号引导细胞命运选择等自然过程中对称性破缺转变的启发，研究动力系统在叉式分岔附近的最优控制问题。

Method: 利用Pontryagin最大值原理进行最优性条件的渐近展开，分析了三种动力学区域（强、弱、中间控制）的最优控制律，并在弱控制区使用边界层方法构造长时间渐近解。

Result: 强控制极限下结果退化为线性二次型控制的已知近似；推导了弱控制和中间控制区间的广义振幅方程；在双稳态生化开关模型中验证了控制规范形的有效性；分析了弱控制区最优控制问题的分岔结构。

Conclusion: 建立了叉式分岔附近动力系统最优控制的渐近理论框架，揭示了不同控制强度标度下的最优控制律，为理解外部信号引导对称性破缺转变提供了理论工具。

Abstract: We study the problem of optimal control for dynamical systems near a pitchfork bifurcation, motivated by the role of external cues in guiding symmetry-breaking transitions in cell-fate selection and other natural processes. Using an asymptotic expansion of the optimality conditions obtained from the Pontryagin maximum principle, the leading-order optimal control law for a general n-dimensional system is examined across three dynamical regimes distinguished by scaling of control strength with respect to the distance from criticality. While in the strong control limit the results reduce to known approximations from linear-quadratic control, we derive generalized amplitude equations for the co-evolution of state and costate variables describing the optimized trajectory in the weak and intermediate control regimes. These control normal forms are validated against numerical solutions of the full optimal control problem for a canonical model of a bistable biochemical switch. The bifurcation structure of the optimal control problem is analyzed in the weak control regime. Finally, we demonstrate the construction of asymptotic solutions in the long time limit in this regime using boundary-layer methods.

</details>

### [33] [Xiaomi-Robotics-1: Scaling Vision-Language-Action Models with over 100K Hours of Real-World Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15330)
*Xiaomi Robotics Team,Jun Guo,Piaopiao Jin,Jason Li,Peiyan Li,Yingyan Li,Futeng Liu,Wanli Peng,Optimus Qin,Yifei Su,Nan Sun,Qiao Sun,Runze Suo,Heyun Wang,Yunhong Wang,Rujie Wu,Caoyu Xia,Lina Zhang,Jack Zhao,Guoliang Chen,Wenlong Chen,Xinze He,Bin Li,Qing Li,Zhuorong Li,Heng Qu,Wenxuan Song,Diyun Xiang,Yifan Xie,Peiran Xu,Hangjun Ye,Wen Ye,Han Zhao,Quanyun Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出小米机器人基础模型Xiaomi-Robotics-1，一种视觉-语言-动作(VLA)基础模型，通过两阶段训练（预训练+后训练），能够零样本执行多种移动操作任务并高效适应新任务，在多个基准上达到SOTA，其中RoboCasa365成功率达57.6%，RoboDojo平均分达20.07。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人操作模型在泛化到未见环境和新任务时能力有限，且缺乏高效适应下游任务的训练策略。本文旨在构建一个兼具广泛泛化能力和高效微调能力的机器人基础策略模型。

Method: 提出两阶段训练方案：(1) 预训练阶段，利用UMI设备采集的超过10万小时真实世界操作轨迹，结合可扩展的自动标注流水线（用自然语言描述场景状态转移）进行训练，赋予模型广泛的动作生成能力；(2) 后训练阶段，对齐模型能力与机器人具体形态以及人类自然使用的指令式提示。

Result: 实验表明模型具有强扩展性，性能随数据规模和模型大小持续提升。在RoboCasa365上达到57.6%成功率（超越此前最好46.6%），在RoboDojo上平均分20.07（此前SOTA为13.07）。在多个模拟基准上均超越现有方法。

Conclusion: Xiaomi-Robotics-1是一个强大的机器人基础策略模型，具备零样本泛化和高效微调双重能力，建立了新的SOTA基准，展示了良好的规模扩展行为。

Abstract: We present Xiaomi-Robotics-1, a foundational vision-language-action (VLA) model capable of (1) following diverse language instructions to perform a wide range of mobile manipulation tasks in unseen environments out-of-the-box, and (2) efficiently adapting to novel downstream tasks with minimal fine-tuning data. We propose a two-stage training recipe consisting of pre-training and post-training. During pre-training, we imbue the model with broad and generalizable action-generation capabilities by training on over 100k hours of real-world manipulation trajectories collected via UMI devices. Crucially, we develop a scalable auto-labeling pipeline that annotates trajectory clips with natural languages describing scene state transitions, providing rich and precise conditioning for action learning. During post-training, we aim to align these capabilities with robot embodiments and imperative instructions that humans naturally use to prompt robots. Extensive experiments demonstrate strong scaling behavior. Xiaomi-Robotics-1 consistently improves with increased data scales and model sizes during pre-training. This scaling behavior directly transfers to post-training, where a stronger pre-training model yields better out-of-the-box real-robot performance in unseen environments. Furthermore, Xiaomi-Robotics-1 serves as a strong robot foundation policy that can be efficiently fine-tuned on complex, dexterous tasks with high data efficiency. Across multiple simulation benchmarks, Xiaomi-Robotics-1 outperforms state-of-the-art methods. Notably, it establishes a new state-of-the-art with a 57.6% success rate on RoboCasa365, surpassing the previous best of 46.6%. Furthermore, it achieves an average score of 20.07 on RoboDojo, significantly outperforming the prior state-of-the-art (13.07). Code and model checkpoints will be released. Project page: https://robotics.xiaomi.com/xiaomi-robotics-1.html

</details>


### [34] [NeuroCommitSSM: Decision-Centric Shared Autonomy for Safe Assistive Manipulation via EEG-EMG-ET Commit Readiness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15395)
*Tipu Sultan,Param Sangani,Kody Cool,Pascal Sikorski,Guangping Liu,Hadi Akbarpour,Madi Babaiasl*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: NeuroCommitSSM是一个面向辅助机器人操作的决策框架，通过融合脑电图、肌电图和眼动追踪信号预测持续的执行就绪度评分，并利用三状态有限状态机（HOLD-ASSIST-COMMIT）在执行前进行可行性检查（目标可见性、逆运动学可解性、无碰撞规划），在32名受试者的日常生活任务实验中达到0.950的平衡精度和极低的误触发率，且在传感器缺失条件下显著优于基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有辅助机器人操作框架主要关注"执行什么"而非"何时执行"，缺乏安全的人机协同控制机制，导致执行决策不稳定和误触发问题，尤其是在传感器信号不可靠或部分缺失时。

Method: 提出NeuroCommitSSM框架：1) 从同步EEG、EMG和眼动追踪信号预测连续的执行就绪度分数c_t∈[0,1]；2) 通过驻留和迟滞滤波将其转换为离散提交事件；3) 设计HOLD-ASSIST-COMMIT (HAC)三状态有限状态机，在执行前同时要求神经模型持续的执行就绪信号和实时感知/机器人状态可行性（目标可见性、IK可解性、无碰撞规划）。采用LOSO交叉验证和7种传感器脱落场景评估。

Result: 在32名受试者5项ADL任务中，NeuroCommitSSM达到0.950动作平衡精度，每1000个REST窗口仅0.75次误触发；在仅EEG条件下达到0.785平衡精度和0.29 FP/1k REST，而TCN基线为99.95 FP/1k REST；HIL验证表明可行性检查在不牺牲任务成功率的前提下减少了误触发和决策不稳定性。

Conclusion: 基于决策就绪度的神经符号框架结合多模态神经信号和机器人可行性检查，能够实现安全、稳定的辅助机器人提交-执行控制，显著优于纯深度学习基线，尤其适用于传感器缺失的鲁棒场景。

Abstract: We present NeuroCommitSSM, a decision-centric framework that models when to execute, not just what to do, for safe commit-to-execute control in assistive robotic manipulation. NeuroCommitSSM predicts a continuous commit-readiness score c_t in [0,1] from synchronized electroencephalography (EEG), electromyography (EMG), and eye-tracking (ET), and converts it into discrete commit events through dwell and hysteresis filtering. A three-state finite-state supervisor, HOLD-ASSIST-COMMIT (HAC), gates execution by requiring both a sustained commit-readiness signal from the neural model and real-time perception and robot-state feasibility, including target visibility, inverse kinematics solvability, and collision-free planning, before initiating motion. We evaluate the framework on N=32 subjects performing five activities of daily living (ADL) tasks aligned with the International Classification of Functioning, Disability and Health (ICF), using leave-one-subject-out (LOSO) cross-validation and seven sensor-dropout scenarios (S0-S6). NeuroCommitSSM achieves 0.950 action-balanced accuracy with 0.75 false commit events per 1000 REST windows (FP/1k REST), and maintains low false commits and stable state transitions under sensor loss. For example, in the EEG-only condition, it achieves 0.785 balanced accuracy and 0.29 FP/1k REST, whereas the Temporal Convolutional Network baseline produces 99.95 FP/1k REST under the same condition. Hardware-in-the-loop (HIL) validation on a Kinova Gen3 arm shows that feasibility-checked execution reduces false starts and decision instability without sacrificing task success. Supplementary materials, including code, datasets, videos, and additional analyses, are available at https://madibabaiasl.github.io/NeuroCommitSSM/.

</details>

*Harshit Thakker,Paul Dela Cruz,Mostafa Mo. Massoud,Jacqueline Libby*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于两部分浇铸的软体气动执行器稳健、可重复且可扩展的制造流程，包括防止内腔堵塞、气密密封以及柔性传感器嵌入方法，并通过有限元建模、PID压力控制实验和自动图像处理进行了验证。该方法经两名操作员24次成功制造验证，具有高度可重复性。​


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体机器人在康复和手术等领域具有优势，但可重复和可扩展的制造挑战限制了其实际部署。现有制造方法多依赖人工、易出错，因此需要一种稳健、可重复且可扩展的制造方法。​

Method: 采用两部分浇铸法制造软体气动执行器，设计了防止内腔堵塞和气密密封的工艺；嵌入薄膜柔性传感器；进行有限元建模分析应力与变形；通过PID压力控制进行气动驱动实验；利用自动图像处理校准柔性传感器与弯曲角度之间的关系。​

Result: 两名操作员24次成功制造验证了方法的可重复性；阶梯输入角度响应实验显示出可重复性能；正弦输入实验显示与粘弹性响应一致的小滞后；仿真与真实弯曲角度响应具有可比性。​

Conclusion: 提出的制造流程具有可重复性和稳健性，结合基准仿真与实验测试，有望推动软体机器人的更广泛应用。​

Abstract: Soft robots are well-suited for applications such as rehabilitation and surgery that require adaptable and safe interaction with their environment. However, the challenges of reproducible and scalable fabrication of soft robots limit their real-world deployment. Various fabrication methods have been introduced, but many are labor-intensive and prone to human error. Therefore, traditional two-part pour casting remains an attractive option. This paper presents procedures for robust, repeatable, and scalable fabrication of soft pneumatic actuators using two-part pour casting. The presented methods prevent internal cavity clogging and ensure air-tight sealing. Additionally, a robust sensor embedding procedure for thin-film flex sensors is presented, which allows for accurate and repeatable data acquisition. Finite Element Modeling (FEM) of the soft actuator is performed to analyze stress and deformation from internal pressure loadings. Pneumatic actuation experiments with PID pressure control are performed. Automated image processing is used to calibrate the embedded flex sensor to bending angle measurements. Staircase and sinusoidal profile actuation experiments validate the performance of the fabricated actuator. Angle response experiments for the staircase input show repeatable performance, and the sinusoidal input shows a small amount of hysteresis consistent with viscoelastic response to pneumatic actuation of soft actuators. Simulated and real-world bending angles show comparable response. These methods provide a repeatable and robust fabrication procedure, validated across two operators and 24 successful fabrications, along with benchmark simulations and experimental testing. These benchmarks will enable more widespread adoption of soft robotics.

</details>


### [36] [VTAP Gripper: Synergizing Fingertip Sensing and a Visuo-Tactile Active Palm for Dexterous In-Hand Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15448)
*Yuhao Zhou,Sheeraz Athar,Zhixian Hu,Binghao Huang,Yunzhu Li,Juan Wachs,Yu She*

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种集成视觉-触觉主动手掌（VTAP）与可重构触觉手指的触觉反应式抓取器，通过结构化手指-手掌协同与多模态感知实现鲁棒抓取和精细操作，并提出了分阶段手势条件重定向框架用于灵巧遥操作，实验验证了其在多种挑战性任务中的高效性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有灵巧抓取器往往追求高自由度拟人化设计，导致结构复杂、成本高，且难以在抓取鲁棒性与精细操作能力之间取得平衡。本文旨在通过结构化手指-手掌协同与多模态感知，在不依赖高自由度拟人设计的前提下，实现高效的抓取与操作性能。

Method: 设计了一种包含视觉-触觉主动手掌（VTAP）和可重构触觉手指的抓取器；手掌采用双模态驱动，结合远距离视觉定位与接触式触觉反馈；为弥合人手运动与异构三指结构之间的具身差距，提出了分阶段、手势条件化的重定向框架用于灵巧遥操作。

Result: 在多种挑战性任务中验证了系统性能：YCB物体及易碎物体的反应式抓取、注射器针筒在手中的重新定向与活塞操作、直径小至3毫米的团簇物体分离、以及视觉-触觉融合的销孔插入任务。

Conclusion: 通过协调的手指-手掌交互与多模态感知，无需高自由度拟人化设计即可实现高操作性能；VTAP抓取器及其重定向框架为灵巧抓取器设计、操作及基于学习的接触丰富数据收集提供了实用的参考架构。

Abstract: This paper presents a tactile-reactive gripper that integrates a Visuo-Tactile Active Palm (VTAP) and compliant, reconfigurable fingers equipped with tactile array sensors. The design exploits structured finger-palm synergy and multi-modal perception to achieve both robust grasping and fine manipulation. The actuated bi-modal palm seamlessly combines long-range visual localization with contact-rich tactile feedback, substantially extending the system's manipulation capability. To bridge the embodiment gap between human hand motion and the heterogeneous three-finger structure, we further propose a staged, gesture-conditioned retargeting framework for dexterous teleoperation. Extensive experiments validate the system across a range of challenging tasks: reactive grasping of YCB and fragile objects, in-hand syringe reorientation and plunger actuation, singulation of clustered objects down to 3 mm in diameter, and vision-tactile peg-in-hole insertion. Results demonstrate that high manipulation performance can be achieved through coordinated finger-palm interaction and multi-modal sensing, without resorting to high degrees of freedom anthropomorphic designs. The VTAP gripper and its retargeting framework offer a practical reference architecture for dexterous gripper design, manipulation, and contact-rich data collection in support of learning-based approaches. Project webpage: https://yuhochau.github.io/vtap/.

</details>


### [37] [Risk-Aware Preference Learning for Stochastic Outcomes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15483)
*Yi-Shiuan Tung,Yuni Wu,Wei Jiang,Alessandro Roncone,Bradley Hayes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究人类风险敏感性对机器人奖励学习的影响，发现基于累积前景理论（CPT）的学习器比基于期望效用（EU）的学习器能更好地从人类偏好中恢复奖励函数，特别是在社交导航等安全关键场景中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有偏好学习大多假设人类按期望效用（EU）线性加权结果概率与效用，但行为证据表明人类具有系统性的风险敏感性，如过度加权小概率负事件和损失厌恶。这种不匹配在安全关键的社交机器人导航中可能导致严重后果，因此需要研究如何建模人类真实的风险偏好。

Method: 在Bradley-Terry偏好学习框架下，对比期望效用（EU）与累积前景理论（CPT）两种人类决策模型。通过初步实验，比较两种模型在风险敏感用户生成的偏好数据上恢复奖励函数的表现。

Result: 当偏好由风险敏感用户生成时，基于CPT的学习器比基于EU的学习器能够恢复出显著更低遗憾（regret）的奖励函数。

Conclusion: 在从随机机器人结果的偏好中学习奖励时，建模人类风险敏感性至关重要。

Abstract: Learning reward functions from human preferences is a widely used approach for aligning robot behavior with user expectations in human-robot interaction. Most existing approaches assume that humans evaluate uncertain outcomes using expected utility (EU), aggregating outcome utilities linearly with their probabilities. However, behavioral evidence shows that humans are systematically risk-sensitive, overweighting rare negative events and exhibiting loss aversion. We study the consequences of this mismatch in social robot navigation, where safety-critical outcomes (e.g., collisions) are rare but highly consequential. We compare EU with Cumulative Prospect Theory (CPT), a nonlinear model of human decision-making, within a Bradley-Terry preference learning framework. Our preliminary experiments show that when preferences are generated by risk-sensitive users, CPT-based learners recover reward functions with substantially lower regret compared to EU-based learners. Our results highlight the importance of modeling human risk sensitivity when learning rewards from preferences over stochastic robot outcomes.

</details>


### [38] [Environment Design for Reliable Shared Autonomy with Probabilistic Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15487)
*Yi-Shiuan Tung,Himanshu Gupta,Gyanig Kumar,Heyang Huang,Bradley Hayes,Alessandro Roncone*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出通过优化工作空间布局来提高共享自主系统中目标推断的可靠性，实验证明优化布局能减少歧义并改善推断性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有共享自主系统大多关注固定环境下的意图推断，忽视了工作空间设计本身对推断难度的影响。本文旨在探索通过环境设计来降低推断不确定性。

Method: 将工作空间布局设计形式化为优化问题，在有界噪声模型下推导概率正确性保证。通过多个桌面场景的仿真实验验证优化布局的效果。

Result: 优化后的布局在目标推断可靠性上优于基线布局，显著减少了歧义。真实共享自主系统集成该框架后同样表现良好。

Conclusion: 环境设计可以作为改善共享自主系统的补充维度，工作空间布局的优化能有效提升目标推断的准确性。

Abstract: Shared autonomy enables humans and robots to collaboratively perform tasks by combining human input with autonomous assistance. Most prior work focuses on improving intent inference under a fixed environment, overlooking how workspace design itself affects inference difficulty. We observe that the physical arrangement of objects directly influences the separability of candidate goals under noisy user inputs. We formulate workspace design as an optimization problem and derive a probabilistic correctness guarantee under a bounded noise model. Through simulation experiments across multiple tabletop scenarios, we show that optimized layouts improve goal inference reliability and reduce ambiguity compared to baseline arrangements. We further demonstrate a real-world shared autonomy system that integrates the proposed inference framework. This highlights the role of environment design as a complementary axis for improving shared autonomy systems.

</details>


### [39] [Multi-Objective Kinodynamic Motion Planning with Asymptotic Pareto Optimality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15508)
*Yusif Razzaq,Anne Theurkauf,Nisar Ahmed,Morteza Lahijanian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对运动动力学约束下的多目标运动规划问题，提出了一个基于改进SST算法的统一框架，包含三种变体：lexSST（词典优化）、coSST（约束优化）和poSST（帕累托前沿逼近），并提供了完备性和最优性的理论保证及实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有成本标量化方法无法在连续域系统中提供正确性保证，亟需一种能够在动力学约束下处理多目标运动规划的统一框架。

Method: 基于稳定稀疏RRT（SST）算法，将每个邻域中维护的单一代表节点替换为局部帕累托最优的代表节点集合，衍生出三种算法分别对应三类多目标优化问题。

Result: 提供了所提算法的完备性和最优性理论保证，并通过广泛实验验证了有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的统一框架能够有效处理运动动力学约束下的多目标运动规划问题，在三种优化范式下均具有理论保障和实际可行性。

Abstract: In this paper, we address the challenge of multi-objective motion planning for systems under kinodynamic constraints. We consider three problem classes: (i) lexicographic optimization, in which objectives are minimized according to a strict priority ordering, (ii) constrained optimization, in which a primary objective is minimized subject to bounds on the remaining costs, and (iii) Pareto front optimization, in which the goal is to approximate the full set of optimal trade-offs among competing objectives. We first show that established cost scalarization methods for multi-objective problems cannot be extended to continuous-domain systems with correctness guarantees. Then, we propose a unified algorithmic framework built upon the Stable Sparse-RRT (SST) algorithm, in which the single representative maintained at each witness neighborhood is replaced by a representative set of locally Pareto-optimal nodes. This structure gives rise to three distinct algorithms: lexSST for lexicographic minimization, coSST for constrained optimization, and poSST for Pareto-front approximation. We provide theoretical guarantees for the completeness and optimality of our algorithms and demonstrate their effectiveness through extensive empirical evaluations.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: PACE框架为人形具身助手部署个性化、可交互、可靠的角色身份建立了可扩展的路径。

Abstract: Equipping humanoid robots with coherent and adaptable personas is crucial for fostering natural, engaging, and trustworthy human-robot interaction (HRI). However, existing approaches often rely on static, hard-coded identities that lack the flexibility to adapt to individual user contexts. In this paper, we present PACE (Persona Adaptation through Conversational Elicitation), a novel framework for the interactive generation and deployment of structured personas on the Ameca humanoid robot. Our system introduces an Interactive Persona Elicitation Pipeline, enabling the robot to dynamically synthesize a tailored, psychologically grounded identity through user Q&A. This elicitation process feeds into a persona prompt compilation phase, generating a structured persona prompt built upon multi-perspective dimensions. We detail the Embodied System Integration required to translate this structured specification into expressive, multimodal humanoid behaviors. Through a comprehensive empirical HRI evaluation, we assess the impact of dynamically generated personas on user trust, perceived anthropomorphism, persona consistency, personal relevance, and interaction quality compared to a generic baseline. These contributions establish a scalable pathway for deploying personalized, interactive, and reliable identities in embodied humanoid assistants. Video demo is available at: https://lipzh5.github.io/PACE/

</details>


### [41] [A Model-Based Decoupling Strategy for Proprioception and Contact Sensing in an Architected Soft Manipulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15582)
*Francesco Stella,Annan Zhang,Cosimo Della Santina,Josie Hughes,Daniela Rus*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于模型的策略，利用流体压力传感器和冗余解析方法，在软体结构机器人中同时实现本体感知和接触检测，通过在单个ITM段上达到0.11±0.02的相对弯曲误差和97%的接触检测率，并成功集成到八段式软体连续机械臂中进行多应用演示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体连续机器人需要嵌入式传感以实现本体感知和接触检测，但将传感器集成到稀疏、高变形结构机器人中仍面临挑战。

Method: 提出基于模型的解耦策略，利用六个流体压力传感器（呈锯齿形局部回路布置于圆周）的过定系统；采用分段常曲率模型将压力映射为形状，通过Huber回归识别残差异常通道以指示外部接触。

Result: 在单个ITH段上：形状估计相对弯曲误差0.11±0.02，178次试验中接触检测率97%。集成八段ITH为Air-Helix软体连续机械臂，完成了触觉示教、导纳控制力调节、触觉物体重建等全臂演示。

Conclusion: 局部流体神经支配结合基于模型的冗余解析是实现软体结构机器人中本体感知与接触传感同步的实用路径。

Abstract: Soft continuum robots require embedded sensing for proprioception and contact detection, yet integrating sensors into sparse, highly deformable architected structures remains challenging. We present a model-based strategy that decouples proprioceptive and contact signals from a common set of fluidic pressure sensors embedded in a soft architected segment. Each segment of the Innervated Trimmed Helicoid (ITH) contains six air channels routed in a localized zigzag pattern along the circumference. With only three principal kinematic degrees of freedom (axial compression, bending in x, bending in y), the six pressure readings form an overdetermined system. A piecewise constant curvature model maps pressures to shape, and Huber regression identifies outlier channels whose residuals indicate external contact. On a single ITH segment, this approach achieves proprioceptive shape estimation with a relative bending error of 0.11 +/- 0.02 and a contact detection rate of 97% across 178 trials. We integrate eight ITH segments into Air-Helix, a tendon-driven soft continuum manipulator, and present exploratory whole-arm demonstrations that include tactile teaching by demonstration, admittance-controlled force regulation, and tactile object reconstruction. The results suggest that localized fluidic innervation combined with model-based redundancy resolution is a practical path toward concurrent proprioception and contact sensing in architected soft robots.

</details>


### [42] [MemoGuard: An Adaptive Runtime for Guarding Against Memory Traps in Communication-Limited Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15589)
*Rajat Bhattacharjya,Hyeonjong Ju,Sing-Yao Wu,Eli Bozorgzadeh,Nikil Dutt*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出MemoGuard，一种轻量级自适应运行时系统，通过在重用前对 episodic memory 进行拓扑、资源和结果契约验证，解决了高相似度但执行无效的"记忆陷阱"问题。在走廊巡检模拟器中，相比纯相似度重用，MemoGuard减少了76.6%的电池安全违规，同时相比始终推理减少了21.4%的fallback调用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通信受限的机器人在灾害巡检、搜救等关键任务中需要可靠的机载决策，但无法访问远程操作员或高容量推理服务。episodic memory重用是一种低成本后备方案，但检索相似度不能保证执行有效性——高相似度的动作可能因拓扑变化、电池余量不足或先前结果不可靠而存在安全隐患，这种"记忆陷阱"导致安全-效率的设计权衡。

Method: MemoGuard是一种轻量级自适应运行时系统，在重用前对episodic memory进行三层契约验证：拓扑契约验证环境结构一致性、资源契约验证电池等资源余量、结果契约验证先前执行结果的可靠性。验证失败时调用fallback本地推理（如llama3.2:3b），验证通过则直接重用记忆。

Result: 在基于图的走廊巡检模拟器中，MemoGuard相比纯相似度top-1重用将电池安全违规减少76.6%，相比始终推理将fallback调用减少21.4%。在NVIDIA Jetson AGX Xavier上以llama3.2:3b作为fallback推理时，每次试验可节省3.67秒和36.97焦耳的fallback推理开销。

Conclusion: MemoGuard通过契约验证机制有效缓解了记忆陷阱问题，在不显著增加计算成本的前提下大幅提升安全性，为通信受限机器人的可靠机载决策提供了一种实用的轻量级解决方案。代码已开源。

Abstract: Communication-limited robots in mission-critical scenarios such as disaster inspection and search-and-rescue must make reliable onboard decisions without access to remote operators or high-capacity reasoning services. Episodic memory reuse is an attractive low-cost fallback, but retrieval similarity does not guarantee execution validity, i.e., a retrieved action may match the current context yet be unsafe due to changed topology, insufficient battery margin, or unreliable prior outcomes. We call such high-similarity but execution-invalid episodes memory traps. This creates a safety-efficiency design space where similarity only reuse minimizes fallback cost but can be unsafe, while always invoking local reasoning improves safety at high computational and energy cost. This paper presents MemoGuard, a lightweight adaptive runtime that validates episodic memories against topology, resource, and outcome contracts before reuse, invoking fallback only when validation fails. In a graph-based corridor-inspection simulator, MemoGuard reduces battery safety violations by 76.6% over similarity-only top-1 reuse while reducing fallback calls by 21.4% over always reasoning. On an NVIDIA Jetson AGX Xavier with local llama3.2:3b fallback reasoning, this corresponds to 3.67 s and 36.97 J of avoided fallback-reasoning overhead per trial. We open-source MemoGuard at https://github.com/hetheiin/memoguard.

</details>


### [43] [AEGIS: Assay-Aware Protocol Validation and Runtime Monitoring for Open-Source Liquid Handling Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15620)
*Priyanka V. Setty,Arvind Ramanathan,Ian Foster,Rick Stevens*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AEGIS是一个双层守护系统，为Opentrons OT-2开源液体处理平台提供协议验证（第一层，基于LLM+规则库，F1=0.97）和运行时物理故障监控（第二层，基于PCA+YOLO视觉，AP=0.89），统一了开源液体处理器的预飞实验验证与实时视觉监控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Opentrons OT-2等低成本液体处理器缺乏Hamilton或Tecan系统的压力式吸取监控，通常以开环方式运行，导致两类故障无法被检测：语法有效但违反实验特异性不变量的协议（如PCR模板与无模板对照间重复使用吸头），以及运行时物理执行失败（部分分配、气泡、吸头缺失）。

Method: 双层架构：第一层将机器可读的实验规则数据库与LLM推理相结合，对OT-2 Python代码进行协议验证；第二层使用PCA世界模型拟合YOLO裁剪的四帧移液器轨迹，进行运行时视觉监控，并通过VLM自投票门控级联分诊。

Result: 第一层在5个实验家族24个协议基准上调整F1达0.97，优于纯规则和纯LLM消融；第二层在留一板泄漏自由评估下AP=0.89、操作点F1=0.71（AUROC=0.80），p20微量移液器分辨极限F1=0.47。实物演示中，无吸头故障确定性捕获，部分分配召回率经VLM自投票门控提升至5/5。级联分诊将VLM成本控制在每板约1.63美元，远低于始终VLM基线的10.33美元。

Conclusion: AEGIS是首个为开源液体处理器统一预飞实验感知验证与运行时视觉监控的系统，开源且免费开源模型可与最佳商业模型媲美。

Abstract: Self-driving laboratories increasingly rely on low-cost liquid handlers such as the Opentrons OT-2, which ship without the pressure-based aspiration monitoring of Hamilton or Tecan systems and are typically run open-loop. Two failure modes go undetected: protocols that are syntactically valid but violate assay-specific invariants (e.g., tip reuse between a PCR template and a no-template control), and physical execution failures (partial dispense, air bubbles, missing tips) at runtime. We present AEGIS, a two-layer guardian for both. Layer 1 pairs a curated machine-readable assay rule database with an LLM that reasons over OT-2 Python code, reaching an adjusted F1 of 0.97 on a 24-protocol benchmark across five assay families and beating rules-only and LLM-only ablations across five backends; a free open-weight model ties the best proprietary one, so no paid API is required. Layer 2 fits a PCA world model to YOLO-cropped four-frame pipette trajectories; under a leakage-free leave-one-plate-out evaluation it reaches average precision 0.89 and operating-point F1 0.71 (AUROC 0.80), a deployment-faithful number that matches the live demonstration, and we characterize the small-pipette (p20) resolution limit (F1 0.47). A live demonstration on a physical OT-2 (five replicates per condition) catches planted no-tip failures deterministically and partial dispense on coloured dyes, with an always-VLM self-vote gate lifting partial-dispense recall to 5/5; transparent water is a principled limit of any front-view-only monitor, which AEGIS surfaces as low-confidence VLM reasoning rather than a wrong verdict. Cascade triage holds VLM cost near $1.63 per plate versus $10.33 for an always-VLM baseline. AEGIS is open source and, to our knowledge, the first system to unify pre-flight assay-aware validation with runtime visual monitoring for an open-source liquid handler.

</details>


### [44] [Think at 5 Hz, Act at 20 Hz: Asynchronous Fast-Slow Vision-Language-Action Inference for Closed-Loop Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15621)
*Yun Li,Jiachen Gong,Simon Thompson,Ehsan Javanmardi,Qunli Zhang,Zifan Zeng,Shiming Liu,Peng Wang,Zixuan Guo,Manabu Tsukada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种快慢架构（fast-slow architecture）用于端到端自动驾驶，通过冻结的7B视觉语言模型作为慢系统提供场景表征缓存，轻量级动作专家作为快系统在每个仿真时钟周期（50ms）实时生成控制指令，解决了大模型推理延迟与车辆控制频率之间的矛盾。在CARLA LangAuto-Short路线上，路线完成率从37.0%提升至94.0%，红灯违规减少三分之一，且动作专家可零样本迁移到未见过的城镇。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在端到端驾驶中具备指令跟随和场景推理能力，但其推理延迟与车辆所需的控制频率不匹配。现有闭环智能体通过在仿真交替时钟周期调用模型、中间重放上一指令来掩盖这一差距，导致一半控制输出忽视最新观测，这一折衷方案制约了驾驶性能。

Method: 提出快慢架构：1）慢系统为冻结的7B视觉语言骨干网络，以低频率处理导航指令和视觉历史，暴露每层key-value缓存作为场景的持续表征；2）快系统为轻量级动作专家，在每个仿真时钟周期（50ms）通过单次前向传播关注该缓存和当前摄像头帧以回归路径点。训练时引入随机延迟对齐，使专家适应异步执行条件下的缓存滞后。

Result: 在CARLA LangAuto-Short路线上，该架构在每个50ms仿真时钟周期生成新鲜控制，路线完成率从帧跳过基线的37.0%提升至94.0%；帧跳过消融实验显示，动作专家本身提升驾驶分数，而逐时钟周期的新鲜性将完成率从82.1%提升至94.0%，红灯违规减少三分之一。在单一城镇训练后，动作专家零样本迁移到两个未见城镇，路线完成率保持84-94%（基线仅31-41%）。开环路径点误差相比骨干网络自身动作头降低近4倍，在单张消费级GPU上逐时钟周期模型开销仅32ms且与历史长度无关。

Conclusion: 快慢架构有效解决了大模型推理延迟与车辆控制频率的冲突，逐时钟周期的新鲜控制显著提升驾驶性能和安全表现，轻量级动作专家具有良好的零样本迁移能力，验证了用缓存表征解耦慢推理与快控制的有效性。

Abstract: Large language models bring instruction following and scene reasoning to end-to-end driving, but their inference latency collides with the control rate a vehicle requires. Existing closed-loop agents hide this gap by invoking the model on alternate simulation ticks and replaying the previous command in between, so half of all control outputs ignore the newest observations. We present a fast-slow architecture that removes this compromise. A frozen 7B vision-language backbone acts as the slow system, digesting navigation instructions and visual history at low frequency while exposing its per-layer key-value cache as a standing representation of the scene. A lightweight action expert acts as the fast system, attending to this cache and to the current camera frame at every simulation tick to regress waypoints in a single forward pass. Since the cache lags behind the world at deployment, we train the expert under randomized staleness, aligning training with asynchronous execution. On LangAuto-Short routes in CARLA, our system produces fresh control at every 50 ms simulation tick and lifts route completion from 37.0 to 94.0 over the frame-skipping baseline. A frame-skip ablation with the same expert separates the two factors at work: the expert raises the driving score on its own, while per-tick freshness raises completion from 82.1 to 94.0 and cuts red-light violations by a third. Trained on a single town, the expert transfers zero-shot to two unseen towns, holding 84-94% route completion where the baseline reaches 31-41%. It reduces open-loop waypoint error by nearly a factor of four compared to the backbone's own action head, at a per-tick model cost of 32 ms that is independent of history length on a single consumer GPU.

</details>


### [45] [Scalable Open-Source Visuotactile Sensor for 6-Axis Contact Wrench Estimation in Tensegrity Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15633)
*Wenzhe Tong,Jonathan Mi,Xili Yi,Nima Fazeli,Xiaonan Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可扩展的开源视触觉传感系统，用于张拉整体机器人，实现六轴力矩估计和接触检测。该系统采用弹性外壳、3D打印TPU接口和带嵌入式摄像头及LED照明的刚性底座，通过新型Gyroid填充粘合技术形成无胶耐久界面，并利用触觉到力矩神经网络将剪切矢量场映射为六维力/力矩测量。实验显示静态验证MSE为0.1531，具备动态泛化能力，并在12kg机器人上验证了地面接触识别能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 张拉整体机器人缺乏低成本、可复制且物理可解释的触觉反馈方案，现有触觉传感系统在可扩展性、耐用性和实用性方面存在不足，难以实现接触感知的本体感知和状态估计。

Method: 设计端盖传感器（弹性外壳+3D打印TPU接口+刚性底座集成摄像头和LED）；提出Gyroid填充粘合技术实现无胶弹性体-TPU键合；构建触觉到力矩神经网络（tactile-to-wrench neural network），将剪切矢量场映射为六维力/力矩。

Result: 静态验证MSE为0.1531，具备域外动态泛化能力；在12kg张拉整体机器人上成功识别地面接触。

Conclusion: 该系统显著提升了张拉整体机器人触觉反馈的实用性，提供了一种低成本、可复制、物理可解释的实现接触感知本体感知和状态估计的途径。

Abstract: This paper presents a scalable, open-source visuotactile sensing system for tensegrity robots that enables six-axis wrench estimation and contact detection. The proposed endcap sensor integrates an elastomeric shell, a 3D-printed thermoplastic polyurethane (TPU) interface, and a rigid base housing an embedded camera and LED illumination ring. A novel gyroid-infill bonding technique is introduced to form a durable elastomer-TPU interface without adhesives, yielding a lightweight and modular design compatible with large-scale tensegrity structures. A tactile-to-wrench neural network maps shear vector fields to six-dimensional force and torque measurements. Experimental results demonstrate accurate and stable wrench estimation with a mean squared error (MSE) of 0.1531 on static validation data and out-of-domain generalization under dynamic motion. Furthermore, full-system integration on a 12 kg tensegrity robot confirms the sensor's ability to reliably identify ground contacts. The system substantially improves the practicality of tactile feedback for tensegrity robots, offering a low-cost, reproducible, and physically interpretable pathway toward contact-aware proprioception and state estimation. Open source files are available at \href{https://github.com/Jonathan-Twz/tensegrity-gelfoot}{github.com/Jonathan-Twz/tensegrity-gelfoot}

</details>


### [46] [IMBench: A Benchmark for Intuitive Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15641)
*Anurag Maurya,Sukhvansh Jain,Prajwal Avhad,Gautham Balachandran,Ziyi Zhou,Atharva Kshirsagar,Satyam Singh,Bowen Li. Rishabh Mukund,Ritul Singh,Jatin Vira,Suvonil Chatterjee,Devesh K. Jha*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: IMBENCH是一个评估直觉操纵能力的基准，涵盖感知、物理推理、动作生成和迭代执行。实验表明现有VLM和VLA模型在整合物理推理与动作执行方面存在明显不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准要么独立评估物理推理而不涉及执行，要么测量策略性能而不要求明确推理，无法捕捉人类将推理与运动控制相结合的直觉操纵能力。

Method: 构建包含35项任务、14K过滤轨迹和可扩展场景生成工具的基准IMBENCH，任务涵盖接触丰富的操纵、工具使用和多阶段依赖，要求模型推断物理结构并生成可行动作序列。

Result: 视觉语言模型表现出部分物理推理能力但无法生成可执行计划，视觉-语言-动作模型难以满足任务约束并在场景间泛化。

Conclusion: 直觉操纵是当前基础模型和通用机器人策略中缺失的关键维度，IMBENCH为评估和推动更整合的自适应物理智能提供了基准。

Abstract: Humans combine reasoning and motor control to solve complex manipulation tasks under diverse constraints. They build an understanding of the physical world that helps them convert reasoning into actions and quickly adapt to new scenes, tasks, and rules. We refer to this capability as intuitive manipulation. Existing benchmarks fail to capture this integration: they evaluate physical reasoning in isolation from execution, or measure policy performance without requiring explicit reasoning. We introduce IMBENCH, a benchmark designed to evaluate intuitive manipulation as an integrated capability spanning perception, physical reasoning, action generation, and iterative execution. Our tasks require models to infer task-relevant physical structure and generate feasible action sequences under explicit constraints, including contact-rich manipulation, tool use, and multi-stage dependencies. We introduce a benchmark of 35 tasks, 14K filtered trajectories, and scalable tools for generating diverse scenarios. Experiments reveal a consistent gap: vision language models show partial physical reasoning ability but fail to produce executable plans, while state-of-the-art vision-language-action models struggle to satisfy task constraints and generalize across scenarios. These results identify intuitive manipulation as a missing axis in current foundation models and generalist robot policies, and position IMBENCH as a step toward evaluating and enabling more integrated, adaptive physical intelligence.

</details>

*Guolei Qi,Feitian Zhang*

TL;DR: 本文提出T-DRN网络，通过Siamese差分特征提取器和双帧时间缓冲区，实现零样本sim-to-real目标导航，在AI2-THOR仿真和实体机器人上均优于基线方法。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: End-to-end deep reinforcement learning (DRL) for zero-shot object-goal visual navigation remains challenged by the sim-to-real gap, particularly variations in object appearance and restricted camera field-of-view (FoV). This letter proposes a Temporal Difference-Relational Network (T-DRN) for robust zero-shot sim-to-real transfer. T-DRN combines a Siamese difference-based feature extractor, which computes relational difference between the target and observed objects to produce domain-independent representations, with a dual-frame temporal buffer that preserves short-term object continuity under narrow FoV. Extensive experiments in AI2-THOR demonstrate that T-DRN improves zero-shot generalization in terms of success rates over strong baselines. Furthermore, T-DRN is systematically validated on a physical wheeled robot, demonstrating robust performance under real sensing and actuation constraints and supporting the feasibility of direct sim-to-real transfer.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主液压挖掘机的开发受限于物理机器访问受限以及真实实验成本高昂。

Method: 利用LSTM网络将挖掘机视为输入-输出算子进行系统级数字替代学习；引入基于自适应卡尔曼滤波的一致性感知状态估计方法处理真实数据测量不一致问题；采用封闭循环自回归评估进行验证。

Result: 该替代模型在角速度和长时程轨迹复现方面均达到高保真度。

Conclusion: 所提模型可作为仿真和物理系统的即插即用替代，支持挖掘自动化算法的可扩展高效开发。

Abstract: Developing autonomous hydraulic excavators is constrained by limited access to physical machines and the high cost of real-world experimentation. This paper proposes a simulation-to-real framework for learning a system-level digital surrogate using Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) networks. Instead of modeling internal dynamics, the excavator is treated as an input-output operator, and the surrogate is trained to reproduce its closed-loop behavior under identical control inputs. The approach is first validated in a MuJoCo simulation environment and then transferred to a real excavator. To address measurement inconsistencies in real-world data, a consistency-aware state estimation method based on adaptive Kalman filtering is introduced. Experimental results demonstrate that the learned surrogate achieves high fidelity in both angular velocity and long-horizon trajectory reproduction under closed-loop autoregressive evaluation. These results confirm that the proposed model can serve as a drop-in surrogate for both simulation and physical systems, enabling scalable and efficient development of excavation automation algorithms.

</details>
