TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过塑性变形实现连续稳定结构（CSSPD）的软体夹爪，无需能量输入即可保持稳定构型，结合仿生爪垫和kirigami金属层优化，实现了高达16N的被动保持力，在400 m/s^2脉冲加速度下仍保持稳定，并可长时间无动力栖息于树枝上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体机器人虽具有柔顺性和适应性，但在动态环境中稳定性不足，尤其是在加速或外部扰动下软体夹爪易发生抓取失败，亟需一种无需持续能量输入即可保持稳定的解决方案。

Method: 提出CSSPD结构，利用塑性变形机制使夹爪在无能量输入下维持连续构型；集成仿生爪垫增强稳定性并实现基于传感的快速抓取；建立数学模型并对金属层的kirigami结构进行优化。

Result: 夹爪无需能量输入即可提供高达16N的被动保持力，性能可比0.3 MPa气动驱动；结合气动驱动时可在400 m/s^2脉冲加速度下保持稳定；可无动力长时间栖息于树枝上。

Conclusion: 所提出的CSSPD结构有效解决了软体夹爪在动态环境中的稳定性问题，无需持续能量输入即可实现高性能抓取与被动保持，在移动机器人应用中具有潜力。

Abstract: Soft robots have seen widespread adoption in interactive tasks due to their inherent compliance and adaptability. However, these advantages often come at the cost of stability, posing challenges in a dynamic environment. This limitation is especially critical in soft grippers, where instability under acceleration or external disturbances can result in grasp failure. In this study, we present a continuously stable structure through plastic deformation (CSSPD), integrated into a soft gripper. By leveraging the mechanism of plastic deformation, the gripper maintains continuous configurations without energy input, while the added stiffness ensures both static and dynamic stability. We introduce a bioinspired paw pad that significantly enhances stability and enables sensing-based rapid object grasping. Then we develop the mathematical model and optimize the kirigami structure of the metal layer. Experimental results show that the gripper can sustain a passive holding force of up to 16 N without energy input, achieving performance comparable to pneumatic actuation at 0.3 MPa. When combined with pneumatic actuation, it remains stable under pulsed accelerations of up to 400 m/s^2. It can also passively perch on tree branches for extended periods without power, demonstrating promise for mobile robotic applications.

</details>


### [50] [A Generative Partially Specified Finite State Machine Approach to Complex Behaviour Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15674)
*Kalana Ratnayake,Michael Pritchard,David Hinwood,Maleen Jayasuriya,Damith Herath*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种生成式部分指定有限状态机（GPSFSM）神经符号架构，将FSM与LLM结合用于机器人行为规划，在导航任务中实现了比BTGenBot更高的规划成功率和更低的延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然LLM已成功与行为树（BT）结合用于动态重规划，但有限状态机（FSM）尽管被广泛采用且计算高效，其在生成式方法中仍未被探索。

Method: 提出GPSFSM神经符号架构，利用FSM的状态和事件触发转换的符号与语义结构实现行为规划；开发Fabric引擎用于解析、验证和执行包含多种控制结构的行为计划；扩展ROS2 Capabilities2包增加异步事件系统和运行时参数注入；设计PromptTools提供统一的ROS2接口连接本地和云端LLM。

Result: 在导航任务中，GPSFSM方法在规划生成成功率上始终优于BTGenBot，尤其在零样本场景下表现突出，同时保持与前沿LLM相当或更低的规划延迟。

Conclusion: 本文首次将生成式FSM框架引入机器人领域，证明了FSM可与LLM有效结合用于动态行为规划，并开源了完整的ROS2实现。

Abstract: Autonomous robots operating in dynamic environments require behaviour planning systems that combine reactivity, interpretability, and adaptability. While Large Language Models have been successfully integrated with Behaviour Trees for dynamic replanning, Finite State Machines, despite their widespread adoption and computational efficiency, remain unexplored for generative approaches. We propose a Generative Partially Specified Finite State Machine (GPSFSM) neurosymbolic architecture that utilises the symbolic and semantic structure of FSMs, including states and event-triggered transitions, to implement Behaviour Planning. This paper introduces the first GPSFSM framework for robotics, featuring Fabric, an FSM engine that parses, validates, and executes behaviour plans that contain Sequential, Recovery, Parallel-Any, and Parallel-All control structures. We extend the Capabilities2 package in ROS2 with an asynchronous event system for behaviour chaining and runtime parameter injection for configurable execution, addressing the ad-hoc function representations that limit current generative systems. PromptTools provides a unified ROS 2 interface to local and cloud LLMs, with prompt buffering, enabling dynamic asynchronous composition of task and context information. Together, these components enable standardised semantic capability descriptions for robot-agnostic development. Experimental evaluation on navigation tasks demonstrates that our GPSFSM approach achieves consistently higher plan-generation success rates than the state-of-the-art BTGenBot system, particularly excelling in zero-shot scenarios where BTs typically struggle, while maintaining comparable or lower planning latency to frontier LLMs. We also demonstrate that our system can generate complex behaviours. We release an open-source ROS2 stack that makes generative FSM planning practical and reproducible for robotic systems.

</details>


### [51] [RAVEN: Reinforcement-Adaptive Visibility-Graph Planning for Robust Humanoid Navigation with Collision-Free MPC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15701)
*Ruochen Hou,Shiqi Wang,Beom Jun Kim,Hanzhang Fang,Mehak Singal,Dennis W. Hong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RAVEN是一种层次化强化学习-模型预测控制（RL-MPC）框架，通过RL自适应调整可见性图规划器的障碍物膨胀等几何参数，结合MPC跟踪实现鲁棒的人形导航，在延迟和噪声条件下优于手动调参基线和纯RL方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典可见性图规划器结合MPC能生成无碰撞轨迹，但依赖手动调参和精确系统建模；实际系统中控制延迟、状态估计噪声和 locomotion 不确定性会导致过冲和约束违反，即使名义路径几何最优。

Method: 采用层次化RL-MPC框架：上层RL通过修改障碍物膨胀和图形参数自适应调整可见性图规划器的几何构建，重塑自由空间几何以改变全局路径拓扑，补偿延迟和跟踪不完美；下层MPC跟踪规划轨迹并显式强制执行速度边界和避障约束。

Result: 相比手动调参的可见性图-MPC基线和纯RL导航策略，RAVEN在障碍物附近过冲减少、狭窄通道鲁棒性提升、在延迟和噪声下导航更可靠。

Conclusion: 强化学习自适应图构建结合约束MPC为人形机器人鲁棒导航提供了有效且可解释的替代方案，优于端到端学习方法。

Abstract: Humanoid navigation in dynamic environments requires long-horizon planning while respecting short-horizon dynamic and safety constraints. Classical visibility-graph planners combined with model predictive control (MPC) can efficiently generate collision-free trajectories, but their performance depends on manually tuned parameters and accurate system modeling. In real robotic systems, control delays, state-estimation noise, and locomotion uncertainties can cause overshoot and constraint violations even when the nominal path is geometrically optimal. We propose RAVEN, a hierarchical reinforcement learning (RL)-MPC framework for robust humanoid navigation. Unlike prior approaches that use learning to tune cost weights or replace planning entirely, RAVEN employs RL to adapt the geometric construction of a visibility-graph planner by modifying obstacle inflation and related graph parameters. By directly reshaping the free-space geometry, the learned planner alters the topology of the global path to compensate for delay and tracking imperfections. A collision-free MPC layer then tracks the planned trajectory while explicitly enforcing velocity bounds and obstacle-avoidance constraints. By training under realistic delays and observation noise, RAVEN learns planning adaptations that improve robustness while retaining explicit long-horizon geometric planning and constrained optimization, in contrast to end-to-end learning approaches. We evaluate RAVEN against a manually tuned visibility-graph MPC baseline and a pure RL navigation policy. Results demonstrate reduced overshoot near obstacles, improved robustness in narrow passages, and more reliable navigation under delay and noise. These findings indicate that reinforcement-adaptive graph construction combined with constrained MPC provides an effective and interpretable alternative to end-to-end learning for robust humanoid navigation.

</details>


### [52] [Implicit Virtual Leader: Decentralized Vision-Only Relative Pose Estimation for Multi-Robot Formations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15708)
*Shiyuan Yang,Zelin Wang,Zhijia Tao,Yilin Wang,Zhengyu Hou,Xiaosong Kong,Borong Zhang,Yip Fun Yeung,Yuankai Luo,Sharon Lee,Qingbiao Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于图神经网络（GNN）的完全去中心化视觉相对位姿估计框架，通过隐式虚拟领导者（IVL）概念，仅利用单目图像和机器人间通信实现无GPS环境下的编队控制，并集成了异方差高斯负对数似然（GNLL）头用于偶然不确定性、MC Dropout用于认知不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决经典领航者-跟随者编队控制中单点故障、误差传播问题，以及对绝对定位传感器（如GPS）的依赖，这些在GPS拒绝环境中不可行。

Method: 提出隐式虚拟领导者（IVL）概念，作为非物理编队参考系，通过GNN从单目图像和机器人间通信隐式学习；集成异方差GNLL头估计偶然不确定性，MC Dropout估计认知不确定性。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界测试集上实现了有竞争力的位姿估计精度，并自然地泛化到异构机器人平台和不同编队规模。

Conclusion: 该去中心化视觉相对位姿估计框架有效克服了传统方法的局限性，具有良好的泛化性和不确定性量化能力。

Abstract: Classical leader-follower formation control suffers from single points of failure and error propagation, and relies on absolute localization sensors that are ill-suited for GPS-denied environments. We address these limitations by introducing a fully decentralized, vision-only relative pose estimation framework based on Graph Neural Networks (GNNs). The key idea is the implicit virtual leader (IVL): a non-physical formation reference frame that is not tied to any individual robot but is implicitly learned within the GNN using only monocular images and inter-robot communication. We attach a heteroscedastic GNLL head for aleatoric uncertainty and MC~Dropout for epistemic uncertainty, and conduct a systematic comparison across simulation and real-world test sets. Our framework achieves competitive pose estimation accuracy and generalizes naturally to heterogeneous robot platforms and varying formation sizes.

</details>


### [53] [AC-VLA: Robust Out-of-Distribution Action Execution via Compositional Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15714)
*Xiaojiang Peng,Kai Peng,Jie Lu,Zheng Lian,Zitong YU,Xiaobo Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AC-VLA通过动作组合学习框架和状态条件非对称掩码策略，解决了VLA模型在重组子任务场景中的分布外泛化问题，在LIBERO-OOD基准上取得约28%的绝对提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在端到端机器人操作中表现出色，但当熟悉的子任务以未见过的配置重新组合时，OOD泛化能力严重不足。现有模型存在两个相互强化的失败模式：轨迹过拟合（模型过度拟合整体轨迹模式而非组合性子技能语义）和感知捷径（动作标记过度依赖腕部视角纹理而忽视全局空间定位）。

Method: 提出AC-VLA框架，包含两个架构无关的组件：(1) 组合学习模块——LLM驱动的指令分解器+本体感受轨迹对齐器生成密集子任务监督，结合完整演示与分解数据的混合训练；(2) 状态条件非对称掩码策略——在夹爪闭合阶段抑制腕部视角输入，强制模型进行全局语义定位。无需修改架构，可直接集成到任何VLA骨干中。

Result: 在π0.5上实例化，在LIBERO和LIBERO-OOD基准上评估，AC-VLA在组合性OOD任务上实现约28%的绝对性能提升，同时保持近乎完美的分布内性能。

Conclusion: AC-VLA通过引入子任务级别的组合学习监督和针对性的视觉输入掩码策略，有效解耦了VLA模型中的轨迹过拟合和感知捷径问题，是一种即插即用、无需架构修改的OOD泛化增强方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models excel at end-to-end robotic manipulation but struggle with out-of-distribution (OOD) generalization when familiar sub-tasks are recombined in unseen configurations. We identify two mutually reinforcing failure modes: \emph{trajectory overfitting}, where models overfit to holistic trajectory patterns rather than compositional sub-skill semantics; and \emph{perceptual shortcut}, where action tokens over-rely on wrist-view textures at the expense of global spatial grounding. To address both, we introduce \textbf{AC-VLA}, a plug-and-play Action Compositional learning framework comprising two architecture-agnostic components: \textbf{(i)} a compositional learning module that uses an LLM-driven instruction decomposer and a proprioceptive trajectory aligner to generate dense sub-task supervision, followed by mixed training on complete demonstrations and decomposed data to endow the model with compositional generalization; and \textbf{(ii)} a state-conditioned asymmetric masking strategy that suppresses wrist-view inputs during closed-gripper phases, enforcing global semantic grounding. All components are architectural modification-free and directly integrable into any VLA backbone. Instantiated on $π_{0.5}$ and evaluated on LIBERO and LIBERO-OOD benchmarks, AC-VLA achieves a ~28% absolute improvement on compositional OOD tasks while maintaining near-perfect in-distribution performance.

</details>


### [54] [A Task-Space Receding Horizon Controller for Fast Collision Avoidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15733)
*Mattia Penzotti,Marco Controzzi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种任务空间滚动时域控制器，通过短时接触一致滚动生成终端运动学参考，结合平滑最小加速度过渡，实现机械臂在动态杂乱环境中的实时避障，兼顾前瞻性与计算效率，在40自由度系统和6自由度平台上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全模型预测控制（MPC）能提供前瞻能力，但随预测时域、模型精度和几何约束数量增加，在线计算代价迅速增长；而无时域的反应式方法计算高效，但在动态杂乱环境中缺乏远见，容易陷入局部困境。因此需要一种兼顾前瞻性与实时性的方法。

Method: 提出任务空间滚动时域控制器：1) 从闭环逆运动学调节律出发，利用迭代动力学求解器对膨胀的凸几何体进行短时接触一致滚动，生成满足内部非穿透约束的终端运动学参考；2) 仅计算朝向该参考的平滑最小加速度过渡的第一个输入。该方法无需完整的约束轨迹优化，通过滚动过程融合机器人与障碍物接触、动态障碍物运动及自碰撞信息。

Result: 理论分析了接触非活跃闭环下的局部指数任务空间调节性；刻画了滚动中接触激活时的离散更新特性，并界定了运动障碍物对正则工作集的影响。40自由度多链系统仿真表明中等预测时域能平衡预见性、响应性和计算开销。6自由度平台硬件实验验证了无需精确惯性参数即可实现一致的仿真到实物迁移，在动态杂乱环境中成功率优于动态优化织物和MPC基线，且求解时间满足实时运行要求。

Conclusion: 该控制器通过短时滚动生成终端参考，弥合了反应式方法与MPC之间的差距，在动态杂乱环境中实现了实时性与前瞻性的有效平衡，且对惯性参数不敏感，具有很强的实用价值。

Abstract: Real-time collision avoidance for robotic manipulators requires fast reactions to unexpected obstacle motion and lookahead to avoid becoming trapped by near-future constraints. Full model predictive control can provide this foresight, but its online cost may grow quickly with horizon length, model fidelity, and the number of active geometric constraints. Conversely, horizon-free reactive methods are computationally efficient but can be short-sighted in dynamic clutter. We present a task-space receding-horizon controller that uses a short contact-consistent rollout to generate a terminal kinematic reference satisfying internal non-penetration constraints, then computes only the first input of a smooth minimum-acceleration transition toward that reference. Starting from a closed-loop inverse-kinematics regulation law, the rollout is performed with an iterative dynamics solver operating on inflated convex robot and obstacle geometries, so that robot-obstacle contacts, dynamic obstacle motion, and self-collisions can shape the terminal reference without requiring full constrained trajectory optimization. We analyze the contact-inactive closed loop and show local exponential task-space regulation under standard regularity assumptions. For contacts activated inside the rollout, we characterize the corresponding discrete updates and bound the effect of moving obstacles on regular operating sets. Simulations on a 40-DOF multi-chain system show that intermediate horizons balance anticipation, responsiveness, and computational cost. Hardware experiments on a 6-DOF platform demonstrate consistent sim-to-real behavior without accurate inertial parameter estimation, and comparisons against dynamic optimization fabrics and model predictive control (MPC) baselines show improved success rates in dynamic clutter while preserving solve times compatible with real-time execution in the tested regimes.

</details>


### [55] [Towards Artificial Nerves: Biomimetic Optical-Fiber Tactile Sensing for Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15746)
*Laura E. Butcher,Chris J. Ford,Nathan F. Lepora,Efi Psomopoulou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OptiTac是一种仿生触觉传感器，通过将机械引脚与光纤人工神经配对，将触觉信息从感知表面转移至远端处理，实现高分辨率、可解释的分布式触觉感知，为机器人提供类人触觉能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人系统需要接近人类皮肤适应性和分辨率的触觉感知能力，以实现灵巧操作和安全交互，但现有触觉传感器在空间分辨率与信号传输方面存在局限。

Method: 采用仿生设计，将每个机械引脚（模拟机械感受器）与一根光纤（模拟人工神经）配对，构成触觉信息的传输通道；将触觉信号视为图像，使用简单解析方法（而非深度学习）推断接触位置、大小和形状。

Result: 实现了将触觉信息从感知表面路由至远端处理的同时保持高空间分辨率；利用可解释的解析方法而非黑箱模型完成触觉感知任务。

Conclusion: OptiTac建立了人工神经启发的触觉感知框架，为未来机器人分布式触觉系统提供了可扩展、可解释的实用路径，验证了生物学进化原理可指导人工神经系统的开发。

Abstract: Robotic systems increasingly demand tactile sensing that approaches the adaptability and resolution of human skin to enable dexterous manipulation and safe interaction. OptiTac is a biomimetic tactile sensor that emulates the mechanoreceptor-to-nerve architecture of human touch by pairing each mechanical pin on a soft skin with an optical fiber acting as an artificial nerve. This design demonstrates an architectural principle for routing tactile information away from the sensing surface while preserving high spatial resolution, establishing a practical route toward distributed tactile sensing in future robotic systems. By treating tactile signals as images, simple analytical methods, rather than opaque deep-learning models, are used to infer contact location, size, and shape, providing interpretable and scalable tactile intelligence. This work demonstrates how evolutionary principles from biology can guide the development of artificial nerve systems for robots, offering a pathway toward human-like tactile perception in next-generation robotic platforms. More broadly, OptiTac establishes an artificial nerve-inspired sensing framework for interpretable robotic touch and a scalable route toward future distributed tactile systems.

</details>


### [56] [SkillNav: Score-Level Skill Intervention for Zero-Shot Object Goal Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15758)
*Ruijie Sang,Yiqun Duan,Pinhan Fu,Ruilin Wang,Wei Sui,Xianda Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SkillNav提出了一种基于行为技能框架的VLM导航方法，通过在已有的好奇心价值地图上以零token成本写入行为记忆，实现三层级技能协作（软缩放、下界提升、硬覆盖），无需训练即可在MP3D、HM3D等数据集上达到最新的SPL和成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM智能体在零样本目标导航中存在单帧推理缺乏跨步行为感知的问题，导致死胡同停滞、室内循环、检测到目标后迂回接近等重复性失败。基于提示的修复方法会膨胀token预算且难以编码空间信号（如角度、地图单元格、视角坐标）。

Method: SkillNav将VLM导航器已有的好奇心价值地图作为可写基板，由可组合技能以零token成本写入行为记忆。技能按行为权限分为三层：软缩放（比例重加权）、下界提升（区域级保证）、硬覆盖（阈值触发的强制动作），按固定组合顺序跨层协作。此外，最小提示通道补充类别级语义提示，形成空间记忆在地图上、语义记忆在短提示中的双表示设计。

Result: 无需训练，在MP3D上SPL达25.5，HM3D v0.1上SPL达39.3、SR达69.7，HM3D v0.2上SPL达43.2、SR达75.9，SPL比最强先前方法提升最多6.0个绝对点。

Conclusion: 能力提升转化为技能注册过程：新行为可即插即用，无需重新训练VLM或干扰现有技能，为持续改进开辟了路径。

Abstract: Vision-Language Model (VLM) agents have advanced zero-shot object-goal navigation, yet single-frame reasoning leaves them without the cross-step behavioral awareness an embodied navigator requires, producing recurring failures such as dead-end stalls, in-room loops, and circuitous approaches to detected targets. Prompt-based remedies inflate token budgets across multi-submodule episodes and still struggle to encode inherently spatial signals such as angles, map cells, and viewpoint coordinates. In this paper, we propose SkillNav, an extensible behavioral skill framework for VLM-based navigation that treats the curiosity value map already maintained by modern VLM navigators as a writable substrate on which composable skills inscribe behavioral memory at zero token cost. Skills are stratified into three tiers by their level of behavioral authority, namely soft scaling for proportional reweighting, lower-bound boost for region-level guarantees, and hard override for threshold-triggered forced actions, and cooperate across tiers under a fixed composition order that establishes a predictable, declared priority among skills. This design turns capability improvement into skill registration: new behaviors plug in without retraining the VLM or disturbing existing skills, opening a path for continual refinement. A minimal prompt channel complements the score-level skills with category-level semantic hints, yielding a dual-representation design in which spatial memory lives on the map and semantic memory in short prompts. Training-free, SkillNav establishes new state-of-the-art SPL across MP3D (25.5), HM3D v0.1 (39.3), and HM3D v0.2 (43.2), improving SPL by up to 6.0 absolute over the strongest prior method, and achieves the highest Success Rate on HM3D v0.1 (69.7) and v0.2 (75.9).

</details>


### [57] [Deployment-Ready UWB Localization for Industrial Ground Robots with Automatic Anchor Calibration and Terrain-Aware Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15807)
*Alexander Raab,Giulio Delama,Roland Jung,Stephan Weiss*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向工业自主移动机器人（AMR）的端到端UWB定位方案，集成了自动锚点标定与多传感器融合估计器，在仓库环境中实现了高精度且一致性更好的定位，并显著减少了人工标定工作量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管UWB测距技术具有高精度和低成本的优势，但在实际部署中面临两个关键挑战：静态锚点标定耗时且易出错，以及UWB与现有车载传感器的融合需要精心设计以保证鲁棒且一致的位姿估计。因此，亟需一种减少人工干预、易于集成且精度满足工业需求的UWB定位方案。

Method: 提出端到端流水线：1) 自动锚点标定阶段，估计锚点位置和距离偏差；2) 多传感器融合定位阶段，将UWB与本体感知（proprioceptive sensing）通过考虑偏差的扩展卡尔曼滤波（EKF）进行融合，面向平面约束的车体运动设计，无需大量参数调优。

Result: 在商用物流AMR的仓库实验中，实现了室内及室内-室外过渡场景的准确定位，相较于先前估计器公式一致性有所提升。在独立叉车数据集上的评估表明该方法具有良好的跨平台可迁移性。在有限视距和稀疏锚点覆盖的测试场景中仍保持有效性。

Conclusion: 该UWB定位方案能够以大幅减少的人工工作量实现工业AMR所需的定位精度，有效推动了UWB在实际工业部署中的应用。仓库数据集已公开。

Abstract: Ultra-Wideband (UWB) ranging has become a viable option for industrial Autonomous Mobile Robot (AMR) localization due to improved accuracy and low cost. However, real-world deployments remain limited by two recurring challenges: calibrating static anchors can be time-consuming and error-prone, and integrating UWB with existing onboard sensors requires careful design to ensure robust and consistent pose estimation. Addressing these challenges, this paper presents an end-to-end pipeline that combines automatic anchor calibration with a generic multi-sensor estimator tailored to surface-bound vehicle motion. It targets existing AMR stacks in scenarios where robot pose priors are available for initialization. The calibration stage estimates anchor positions and range biases, while the localization stage fuses UWB with proprioceptive sensing in a bias-aware Extended Kalman Filter to improve consistency without extensive parameter tuning. Experiments on a commercial logistics AMR in a warehouse setting demonstrate accurate positioning indoors and across outdoor transitions, with improved consistency compared to an earlier estimator formulation. Evaluation on an independent forklift dataset further indicates transferability to other platforms. The method remains effective in test cases with limited line-of-sight and sparse anchor coverage. These results show that UWB localization can be deployed with substantially reduced manual effort while preserving the accuracy required for industrial AMRs. The collected warehouse dataset is made publicly available.

</details>


### [58] [Beyond Frontiers: Scene-Anomaly Guided Autonomous Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15828)
*Akash Kumbar,Abhinav Raundhal,Madhava Krishna*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SCAGE将自主3D探索重新定义为几何异常最小化问题，通过检测实时观测与先验室内建筑知识之间的不一致（场景异常）来引导机器人探索，从而在实现高覆盖率（~90%）的同时提升3D重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于覆盖最大化的几何启发式探索方法忽略底层结构上下文，导致轨迹低效且最终3D重建保真度受限，需弥合空间覆盖与重建质量之间的差距。

Method: 提出SCAGE框架，将探索重新定义为几何异常最小化问题。机器人携带室内标准建筑先验知识，在导航过程中持续评估实时3D点云观测与学习先验的差异，检测结构异常（如断裂的墙壁、不完整的桌子），并将这些异常作为引导信号，驱动机器人前往最优视角进行解析。

Result: 在多种场景下实现约90%的体积覆盖率，并在3D重建质量上显著超越现有最先进基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过主动针对重建不良区域而非空白空间进行探索，SCAGE成功将空间发现与高保真建图无缝耦合，为自主探索与重建质量联合优化提供了新范式。

Abstract: Autonomous exploration of unknown 3D environments is traditionally driven by coverage-maximizing geometric heuristics. However, these methods typically determine exploration targets without considering the underlying structural context. This leads to inefficient trajectories often limiting the fidelity of the final 3D reconstruction. To bridge the gap between spatial coverage and reconstruction quality, we introduce a novel paradigm: reframing exploration as a geometric anomaly minimization problem. We present SCAGE: SCene Anomaly Guided Exploration, a novel autonomous exploration framework that operates directly on unstructured 3D point clouds. Instead of blindly chasing volumetric boundaries, we equip the robot with a foundational understanding of standard indoor architecture. As the robot navigates, it continuously evaluates its live 3D observations against these learned expectations. When the incoming geometry contradicts the learned priors of a typical indoor environment, such as a fragmented wall or a partial table, the system flags these regions as scene anomalies. These geometric inconsistencies act as a guiding signal, naturally drawing the robot to investigate and resolve these structural anomalies from optimal vantage points. By actively targeting poorly reconstructed regions rather than just empty space, our approach seamlessly couples spatial discovery with high-fidelity mapping. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that SCAGE achieves superior volumetric coverage (~90% in all scenes) and higher 3D reconstruction quality compared to state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [59] [Minimum Time Dubins Airplane Paths with Asymmetric Climb Rates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15863)
*Jaeyoung Lim,Giuseppe Loianno*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出非对称Dubins飞机路径，通过考虑爬升与下降时不同的爬升率约束，相比传统对称约束方法可减少71%的最小飞行时间，并使中位求解速度提升2.8倍，在真实飞行中验证了其实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Dubins飞机路径采用对称爬升率约束，导致路径次优且保守，未能充分利用飞行器性能。

Method: 提出非对称Dubins飞机路径模型，放宽爬升与下降的爬升率对称约束；重新推导时间最优性条件，证明非对称飞行路径角约束仍保持最优性。

Result: 在随机生成的状态连接中，最小时间减少71%；在崎岖地形采样规划中，中位求解时间提升2.8倍；真实飞行实验验证了可行性。

Conclusion: 非对称爬升率约束能更充分利用飞行器性能，显著提升路径规划效率与可行性，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Dubins airplane paths approximate the limited maneuverability of fixed-wing vehicles with minimum curvature and climb rate constraints. However, the symmetric climb rate constraints result in sub-optimal paths and conservative vehicle performance. In this work, we propose asymmetric Dubins airplane paths, which consider asymmetric climb rates for climbing and descending. We revisit the time optimality conditions and show that the asymmetric flight path angle constraints preserve optimality. We show that by considering asymmetric climb rates, we can take advantage of full performance of the vehicle, reducing the minimum time by 71% for connecting randomly generated states. We also demonstrate that the added climb rate results in 2.8 times faster to find the median solution time when integrated into a sampling-based planning task on rugged terrain, due to the added feasibility. We further demonstrate the practicality of the approach with a real-world flight.

</details>


### [60] [Dynamics-Aware Meta-Imitation for Generalization to Unseen Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15880)
*Zhenduo Shang,Xiyao Liu,Bohan Li,Xudong Wang,Teng Ren,Lianqing Liu,Zhi Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Dynamics-Aware Meta-Imitation (DAMI) 框架，通过元学习构建共享技能空间，结合 VMT 模块捕捉时空动态、U2T 块融合多模态观测、TCFM 机制调制3D特征，实现从演示中学习任务逻辑而非静态记忆，在仿真和真实环境中均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模仿学习方法主要聚焦于领域内任务，缺乏对未见任务的泛化能力，且受限于数据稀缺和环境泛化问题。

Method: 提出 DAMI 框架：1) 元学习构建共享技能空间实现快速适应；2) VMT 模块捕捉任务潜在空间中的复杂时空动态；3) U2T 块融合非结构化多模态观测；4) TCFM 机制调制低层3D特征；5) 从随机完整参考演示中学习内在动态和任务逻辑。

Result: 在仿真和真实环境实验中，DAMI 在已见任务的直接推理和未见任务的少样本微调适应上均超越现有最先进基线。

Conclusion: 通过捕捉任务内在动态而非记忆静态线索，DAMI 实现了有效的泛化能力，弥合了模仿学习中的泛化差距。

Abstract: Imitation Learning aims to learn skills from extensive observations and demonstrations for robots, so it suffers from data scarcity and environment generalization. The existing methods predominantly focus on imitation from in-domain tasks and consequently struggle with generalization to unseen tasks. To bridge this generalization gap, we propose the \textbf{D}ynamics-\textbf{A}ware \textbf{M}eta-\textbf{I}mitation (DAMI) framework. By integrating meta-learning to construct a shared skill space, DAMI equips agents for rapid adaptation to novel tasks. We introduce the Visual-Motor Trajectory (VMT) module to capture complex spatio-temporal dynamics within the task latent space. Furthermore, we propose the Unpaired Unified Task (U2T) block to fuse unstructured multimodal observations. To coordinate these representations, we integrate a Task-Conditioned Feature Modulation (TCFM) mechanism customized for modulating low-level 3D features. By capturing intrinsic dynamics from a random complete reference demonstration, our framework learns the underlying task logic rather than memorizing static cues, ensuring effective generalization. Extensive experiments in both simulation and real-world settings demonstrate that our approach outperforms state-of-the-art baselines regarding direct inference on seen tasks and adaptation to unseen tasks via few-shot fine-tuning.

</details>


### [61] [Learning Reach-Avoid Task with Reinforcement Learning: Vectorized Simulation and Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15935)
*Jonas Weihing,Shahram Eivazi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文首次提出了一个针对机器人手臂触及-避障任务的综合基准测试，使用MuJoCo MJX和Brax库实现并行化模拟，在真实复杂场景下评估DRL算法，结果显示虽然DRL在简化设置中表现完美，但在现实场景中性能显著下降（触及任务成功率96.1%-98.8%，静态触及-避障任务86.8%-95.2%），表明仍需进一步研究才能宣称DRL成功解决了该任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DRL在触及-避障任务上的研究多局限于简化的桌面场景，其在复杂真实环境下的表现尚不明确，缺乏一个能够准确反映现实复杂性的综合基准测试。

Method: 利用MuJoCo MJX物理引擎和Brax库并行化模拟环境与DRL算法，构建了涵盖多种设置的机器人手臂触及-避障任务基准，在UR5e和Franka Emika Robot两种机器人平台上进行实验。

Result: 触及任务：UR5e成功率96.1%，Franka成功率98.8%；静态触及-避障任务：UR5e成功率86.8%，Franka成功率95.2%。研究表明在简化设置中表现完美的DRL智能体在现实场景中性能显著下降。

Conclusion: 尽管取得了当前最佳结果，但DRL在真实复杂场景下解决机器人手臂触及-避障任务仍存在挑战，需进一步研究才能声称该问题已被成功解决。

Abstract: Deep reinforcement learning (DRL) has a longstanding tradition in addressing the reach-avoid task problem, especially for controlling robotic arms. While this task serves as a baseline environment within the research community, the ability of DRL to effectively learn the each-avoid task in complex and realistic scenarios beyond simplified and restricted tabletop settings remains uncertain. In this paper, we present, for the first time, a comprehensive benchmark for the reachavoid task that accurately captures real-world complexities without simplifications. We demonstrate a diverse range of settings for robotic arm reach-avoid task, which can be used for evaluating DRL research. We achieved this by utilizing the MuJoCo MJX physics engine and parallelizing both the simulation environment and DRL algorithms using the Brax library. We achieved state-of-the-art results with success rates of 96.1% (UR5e) and 98.8% (Franka Emika Robot) for the reach task and 86.8% (UR5e) and 95.2% (Franka) for the static reachavoid task. Our results indicate that while in previous works DRL agents could solve, for example, a reach task in a simplified setting perfectly, their agents performance collapses when evaluated in realistic scenarios. Overall, this work identifies that additional research is still required to claim the successful resolution of the robotic arm reach-avoid task using DRL. The environment and benchmarking code is available as open source at the following link

</details>


### [62] [Embodied Active Learning under Limited Annotation and Navigation Budget for Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15974)
*Hadrien Crassous,Mohamed Yassine Kabouri,Minahil Raza,Joni Pajarinen,Riad Akrour*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种在机器人导航时间和标注预算双重约束下，通过空间不一致性引导的体感式批量主动学习框架，自适应未知环境中的目标检测器，实验证明该方法在相同预算下实现了最高的检测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器人导航时间和人工标注预算有限的实际部署场景中，计算机视觉目标检测器需要快速适应未知环境，但现有方法未能有效结合导航采样与主动学习来针对性解决检测器的失败案例。

Method: 提出体感式批量主动学习框架，每个轮次中机器人在有限的导航预算下采集候选样本，并在有限的标注预算下选择最相关的图像；利用空间一致性识别标签不一致的图像，从而选择最能提升视觉模型的样本。

Result: 在AI2-THOR模拟器大规模场景和波士顿动力Spot机器人+YOLOv5真实场景上的实验表明，相比多种基线方法，空间不一致性引导的方法在相同预算下实现了最高的检测精度。

Conclusion: 空间不一致性作为一种无需外部监督的信号，能有效指导机器人的导航与样本选择，在有限预算下高效完成目标检测器的环境自适应。

Abstract: This paper studies how to adapt a computer vision object detector to an unknown environment under both a robot navigation time and annotation budget constraint. Our approach selects informative robot trajectories and image samples to retrain the detector, explicitly targeting its failure cases. Formally, the approach is an embodied variant of batch active learning, where at each round an agent has a limited navigation budget to collect candidate samples and a limited annotation budget for the most relevant images. We leverage spatial consistency to identify images with inconsistent labels, which are likely to provide the greatest improvement to the vision model. We evaluate the approach using different active learning objectives on large scenes from the AI2-THOR simulator and on a real-world setup using a Boston Dynamics Spot robot with the real-time object detector YOLOv5. Through comparison against several baselines, our experimental results show that spatial inconsistency helps guide the agent and select relevant images without external supervision, achieving the highest detection accuracy at the end of the adaptation process under the same budget. The open-source project can be found at https://mkabouri.github.io/embodied-active-learning-od

</details>


### [63] [Data and Learning Where it Matters for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15982)
*Oliver Hausdörfer,Linus Schwarz,Gabor Marko,Christian Dietz,Timo Class,Luka Hofer,Jim Yun-Jin Li,Johannes Hechtl,Ralf Römer,Angela P. Schoellig*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自动化数据收集方案，结合离线深度强化学习，仅对接触密集任务的关键段进行密集数据收集，在自由空间运动中依赖传统规划。在四个真实世界任务中，仅用2-2.5小时自主数据收集即达到96%平均成功率（最强基线为55%），并在分布外场景中保持高性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端学习的策略在高精度任务中仍然脆弱且泛化能力差，这些限制主要源于数据收集缺乏结构和重点。

Method: 提出自动化数据收集方案，结合离线深度强化学习，仅对接触密集任务的关键段进行密集数据收集，自由空间运动使用传统规划，无需遥操作员技能和在线策略更新。

Result: 四个真实世界任务中，仅2-2.5小时自主数据收集即达96%平均成功率，最强基线为55%。在分布外场景中性能保持高水平。

Conclusion: 为接触密集任务的针对性数据收集和高精度应用的高成功率铺平了道路。

Abstract: Learned policies trained end-to-end on large datasets often remain brittle in high-precision tasks and struggle with generalization. We find that these limitations largely stem from a lack of structure and focus in data collection. Our key insight is to leverage dense data collection only for the critical segment of contact-rich tasks and to rely on traditional planning during simple free-space motion. We propose an automated data-collection scheme in combination with offline deep reinforcement learning for the critical segment of the task, eliminating reliance on a teleoperator's skill and on online policy updates. Across four challenging real-world tasks, using only 2 to 2.5 hours of autonomous data collection, we achieve an average success rate of 96%, compared to the strongest baseline at 55%. Notably, performance remains high in out-of-distribution scenarios where end-to-end approaches struggle. Our results pave the way for targeted data collection for contact-rich tasks and for high success rates in precision applications.

</details>


### [64] [A Morphing-Designed Hexarotor Prototype combining Practical Resilience and Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16002)
*Murat Bronz,Mahmoud Hamandi,Elgiz Baskaya,Chiara Gabellieri,Antonio Franchi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过实验验证了名为Opti-Hexa的六旋翼原型机，该原型机利用新型开源变形平台，通过调节相邻螺旋桨夹角，在保证单螺旋桨故障下实用鲁棒性的同时，实现了与标准星形原型机相当的能量效率，并确定了几何可行区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有实用鲁棒性设计通常伴随气动效率损失，本文旨在探索是否存在一种六旋翼构型，能够同时实现单桨故障鲁棒性和与传统星形构型相当的能量效率。

Method: 采用开源变形平台，通过改变相邻螺旋桨夹角探索连续几何空间；利用数据拟合的经验功率模型研究实际效率；通过对比故障时位置精度和旋转动能与标称悬停状态评估实际鲁棒性。

Result: 实验证实了Opti-Hexa存在一个几何可行区域，在该区域内无需牺牲气动效率即可保证鲁棒性，且能量效率与标准星形原型机相当。

Conclusion: Opti-Hexa实现了单桨故障鲁棒性与能量效率的兼顾，且硬件和软件已开源以支持进一步研究。

Abstract: This work demonstrates experimentally the existence of a hexarotor prototype, termed Opti-Hexa, that simultaneously achieves practical resilience to single-propeller failures and energy efficiency comparable to a standard Star-shaped prototype with the same size, weight, hardware and software. Leveraging a novel open-source morphing platform, we investigate the trade-offs across a continuous range of geometries by varying the angles between adjacent propellers. We study practical efficiency through a data-fitted empirical power model and evaluate practical resilience by comparing the position accuracy and rotational kinetic energy during failure to those observed under nominal hovering conditions. Our experiments confirm the existence of a geometric viability region for this specific morphing platform, where resilience is ensured without the aerodynamic efficiency losses typically associated with practically resilient designs found in the state of the art. The complete hardware and software of the morphing platform are released to support further research.

</details>


### [65] [Let the Body Follow: Coupled Egocentric Control for Whole-Body Robot Teleoperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16095)
*Tsung-Chi Lin,Yichen Xie,Chien-Ming Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出耦合自我中心控制方法，让机器人躯干和底座自动跟随操作者头部和手臂运动，在全身遥操作中提升效率、降低工作负荷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全身遥操作需要用户同时协调机器人的头部、手臂、躯干和底座等多个部件，同时维持任务感知并避免运动学或环境约束，操作难度大。

Method: 提出耦合自我中心控制方法：头部俯仰调节躯干高度，头部偏航驱动底座旋转，末端执行器高度调节躯干运动，末端执行器工作空间边界触发底座平移。

Result: 在TIAGo移动操作手家庭护理任务用户研究中，相比基线混合接口，该方法提升了物体操作效率，减少了按键控制负担和手臂奇异位形，降低了心理需求和整体工作负荷，提高了易用性、易学性、信心以及用户对躯干和底座控制的偏好。

Conclusion: 耦合自我中心控制通过让机器人自主响应操作者自然运动，有效简化了全身遥操作的协调难度，是一种更直观高效的控制范式。

Abstract: Whole-body teleoperation requires users to coordinate perception, manipulation, posture, and mobility across multiple robot components. This coordination is difficult because users must simultaneously control the robot's head, arms, torso, and base while maintaining task awareness and avoiding kinematic or environmental constraints. In this paper, we propose coupled egocentric control, a body-following teleoperation approach in which the robot's torso and base automatically respond to the operator's head and arm motions. Rather than requiring explicit touchpad commands for every torso or base adjustment, the system lets users focus on gaze and hand control: head pitch adjusts torso height, head yaw drives base rotation, end-effector height adjusts torso motion, and end-effector workspace boundaries trigger base translation. We evaluate this approach in a user study on whole-body teleoperation of a TIAGo mobile manipulator for home-care-inspired tasks. Compared with a baseline hybrid interface, coupled egocentric control improves object manipulation efficiency, reduces button-based control effort and arm singularities, lowers mental demand and overall workload, and increases ease of use, ease of learning, confidence, and user preference for torso and base control.

</details>


### [66] [BayesContact: Uncertain Pose Estimation via Visuo-Tactile Proposals and Simulation-based Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16123)
*Aditya Kamireddypalli,Matias Mattamala,Joao Moura,Russell Buchanan,Sethu Vijayakumar,Subramanian Ramamoorthy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出BayesContact，一种基于仿真的推断框架，用于视觉-触觉融合的位姿估计，在销孔插入任务中相比纯视觉方法提升30%的插入成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富的操作任务需要高精度位姿估计，而纯深度传感器难以满足需求；现有视觉-接触融合方法需要昂贵的离线训练且难以泛化到新环境。

Method: 基于仿真推断框架，维护物体位姿的粒子信念，融合深度观测与力/力矩触觉证据；针对每位姿假设，用渲染器预测深度、用物理仿真器预测接触结果，与真实观测对比更新信念；利用多模态信念的信息增益主动选择探测动作。

Result: 在仿真和真实机器人实验中，相比纯视觉推理，位姿可观测性和插入成功率提升30%。

Conclusion: 仿真驱动的推断框架能有效融合视觉与触觉信息进行位姿估计，无需重新训练即可适应新环境和新几何形状，且多模态信念支持主动信息采集。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation requires pose estimates that are often more accurate than what depth-only sensing provides. Existing methods, relying on vision and contact, employ costly offline training procedures that need to be retrained for new environments and geometries. We propose BayesContact, a Simulation-Based Inference framework for visuo-tactile pose estimation in peg-in-hole insertion. BayesContact maintains a particle belief over object pose and fuses depth observations with force/torque-derived contact evidence. We employ simulation based forward models to approximate these observation likelihoods. For each pose hypothesis, a renderer predicts depth measurements and a physics simulator predicts contact outcomes under guarded probing actions; both are scored against real observations to update the belief. The resulting multimodal belief also enables information-gain-based probing for active disambiguation. Across simulated geometries and real-robot experiments, BayesContact improves pose observability and insertion success over vision-only inference by 30%

</details>


### [67] [A New Implementation of NeoSLAM and a Comparative Evaluation with RatSLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16143)
*Joao Victor T. Borges,Fabio Coelho,Paulo Padrao,Jose Fuentes,Ramon R. Costa,Liu Hsu,Leonardo Bobadilla*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于ROS2的NeoSLAM模块化重实现，通过现代框架实现实时执行，并在三个数据集上与RatSLAM进行了对比评估，结果表明新实现在处理吞吐量上优于原始版本，在地图重建上达到与RatSLAM相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始NeoSLAM算法在实时执行方面存在局限，且缺乏模块化架构，难以适应现代机器人系统的需求。本文旨在通过重构NeoSLAM，实现实时执行并减少输入数据丢弃，同时系统性地对比其与RatSLAM的性能差异。

Method: 将NeoSLAM完全重写为模块化架构，采用现代框架（ROS2）进行实现，并在三个不同环境条件的数据集上，与RatSLAM进行映射一致性和轨迹重建的对比评估。

Result: 新NeoSLAM实现相比原始版本在处理吞吐量上显著提升，能够实现实时执行且最小化数据丢弃；在地图重建质量上与RatSLAM表现相当。

Conclusion: 基于ROS2的模块化NeoSLAM实现具有良好的实时应用可行性，在吞吐量上超越原始版本，同时保持了与RatSLAM可比的地图重建性能，验证了该实现的实际应用价值。

Abstract: This paper presents a new implementation of the NeoSLAM algorithm. The proposed version is a complete rewrite of NeoSLAM into a modular architecture using modern frameworks that, together, enable real-time execution with minimal discarding of input data. This work also provides a comparative evaluation between NeoSLAM and RatSLAM across three datasets under varying environmental conditions. The experimental results highlight differences in mapping consistency and trajectory reconstruction, demonstrating the effectiveness and practical applicability of the proposed ROS2-based implementation. The results indicate that the new NeoSLAM outperforms the original in terms of processing throughput for real-time applications and achieves comparable performance to RatSLAM in terms of map reconstruction across the evaluated datasets.

</details>


### [68] [VTLoc: Learning-based Tactile Contact Localization in Visual Point Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16146)
*Zhiyuan Wu,Zhuo Chen,Shan Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出VTLoc框架，通过几何多模态对齐模块和迭代定位更新器，利用3D点云和触觉数据实现接触点定位，在100个真实物体数据集上提升了单点接触定位精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉提供全局物体信息，触觉提供局部接触信息，但两者空间对齐困难，现有方法难以准确预测触点在物体表面的位置。

Method: VTLoc包含两个关键模块：1) 几何多模态对齐模块，从融合的视觉-触觉特征重建伪点云并与视觉点云对齐；2) 迭代定位更新器，利用融合特征迭代优化预测的接触位置。

Result: 在100个真实物体的新基准上评估，VTLoc提升了单次接触定位性能，减少了局部到全局的对应模糊性。

Conclusion: VTLoc通过几何对齐和迭代优化有效解决了视觉-触觉空间对齐难题，为多模态接触定位提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Vision and touch are complementary modalities essential for robotic perception and manipulation. While vision provides global object context, touch offers precise local information at contact points. Integrating these modalities for contact localization, i.e., predicting the location of touch on an object's surface, poses significant challenges due to the need for accurate spatial alignment between tactile data and visual geometry. To address this challenge, we propose VTLoc, a novel visual-tactile framework that localizes contact points from tactile readings using a 3D point cloud as visual input. VTLoc introduces two key components: a geometric multi-modal alignment module, which reconstructs a pseudo-point cloud from fused visual-tactile features and aligns it with the visual point cloud to enforce spatial consistencies across modalities; and an iterative localizing updater, which iteratively refines the predicted contact location using fused visual-tactile features. Evaluated on a new benchmark of 100 real-world objects, VTLoc improves single-touch contact localization by reducing local-to-global correspondence ambiguity.

</details>


### [69] [Vision-Language-Motion Maps: An Open-Vocabulary, Uncertainty-Aware, Queryable Motion Attribute for 3D Scene Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16173)
*Dibyendu Ghosh,Ayushi Shakya*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VLMM是一种开放词汇、自然语言可查询的3D地图，每个元素融合了语义运动先验和几何观测运动，并带有不确定性量，支持区分已运动、可运动但未运动和静止物体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放词汇3D地图仅适用于静态场景，无法回答关于场景元素行为（如运动属性）的查询。

Method: 为地图每个元素融合两种运动属性：VLM/LLM语义运动先验 + 几何观测的跨帧运动，并附加逐元素不确定性；查询降级为属性过滤器。

Result: 模拟器消融实验表明语义与观测运动字段不可替代；真实数据集上不确定性通道一致提升运动检测AP并减少误报；后验等温校准达到0.10 ECE。

Conclusion: VLMM是首个同时具备开放词汇、语言可查询、融合先验-观测运动、逐元素不确定性四项属性的地图表示。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary 3D maps let robots answer language queries about what and where, but they assume a static world and cannot answer queries about how scene elements behave. We introduce Vision-Language-Motion Maps (VLMM), an open-vocabulary, natural-language-queryable 3D map in which each element carries a fused motion attribute: a VLM/LLM semantic movability prior combined with geometrically observed cross-frame motion, together with a per-element uncertainty. Queries reduce to attribute filters that distinguish what has been seen to move, what could move but has not, and what stays still. On a controlled simulator benchmark with exact ground truth (AI2-THOR, three scene types) we show through ablation that the schema fields are non-substitutable: a semantic-only baseline fails motion queries even with strong features, and neither motion field substitutes for the other (the prior cannot answer "what is moving," observed motion cannot answer "what could move"). On real dynamic RGB-D (TUM and Bonn, six sequences) we show the uncertainty channel-our key difference from prior fused-motion work-consistently improves moving-vs-static average precision and reduces false motion flags, and is robust to estimated (noisy) poses. The raw confidence is not calibrated, but post-hoc isotonic calibration reaches an expected calibration error of 0.10. VLMM is a representation contribution: the closest prior maps each lack at least one of the four properties-open-vocabulary, language-queryable, fused prior-and-observed motion, and per-element uncertainty-that our combination provides.

</details>


### [70] [Handroid: Bridging Dexterous Hand and Humanoid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16187)
*Ruogu Li,Chenyang Ma,Sikai Li,Zhenyu Wei,Yunchao Yao,Haochen Shi,C. Karen Liu,Shuran Song,Mingyu Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Handroid是一种桌面级双形态机器人平台，通过重新配置同一套27自由度机电系统，既可作为灵巧手进行精细操作，也可作为33厘米高的人形机器人进行移动和全身交互，并提供了统一的控制与学习框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有灵巧手和人形机器人通常作为两种独立的形态分别开发，前者擅长物体尺度的接触丰富操控，后者适合人机环境中的移动和全身交互，缺乏一个能将两者能力集成在同一可重构平台上的紧凑解决方案。

Method: Handroid复用同一套27自由度机电系统，在灵巧手形态下20个自由度构成拟人化手部结构，在人形形态下重新配置为带头部、手臂和腿部的桌面人形机器人（含12自由度下肢结构）。平台提供统一的控制与学习框架，支持手部遥操作、灵巧抓取、手内操作、人形 locomotion、步态生成和交互式动作编排。

Result: 通过真实世界的灵巧操作、基于强化学习的 locomotion、关键帧动作部署以及包含形态重配置、移动、对接和灵巧抓取放置的长时域任务验证了平台的有效性。

Conclusion: Handroid是一个紧凑且可复现的平台，为形态可重构机器人和跨形态机器人学习提供了新的研究方向。

Abstract: Dexterous hands and humanoid robots are typically developed as distinct embodiments: the former enable contact-rich manipulation at the object scale, whereas the latter provide mobility and whole-body interaction in human-centered environments. We introduce \textbf{Handroid}, a desktop-scale dual-embodiment robot that integrates both capabilities within a single reconfigurable platform. Handroid reuses one 27-DoF electromechanical body as either a dexterous hand or a desktop humanoid, measuring 0.33 m in height and 2.05 kg in weight. In the dexterous hand embodiment, 20 DoFs form an anthropomorphic hand closely matching the kinematic structure of the human hand. In the humanoid embodiment, the same articulated modules are reconfigured into a humanoid with a head, arms, and legs, including a 12-DoF lower-limb structure for locomotion and whole-body motion. Handroid further provides a unified control and learning framework supporting hand teleoperation, dexterous grasping, in-hand manipulation, humanoid locomotion, gait generation, and interactive motion authoring. We validate the platform through real-world dexterous manipulation, reinforcement-learning-based locomotion, keyframe motion deployment, and a long-horizon task involving embodiment reconfiguration, locomotion, docking, and dexterous pick-and-place. These results position Handroid as a compact and reproducible platform for advancing morphology-reconfigurable robotics and cross-embodiment robot learning.

</details>


<div id='math.NA'></div>

### [71] [A persistent-homology-Gaussian prior for solving infinite-dimensional Bayesian inverse scattering problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15289)
*Zhiyuan Wang,Hang Qi,Xiaofei Guan,Zhiliang Deng,Xiaomei Yang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种持久同调-高斯（PHG）先验，用于无限维贝叶斯框架下的声学障碍物散射反问题，通过结合加权持久性正则化项与周期高斯参考测度，实现了对不连续或陡变目标函数的准确稳定重建，并建立了后验测度的适定性及有限维近似收敛性理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统高斯先验无法有效重建实际应用中（如障碍物重建）出现的不连续或陡变目标函数；尽管混合先验已显示出潜力，但在工程应用中仍缺乏理论严谨且计算可处理的理论框架。

Method: 提出持久同调-高斯（PHG）先验，通过吉布斯倾斜将加权持久性正则化项与周期高斯参考测度结合；利用单位圆上的对数径向函数表示复杂边界，将远场数据重建转化为函数空间反问题；采用预条件Crank-Nicolson（pCN）方法进行后验采样。

Result: 在Hellinger、全变差和Wasserstein-p度量下建立了后验测度的适定性；获得了有限维后验近似的收敛性；数值实验表明，PHG先验在更广泛的噪声条件下能实现准确稳定的重建，对多尺度拓扑特征具有显式控制能力，性能优于传统先验。

Conclusion: PHG先验为函数空间贝叶斯反问题中处理不连续/陡变目标提供了一种理论严谨且计算可行的新框架，尤其适用于声学障碍物散射等工程应用场景。

Abstract: Bayesian inference methods have been developed to address inverse problems in function spaces where the unknown parameters are of infinite dimension. However, conventional Gaussian priors remain inadequate for reconstructing discontinuous or sharply varying target functions encountered in practical applications like obstacle reconstruction. Although hybrid priors have emerged as a promising solution, significant challenges remain in developing theoretically rigorous and computationally tractable frameworks in engineering applications. To address these issues, we propose a persistent-homology-Gaussian (PHG) prior for solving the acoustic obstacle scattering inverse problem in the infinite-dimensional Bayesian setting, which combines a weighted persistence-based regularization term with a periodic Gaussian reference measure through a Gibbs tilt. Then, the complex boundary is represented by a log-radial function on the unit circle, so that the reconstruction from far-field data is formulated as a function-space inverse problem. The well-posedness of the resulting posterior measure is established in the Hellinger, total variation, and Wasserstein-\(p\) metrics. Furthermore, the convergence of finite-dimensional posterior approximations is obtained, and posterior sampling is performed by a preconditioned Crank--Nicolson (pCN) method. Numerical experiments show that the proposed PHG prior yields accurate and stable reconstructions under more extensive noisy conditions, providing explicit control of multiscale topological features and better performance compared to other conventional priors.

</details>


### [72] [An FFT-Based Direct Splitting Method for Efficient Micromagnetic Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15290)
*Hang Qi,Changqing Ye,Xiaofei Guan*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert方程的数值模拟，提出了一种基于切平面公式的矩阵自由预处理方法。通过将离散问题转化为广义鞍点系统，结合快速傅里叶变换和分裂迭代法设计了高效预处理器，并推广至Crank-Nicolson格式。数值实验验证了该方法在约束保持、计算效率和鲁棒性方面的优越性，适用于大规模微磁模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert方程因非线性、非凸单位长度约束和非局部场贡献，数值模拟极具挑战。现有隐式或半隐式格式虽无条件稳定，但需反复求解非线性或线性化系统，计算成本高昂。本文旨在设计更高效的数值方案以降低计算开销。

Method: 重新审视切平面公式，将每时间步离散问题转化为广义鞍点系统；利用该结构，结合FFT技术和分裂迭代法开发了隐式欧拉格式的矩阵自由预处理器；分裂方法中各组件通过快速泊松求解器直接高效实现；进一步将Crank-Nicolson格式纳入同一算法框架，可直接复用预处理器和求解器。

Result: 2D和3D数值实验表明该方法精确保持约束、计算效率显著提升、求解器性能鲁棒，在大规模微磁模拟中具有良好的可扩展性。

Abstract: The Landau--Lifshitz--Gilbert equation poses significant challenges for numerical simulation due to its nonlinearity, nonconvex unit-length constraint, and nonlocal field contributions. To enhance geometric consistency of the tangent vector and reduce projection steps, a Crank--Nicolson scheme is further investigated, retaining the same algorithmic structure as the Euler scheme, thus allowing direct reuse of preconditioners and solvers. Extensive 2D and 3D numerical experiments are conducted to validate the framework, demonstrating accurate constraint preservation, improved computational efficiency, and robust solver performance. Abstract: We develop a physics-informed neural network (PINN) framework for nonlocal partial differential equations arising in dynamic density functional theory (DDFT). Such equations are challenging for standard PINN methods because they involve nonlinearities, nonlocal interaction terms, and an underlying gradient-flow structure, often leading to slow convergence and difficult optimization. We adapt the PINN methodology to DDFT gradient-flow equations and introduce two computational components: a modified Lorentzian activation function that behaves approximately linearly for small inputs and decays toward zero as the input magnitude increases, and a precomputed discrete operator for evaluating the nonlocal convolution term efficiently during training. The method is tested on four examples in one and two space dimensions. In the first example, the exact stationary solution is known, while in the remaining cases the neural-network approximations are validated against reference solutions computed using continuous and discontinuous Galerkin finite element discretizations. Accuracy and physical consistency are assessed through $L^1$, $L^2$, and $L^\infty$ errors, together with mass conservation and free-energy dissipation. The results show that the proposed activation function accelerates convergence relative to the standard $\tanh$ function, while the overall framework maintains good agreement with the reference solutions and captures the expected gradient-flow behaviour. These findings demonstrate the potential of the proposed PINN framework for solving nonlocal gradient-flow equations arising in DDFT.

Abstract: The time-domain Boundary Element Method (BEM) for linear elastodynamics with vanishing initial conditions is considered. Spatial discretization uses standard low-order boundary elements, while temporal discretization employs the generalized Convolution Quadrature (gCQ) method. The gCQ framework requires evaluating BEM matrices in the Laplace domain at several complex frequencies along a chosen contour, producing a three-dimensional tensor with one spatial matrix slice per frequency. To reduce storage and computational cost, a low rank approximation of the tensor is computed using 3D-Adaptive Cross Approximation (3D-ACA), extending the classical ACA to handle both the additional frequency dimension and the tensorial structure of elastodynamics. Within each frequency slice, the BEM matrices are further compressed using either the classical ACA algorithm using the $\mathcal{H}$-matrix approach or a Chebyshev interpolation based Fast Multipole Method (FMM). A comparative study of all proposed methods is carried out using two academic examples, and the structural vibration of an induction machine is analyzed.

</details>

Method: 对RCLUPPr算法进行全面的舍入误差分析，该算法先对高瘦矩阵执行带部分选主元的LU分解（预处理），再进行后续随机化步骤。论文还提出了针对实际实现的加速策略。

Abstract: In this work, we present the comprehensive rounding error analysis of RCLUPPr proposed in \cite{RCLUPP}, which is a novel randomized CholeskyQR-type algorithm performing LU decomposition with partial pivoting (LUPP decomposition) directly on the tall-skinny $X\in\mathbb{R}^{m\times n}$ with $m \ge n$ and $\mbox{rank}(X)=n$. In contrast to the existing RCLUPP in \cite{RCLUPP}, which applies matrix sketching before LUPP decomposition, RCLUPPr places LUPP decomposition as a preconditioning step first, significantly reducing error propagation. Our analysis rigorously proves that RCLUPPr enjoys markedly better applicability to the ill-conditioned matrices than the existing CholeskyQR-type algorithms and remains stable and accurate in the mixed-precision arithmetic. We further propose practical acceleration strategies in the real implementations of RCLUPPr. Extensive numerical experiments on the real-world problems confirm the theoretical results in this work, demonstrating the robustness and practicality of RCLUPPr in the single, double, and the mixed-precision architecture.

Abstract: Assessing the asymptotic stability of linear self-adjoint homogeneous systems of differential-algebraic equations requires testing the Hurwitz stability of the associated Hermitian matrix polynomial $P(λ)$. We establish several novel sufficient conditions for stability, based on the numerical range of $P(λ)$. Based on the new results, we propose algorithms with $O(d n^3)$ asymptotic complexity. Our methods rely on very efficient core numerical linear algebra routines, such as the Cholesky decomposition of $n \times n$ matrices or the computation of the largest eigenvalue of $n \times n$ definite pencils. Therefore, a significant computational advantage can be expected in favor of the proposed approach even for moderate values of $d$ or $n$, and we verify this with numerical experiments.

Abstract: We prove a finite-sample formulation gap for physics-informed learning of nonlinear multiscale elliptic equations. For a uniformly monotone divergence-form class with coefficients oscillating at scale $ε$, we derive a finite-width, finite-sample, and finite-iteration error bound for a boundary-compatible variational neural solver. Its stability, sampling, and optimization constants are independent of $ε$, while all unresolved scale dependence is isolated in the best-approximation error.
  We then construct a minimal obstruction witness for a one-dimensional periodic diffusion equation with cubic reaction. Already on the one-parameter family $v_c(x)=cx(1-x)$, the empirical Rademacher complexity of the strong residual is bounded below by a constant multiple of $(ε\sqrt{N})^{-1}$, while that of the squared strong-residual loss is bounded below by a constant multiple of $(ε^2\sqrt{N})^{-1}$. These are optimizer-independent properties of the sampled residual and loss classes, rather than neural-tangent-kernel conditioning statements. The corresponding variational energy complexity is bounded above by a constant multiple of $N^{-1/2}$ uniformly in $ε$.
  A tensor-product construction shows that the same obstruction rates persist in every spatial dimension. Numerical evaluation gives fitted exponents $0.9971$, $1.9860$, and $-0.0028$ for the strong residual, squared strong loss, and variational energy, respectively. Thus, differentiating the microscopic coefficient creates finite-sample statistical ill-conditioning. The variational formulation removes this statistical penalty but does not remove the separate multiscale approximation problem.

Abstract: Can a model be identified from one experiment? We study analytic systems that are linearly parameterized by a combination of prescribed dictionary terms, such as partial differential operators and dynamical systems. For a single input-response pair, recovery is possible exactly when the evaluated dictionary terms are linearly independent. We prove a sharp zero-one law: either no input uniquely determines the coefficients, or almost every random input sampled from a nondegenerate Gaussian measure does. This dichotomy reduces one-shot system identification to a question about degenerate inputs and provides an a posteriori certificate for any recovered model. Numerical examples recover dynamical systems, nonlinear partial differential equations, and structured matrix families from single trajectory data, while also detecting when an extra probe is necessary.

</details>

Abstract: This paper presents a practical guide to building lightweight neural-network surrogates for the plastic return-mapping process in concurrent multiscale (FE2) simulations. Rather than proposing a new architecture, we show how a deliberately simple feed-forward network, structured to mirror the classical return-mapping update, can replace the prohibitively expensive nested fine-scale solves that dominate the cost of conventional FE2 schemes based on FFT homogenization at the meso-scale. We walk through the full workflow: generating training data from incremental homogenization analyses, constructing a compact yet sufficient dataset, embedding material symmetries directly into the mapping, and deploying the trained network as a user-defined material subroutine (UMAT) in a standard finite-element solver -- enabling widespread use. A sensitivity study examines the model's robustness to data density, increment size, and mesh refinement, and we characterize the regimes in which the surrogate holds and where it breaks down. For the macroscopically isotropic, two-dimensional plane-stress setting considered here, the surrogate reproduces the reference response while reducing the per-analysis cost from hours to seconds with speed-ups up to 30,000 over standard FE2. The approach extends naturally to three dimensions and to weaker symmetry assumptions, given an appropriate sampling strategy and dataset.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 在漂移满足多项式Lipschitz和单边Lipschitz条件、扩散满足标准正则性假设下，建立了PRM方法的一步均方稳定性估计和局部残差界，证明了PRM方法在L2意义下达到一阶强收敛。数值实验验证了理论收敛阶，并展示了方法对不可微、超线性增长漂移SDE的适用性。

Conclusion: PRM方法成功解决了现有Milstein型方法处理不可微超线性增长漂移系数时的局限性，在更宽松的假设下实现了一阶强收敛，拓展了随机化Milstein方法的应用范围。

Abstract: We propose a projected drift-randomized Milstein (PRM) method for stochastic differ ential equations with non-differentiable and super-linearly growing drift coefficients. The method extends the randomized Milstein approach beyond the globally Lipschitz setting by incorporating a drift projection into the randomized quadrature approximation. Moreover, unlike existing first-order Milstein-type methods for SDEs with super-linearly growing drift coefficients, the proposed method does not require spatial differentiability of the drift coefficient. Under suitable polynomial Lipschitz and one-sided Lipschitz conditions on the drift, together with standard regularity assumptions on the diffusion, we establish a one-step mean-square stability estimate and derive the required local residual bounds. These esti mates yield first-order strong convergence of the PRM method in the L2-sense. Numerical experiments confirm the theoretical convergence rate and demonstrate the applicability of the method to SDEs with non-differentiable and super-linearly growing drifts.

</details>


### [82] [Accelerated Exact Recovery from Noisy Data via Averaging and Noise-Aware Adaptive Bregman-Kaczmarz](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16003)
*Lionel Tondji,Abakar A. Mahamat,Idriss Tondji*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了自适应Bregman-Kaczmarz方法的块版本收敛性，证明块平均能实现单调收敛加速（增益由系统矩阵稳定秩决定），并提出噪声感知加权策略以应对异构噪声，同时分析了自适应步长在精确恢复中的插值行为。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: (1) 将块求和替换为块平均，将分析归结为单个半正定矩阵，证明收敛保证随批量大小单调提升，总增益由系统矩阵的稳定秩决定。(2) 提出噪声感知加权策略，对不可靠测量降权，证明当噪声与行范数不成比例时（实践中几乎总是），该策略严格优于均匀加权。(3) 分析自适应步长在快速初始阶段与缓慢衰减尾部之间的自动插值行为，并给出无需真解的超参数估计方法。

Result: 块平均保证收敛速度随批量大小单调提升，增益受系统矩阵稳定秩控制；噪声感知加权在异构噪声下严格优于均匀加权；自适应步长自动在快速初始收敛与精确零误差收敛之间插值；数值实验验证了上述理论发现及启发式步长估计的有效性。

Conclusion: 块平均是比块求和更优的并行化策略，保证了批量增大带来的收敛加速；噪声感知加权与块平均两种改进兼容且收益可叠加；自适应步长的超参数可在无先验知识下有效估计，使方法实用化。

Abstract: The adaptive Bregman-Kaczmarz method recovers the exact, noise-free solution of a linear inverse problem even when every measurement it queries is corrupted, provided the corruption is fresh, independent and zero-mean. A block version was proposed for parallel hardware, but whether larger blocks actually converge faster was left open. We show the answer hinges on how the block is used: replacing the block sum with a block average collapses the analysis onto a single positive-semidefinite matrix, through which we prove that the guaranteed convergence improves monotonically with the batch size, with a total gain governed by the stable rank of the system matrix. We then address heterogeneous noise by introducing a noise-aware weighting that down-weights unreliable measurements, and prove it is strictly better than uniform weighting whenever the noise is not proportional to the row norms - a condition that essentially never holds in practice. The two improvements are compatible and their benefits combine. Finally, we show the adaptive step size interpolates automatically between a fast initial phase and a slowly vanishing tail that carries the error exactly to zero, and we explain how its hyperparameters can be estimated without knowing the true solution. Numerical experiments under sparse, heterogeneous noise illustrate these findings and confirm that the heuristic estimates produce effective step sizes.

</details>


### [83] [IMEX Schemes for Compressible Flow using Hybridizable Discontinuous Galerkin Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16044)
*Jan Ellmenreich,Edmond K. Shehadi,Philip L. Lederer*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文开发了一种几何分裂的隐式-显式（IMEX）框架用于可压缩流动方程。在刚性区域采用隐式混合化间断伽辽金（HDG）方法，在非刚性区域采用显式间断伽辽金（DG）方法，通过适当的界面条件实现空间耦合，并利用加性龙格-库塔（ARK）方案保持时间同步。数值实验表明该方法在时空上均能达到高阶精度，且能有效缓解几何诱导的刚性，在适当选择隐式区域时，相对完全显式DG方案可实现约50倍的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可压缩流动方程中几何诱导的刚性区域（如高纵横比或局部细化网格区域）会导致显式时间步长受限，计算效率低下。传统统一隐式方法虽能解决刚性但计算代价高，因此需要一种能根据局部刚性自适应切换隐式/显式处理的计算框架，在保持精度的同时提升计算效率。

Method: 提出一种几何分裂的IMEX框架：在刚性区域采用隐式HDG方法（包括混合HDG方法HDG-MX和原始内部惩罚HDG方法HDG-IP两种形式），在非刚性区域采用显式DG方法；通过保守的界面条件实现空间耦合，使用加性龙格-库塔（ARK）方案实现时间同步。

Result: 数值实验验证了IMEX方案在空间和时间上均达到高阶精度；性能研究表明该方法能有效缓解几何诱导的刚性，在适当选择隐式区域的情况下，相对完全显式DG方案可获得最高约50倍的加速比。

Conclusion: 本文提出的几何分裂IMEX框架能够根据局部几何刚性自适应地切换隐式/显式处理，在保持高阶精度的同时显著提升计算效率，为可压缩流动中几何诱导刚性问题提供了一种有效的计算策略。

Abstract: In this work, we develop a geometry-split implicit-explicit (IMEX) framework for the compressible flow equations, wherein stiff regions are treated via an implicit hybridizable discontinuous Galerkin (HDG) method, while non-stiff regions are treated via an explicit discontinuous Galerkin (DG) method. Two implicit formulations are investigated: a mixed HDG method (HDG-MX) and a primal interior-penalty HDG method (HDG-IP). The spatial coupling between the implicit and explicit solutions is achieved in a conservative manner by appropriate interface conditions, while the temporal synchronization is maintained through the use of additive Runge-Kutta (ARK) schemes. We provide a detailed discussion on the computational performance of the resulting IMEX schemes. Verification and validation over a range of numerical experiments confirm that the proposed IMEX schemes achieve high-order accuracy in both space and time. Performance studies further indicate that the approach effectively alleviates geometry-induced stiffness and can provide speedups of up to approximately 50 relative to a fully explicit DG scheme, provided that the implicit region is chosen appropriately.

</details>


### [84] [The $hp$-FEM does not suffer from the pollution effect for piecewise-smooth Helmholtz problems with Gevrey regularity at boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16073)
*Jeffrey Galkowski,Mostafa Meliani,Euan A. Spence*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文证明，在特定光滑性条件下（系数分段光滑、边界Gevrey类），hp-FEM求解Helmholtz散射问题时，当$p\geq 1+\varepsilon \log k$且$hk/p$足够小时，可实现拟最优性（无污染效应），将此前针对解析系数和解析边界的结论推广到更广泛的散射体类别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将hp-FEM无污染效应的理论保证从解析系数/解析边界（Bernkopf et al. 2025; Galkowski et al. 2024）推广到更一般的Gevrey类边界和分段光滑系数情形，覆盖更广泛的实际散射问题。

Method: 考虑含完美匹配层截断的Helmholtz散射问题，散射体含Dirichlet/Neumann/可穿透障碍及变系数。假设解算子关于k多项式有界、系数分段光滑、边界曲面为Gevrey类、边界上的系数及其法向导数为Gevrey类，分析hp-FEM的拟最优性条件。

Result: 当多项式阶$p\geq 1+\varepsilon \log k$且网格步长与阶数之比$hk/p$足够小时，hp-FEM是拟最优的，即不出现污染效应。

Conclusion: 成功将无污染效应的理论保证从解析情形推广到Gevrey类光滑性情形，显著扩展了hp-FEM在高波数Helmholtz问题中的适用范围。

Abstract: We consider the $hp$-FEM applied to the Helmholtz scattering problem with wavenumber $k$, truncated with a perfectly-matched layer. The scatterer consists of a combination of Dirichlet, Neumann, and penetrable obstacles together with variable coefficients. Provided that the Helmholtz solution operator is polynomially bounded in $k$, all coefficients are piecewise smooth, all boundary surfaces are Gevrey and all coefficients restricted to boundary surfaces are Gevrey together with all their normal derivatives, we show that the $hp$-FEM is quasioptimal when $p\geq 1+\varepsilon \log k$ and $hk/p$ is sufficiently small; i.e., the $hp$-FEM does not suffer from the pollution effect. This result generalises the analogous results in both [Bernkopf, Chaumont-Frelet, Melenk 2025] (proved for piecewise analytic coefficients and analytic boundaries) and [Galkowski, Lafontaine, Spence, Wunsch 2024] (proved for smooth coefficients that are analytic near analytic obstacles) to a much larger class of scatterers.

</details>

### [85] [trueform: Fast And Robust Mesh CSG Via Topological Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15905)
*Žiga Sajovic,Dejan Knez*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于精确谓词和拓扑聚合的网格CSG计算方法，通过整数精确的局部排列和两层级身份标识实现水密输出，速度比现有方法快两个数量级，并支持浏览器端交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网格CSG计算中浮点数的舍入会导致几何不一致（如交叉、排序错误），仅基于索引的拓扑能承受实际数值化。现有方法难以在保证精确性的同时兼顾性能。

Method: 1) 使用整数精确谓词在原始输入平面上进行排列，无需精确构造；2) 将成对交集分为5种规范类型（VV、VE、VF、EE、EF）；3) 每个切割面在自身平面内独立排列，通过两层级身份标识跨面保持一致性；4) 通过拓扑聚合（多数投票）解决跨面决策分歧；5) 构建一次排列和域划分，可任意次查询布尔运算。

Result: 与现有技术相比，该方法产生有效、水密的输出，运行速度提升两个数量级，在浏览器中保持交互性。

Conclusion: 通过精确谓词+拓扑聚合的组合，在不依赖全局结构的前提下实现了高效、精确的网格CSG运算。

Abstract: Mesh CSG output is consumed in floating point: however exact the computation, every emitted coordinate is materialised -- rounded to a representable position -- and the next stage can observe crossings and orderings the exact result never had. Only index-based topology survives materialisation. We keep it exact: within the build, the arrangement's radial structure is ordered by exact predicates on the original input planes -- exact without exact constructions -- and where a decision spans faces, the intended answer is recovered by topological aggregation: a majority vote over the disagreeing geometric observations within their topological unit.
  We compute the arrangement locally with integer-exact predicates, every stage a graph problem on graphs it never explicitly constructs. Pairwise intersections are classified into five canonical types (VV, VE, VF, EE, EF), each cut face is arranged in its own plane, and a two-level identity keeps the result consistent across faces with no global structure. The arrangement and its domain partition are built once and queried arbitrarily often: a boolean of any arity is a per-domain bit test, volumetric regions read straight off the partition, and open surfaces -- declared as oriented sheets -- cut volumes through the same algebra. The method is implemented in the header-only trueform library, in C++ with Python and TypeScript bindings. Compared to prior art, it produces valid, watertight output while running up to two orders of magnitude faster, and stays interactive in the browser.

</details>


### [86] [On the Stability of Minimum-Weight Perfect Matching on the Line](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16137)
*Mark de Berg,Ulrike Schmidt-Kraepelin,Andree-Ovidiu Stef*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 本文研究欧氏空间中动态点集的最小权重完美匹配问题，关注在插入/删除操作下保持近似最优解且限制匹配边变化数量的k-稳定算法。在一维情况下，提出了O(√n)-稳定的2-近似算法（证明其子线性稳定性最优），并证明任何o(log n)-稳定算法会导致无界近似比。下界在仅插入情形及二分图变体中也成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典几何优化问题（欧氏空间最小权重完美匹配）在动态场景下缺乏对解稳定性的理论分析，即如何在点集更新时既保持近似质量又限制解的大幅变动（bounded recourse）。

Method: 在一维点集上，通过构造特定的插入/删除序列，利用组合论证和对手策略证明下界；同时设计基于局部调整的匹配维护算法达到上界。

Result: 1. O(√n)-稳定算法可维持2-近似，且该稳定性在子线性范围内最优；2. 任何o(log n)-稳定算法的近似比无界；3. 下界在仅插入和二分图情形均成立。

Conclusion: 在一维欧氏空间中，动态完美匹配的稳定性与近似比之间存在本质权衡：子线性稳定性只能达到常数近似，而亚对数稳定性则完全不可行（无界近似比）。

Abstract: Computing a minimum-weight perfect matching for a point set $P$ in Euclidean space is a classic geometric optimization problem. We consider the problem in a dynamic setting, where pairs of points may be added to or removed from the set $P$. Our focus is on maintaining an approximately optimal solution without making too many changes to the solution. More precisely, we are interested in $k$-stable algorithms, which change at most $k$ edges in the matching after each update to the set $P$. In other words, we consider an online setting (with insertions and deletions) with bounded recourse.
  We study trade-offs between the stability of the algorithm and the approximation ratio of the maintained solution for point sets in $\mathbb{R}^1$. First, we present an $O(\sqrt{n})$-stable algorithm that maintains a $2$-approximation, which we show to be optimal among all algorithms with sublinear stability. Second, we prove that any $o(\log n)$-stable algorithm has unbounded approximation ratio. Our lower bounds hold even in the insertion-only case, while our algorithm works in the fully dynamic case. Moreover, our lower bounds also hold for the bipartite variant of the problem.

</details>


### [87] [Updating zigzag representatives efficiently](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16153)
*Tamal K. Dey,Tao Hou,Dmitriy Morozov*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于R=DV分解的zigzag持续同调代表元更新算法，能够在邻接关系变化的情况下实现二次时间复杂度的高效更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Zigzag持续同调的计算近年来取得了进展，许多问题的复杂度已与非zigzag情况接近，但zigzag代表元的更新效率仍然存在较大的差距。

Method: 基于非zigzag构造的R=DV分解，针对延长或缩短过滤序列两种操作中出现的邻接关系变化，设计了高效的zigzag代表元更新算法。

Result: 尽管存在邻接关系变化，更新仍可在二次时间复杂度内高效完成。

Conclusion: 本文提出的算法有效缩小了zigzag与非zigzag持续同调在代表元更新方面的效率差距。

Abstract: Computation of zigzag persistence has progressed in recent years, with results showing that complexities of many problems closely align with those in the non-zigzag setting. The major efficiency gap now lies in the updating of zigzag representatives. In this paper, we propose efficient algorithms for updating zigzag representatives based on a recent algorithm for extracting zigzag representatives from a $R=DV$ decomposition of a constructed non-zigzag. The main difficulty for designing our update algorithms lies in the adjacency change occurring in two operations that elongate or shorten a filtration. Despite the adjacency change, we find that the update can still be done efficiently in quadratic time.

</details>

### [88] [Field-induced first order transitions and phase coexistence in the Kitaev quantum spin liquid candidate BaCo2(AsO4)2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15346)
*Tanner J. Legvold,Bin Gao,Rong-Zhu Lin,Violet Williams,Tong Chen,Dehong Yu,Chien-Lung Huang,Gage Eichman,Renjie Lui,Benedetta Flebus,Pengcheng Dai,Douglas Natelson*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 对BaCo2(AsO4)2（BCAO）的中子散射研究表明，场诱导的双锯齿到上-上-下（UUD）转变伴随着局域UUD自旋激发与常规自旋波的共存，而UUD到铁磁（FM）转变是一级相变，不存在量子自旋液相；宽激发源于束缚自旋翻转对，低能色散支携带与FM序相同符号的磁化，解释了自旋塞贝克响应的符号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前在BCAO中观察到极化相附近狭窄场区域存在有限残余热导率，暗示可能存在场诱导量子自旋液相，这一争议亟需通过微观自旋动力学探测来澄清。

Method: 利用中子散射技术探测BCAO在不同磁场下的自旋激发谱，并结合建模分析激发机制；同时测量自旋塞贝克系数作为相变响应的探针。

Result: 双锯齿到UUD的转变（~0.15 T）伴随局域UUD激发与自旋波共存；UUD到FM的转变（~0.5 T）为一级相变，UUD与FM相共存，且自旋塞贝克系数出现尖锐响应；宽激发由束缚自旋翻转对产生，低能色散支携带与FM序同号的磁化。

Conclusion: 结果不支持临界场附近存在量子自旋液相；观察到的激发特征可通过经典磁序框架下的束缚自旋翻转对和低能色散支解释，并且这些激发自然地解释了自旋塞贝克信号的符号以及热导率异常。

Abstract: BaCo2(AsO4)2 (BCAO) is an insulating Kitaev quantum spin liquid candidate with a rich low-temperature phase diagram. Below 5 K, it exhibits double-zigzag magnetic order. Upon application of an in-plane magnetic field, the magnetic structure first transforms into an up-up-down (UUD) state near 0.12 T and then enters a fully spin-polarized ferromagnetic (FM) state near 0.5 T. In the narrow field regime close to the polarized phase, a finite residual thermal conductivity has been reported, suggesting a possible field-induced quantum spin liquid phase. Using neutron scattering, we show that the field-induced double-zigzag-to-UUD transition near 0.15 T is accompanied by the emergence of a cluster of localized UUD spin excitations that coexist with conventional spin waves. Upon further increasing field, BCAO undergoes a first-order UUD-to-FM transition with coexistence of UUD and FM phases, accompanied by a sharp response in the spin Seebeck coefficient. These results do not support a quantum spin liquid scenario near the UUD-to-FM critical field. Instead, modeling indicates that the broad excitations arise from bound spin-flip pairs, while a low-lying dispersive branch near the FM phase boundary carries the same sign of magnetization as the FM order. These excitations naturally account for the observed sign of the spin Seebeck response and are likely relevant to the thermal conductivity near the critical field.

</details>


### [89] [Data-efficient reconstruction of critical quantum dynamics via blind fractional-envelope extrapolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15458)
*Hyunju Kim,Hyun-Yong Lee,Heung-Sik Kim*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于分数阶微积分的包络外推方法（fSD），通过分数阶Schwinger-Dyson方程处理量子临界动力学中的幂律连续谱问题，相比传统有限极点方法在临界XXZ链等体系中显著提升了短时动力学外推精度，并能自动识别临界指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模拟量子系统实时动力学常因纠缠增长而限于短时间，有限极点重构方法（如线性预测）在谱为离散激发集时有效，但在临界点处低能谱为幂律连续谱（支点割），有限极点无法表示其长时尾行为，需发展新的外推方法。

Method: 提出分数阶Schwinger-Dyson（fSD）方程框架，其中拉普拉斯符号s^α解析地承载支点割，剩余自能保持亚纯。在实际数据上，在拟合窗口内盲选指数α，用t^α对信号去趋势，对残差用稳定化有限极点模型拟合，最后恢复代数包络。该方法称为盲选分数阶包络法（fSD）。

Result: 在临界XXZ链上，fSD方法比有限极点方法外推精度高数倍，指数α可从拟合窗口盲选得到，在弱耦合下与闭式Luttinger值一致。在Δ=1饱和相变点盲选得到z=2的稀磁振子指数α=1/2，在有能隙的自由磁振子相中也保持优势。在非临界动力学平均场谱（低频响应正则）上，fSD不产生虚假幂律，退化为标准极点结果。

Conclusion: fSD方法为在仅有短时间模拟数据可访问时，高效准确地获取量子临界动力学提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: Simulating real-time dynamics of quantum systems is often limited to short times by entanglement growth. Finite-pole reconstructions such as linear prediction and related machineries extrapolate such data reliably when the spectrum is a finite set of excitations, but at criticality the low-energy spectrum is a power-law continuum $A(ω)\sim|ω|^{α-1}$ -- a branch cut whose real-time tail $G(t)\sim t^{-α}$ finitely many poles cannot represent. Here we develop a fractional-calculus-motivated envelope extrapolation for such data. Its structure is motivated by a fractional form of Schwinger--Dyson (fSD) equation, in which the Laplace symbol $s^α$ carries the branch cut analytically while the residual self-energy remains meromorphic. On real data we employ the corresponding operational alternative -- the exponent $α$ is selected blindly inside the fit window, the signal is detrended by $t^α$, the residual is fitted by a stabilized finite-pole model, and the algebraic envelope is restored. On the critical XXZ chain this blind fractional-envelope method (fSD for short) extrapolates short-time data typically several-fold more accurately than finite-pole methods, with the exponent $α$ identified blindly from the fit window alone and bracketing the closed-form Luttinger value at weak coupling. The same blind search finds the $z=2$ dilute-magnon exponent $α=1/2$ at the $Δ=1$ saturation transition, and the advantage persists in the gapped free-magnon phase with its sharp band edges. On noncritical dynamical mean-field spectra, whose low-frequency response is regular, fSD by contrast fails to select any stable fractional envelope and reduces to the standard pole result rather than manufacturing a spurious power law, making it an efficient and accurate route to quantum critical dynamics when only short simulation times are accessible.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 使用密度泛函理论(DFT)计算识别能带拓扑性质；通过部分Wannier化方法并借助自旋C₃z对称性表示，从DFT波函数构建紧致分子轨道，提取出局域f轨道和互补拓扑c轨道的跳迁和杂化振幅。

Result: 发现tWSe₂顶部价带在相关转角范围内拓扑非平凡，K谷携带Chern数C=(+1,+1)；成功构建了局域f轨道与拓扑c轨道，并提取了第一性原理的哈密顿量参数。

Conclusion: 该工作为理解moire TMDs中的超导机制奠定了基础，并突出tWSe₂是探索拓扑超导的有前景平台。

Abstract: Recent observations of superconductivity in twisted bilayer WSe$_2$ (tWSe$_2$) have motivated theoretical proposals for unconventional pairing mechanisms. A central question is whether band topology plays an essential role in the system's correlation physics. In this letter, we develop a first-principles-based description of the top moiré valence bands in tWSe$_2$. Using density functional theory (DFT) calculations, we identify the bands in the relevant range of twist angles to be topologically non-trivial, with the top valence bands carrying Chern numbers $C=(+1,+1)$ for the $K$ valley. In order to treat the strong correlation physics, we construct compact molecular orbitals directly from the DFT wave functions through a partial Wannierization procedure and with the guidance of spinful $C_{3z}$ symmetry representations. This yields a localized $f$ orbital together with a complementary topological $c$ orbital, allowing us to extract hopping and hybridization amplitudes from first principles. The resulting parameters provide an ab initio benchmark for the effective Hamiltonian. Our work establishes a foundation for understanding superconductivity in moiré TMDs and highlights tWSe$_2$ as a promising platform for exploring topological superconductivity.

</details>

Abstract: We investigate $dd$ excitations in Resonant Inelastic X-ray Scattering spectra of YBa$_2$Cu$_3$O$_6$ and La$_2$CuO$_4$ using the local single-ion model. The data are analyzed by conventional global fitting and by a convolutional neural network trained within the same theoretical framework. For YBa$_2$Cu$_3$O$_6$, the excited state energies obtained with the two methods coincide, leading to the $xy$, $3z^2-r^2$, $xz/yz$ sequence for increasing energy. This result validates the use of machine learning tools for the analysis of RIXS spectra dominated by $dd$ excitations. By contrast, for La$_2$CuO$_4$, the two methods do not converge to a single solution, revealing the limitations of the single-ion model in describing $dd$ excitations in cuprates and pointing to the role of additional contributions beyond a purely local picture in shaping high-energy excitations.
