Abstract: We derive a local spin-1/2 Hamiltonian with a resonating valence bond ground state on the checkerboard lattice. The state is characterized by the exponential decay of singlet-singlet correlations, whereas dimer-dimer correlations decay with a power law in the corresponding Quantum Dimer model. This observation leads to a novel mechanism for thermal Order by Disorder whereby thermal decoherence suppresses destructive quantum interference between different contributions to the correlations in the ground state and results in a qualitatively different, quasi-long-range ordered mixed state.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在没有长程磁有序或轨道有序的情况下，交变磁性是否仍能涌现存在，以拓展交变磁概念到低维和强涨落系统。

Result: 发现当子系统间交换作用超过临界值Kc(T)时，即使每个位点自旋和赝自旋平均值为零，仍会形成非零的自旋-赝自旋关联复合态；激发谱分裂为声学和光学支，沿qx=qy出现节线；一维体系中相边界非单调，存在重入相变。

Conclusion: 确立了一种无长程序的“交变磁顺磁体”或“交变磁液体”概念，为低维和强涨落系统中的交变磁性研究提供了理论依据。

Abstract: The Kugel-Khomskii spin-pseudospin model, originally developed for transition-metal compounds with orbital degrees of freedom, has recently been reinterpreted in the context of altermagnetism. In this work, we theoretically investigate the emergence of altermagnetic behavior in the absence of long-range magnetic or orbital order. Using the rotation-invariant Green's function method for the SU(2) x SU(2) symmetric model on a square lattice and on a linear chain, we analyze spin-spin and spin-pseudospin correlation functions, excitation spectra, heat capacity, and susceptibilities. We show that beyond a critical intersubsystem exchange Kc(T), a composite state arises with nonzero spin-pseudospin correlations, even though the average spin and pseudospin at each site are zero. The excitation spectrum splits into acoustic and optical branches, with nodal lines along qx = qy - a direct signature of altermagnetic symmetry. A peak in heat capacity and a jump in susceptibility are observed at the phase boundary. In 1D, the phase boundary is nonmonotonic and demonstrates reentrant transition. These results establish the concept of an "altermagnetic paramagnet" or "altermagnetic liquid" without long-range order, relevant for low-dimensional and strongly fluctuating systems.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索开放量子自旋系统作为实现退禁闭量子临界性的新途径，揭示耗散对量子相变和自旋子退禁闭行为的影响。

Abstract: Open quantum spin systems offer a previously unexplored route to realizing deconfined quantum criticality. We consider a spin-1/2 $J$-$Q_3$ chain, consisting of an antiferromagnetic (AFM) Heisenberg exchange and a competing multi-spin interaction favoring a valence-bond solid (VBS) state, with each spin component coupled to a bosonic bath. Using non-Abelian bosonization and renormalization-group (RG) analysis, combined with large-scale quantum Monte Carlo (QMC) simulations, we determine the phase diagram and the associated phase transitions of the model. We show that strong dissipation stabilizes an AFM phase for sub-Ohmic, Ohmic, and super-Ohmic baths. Continuous AFM-VBS transitions at finite dissipation are found upon increasing the multi-spin interaction in both the sub-Ohmic and Ohmic regimes. Critical properties are obtained through perturbative RG analysis and QMC simulations. In the Ohmic case, the critical point features spinon deconfinement and emergent O(4) symmetry. In the sub-Ohmic regime, the transition may also involve spinon deconfinement, provided that spinons remain deconfined in the dissipative VBS phase. In addition, in the super-Ohmic regime, we propose a transition from AFM phase to a quasi-long-range ordered phase.

</details>

Motivation: 奇异金属是高度集体性的量子多体系统，需要新的表征手段，量子信息方法有望为此做出贡献。

Abstract: Strange metals exemplify highly collective quantum many-body systems that call for new means of characterization, and there is considerable potential for quantum information approaches contributing to the cause. We investigate multipartite entanglement across the quantum phase transition of a Kondo lattice model using the quantum Fisher information (QFI). We show that the QFI associated with the spin components transverse to the order parameter characterizes the destruction of heavy quasiparticles in the Kondo-destroyed magnetic-ordered phase. The physical origin of this observation is elucidated through an analysis of the antiferromagnetic Heisenberg model. We propose to test the results in terms of both unpolarized and polarized inelastic neutron scattering measurements in the ordered part of the heavy fermion phase diagram. Our findings illustrate how different operators of a many-body system can be employed to not only witness multipartite entanglement in different sectors and but also elucidate the overall physics across different parts of the phase diagram.

</details>

### [96] [Mitigating Detector Ageing Effects with Graph-Based Multi-Modal Track Reconstruction at Belle II](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15361)

TL;DR: 本文评估了基于图神经网络（GNN）的统一径迹寻找算法在Belle II中心漂移室长期探测器老化条件下的性能。通过将径迹寻找建模为使用目标凝聚（object condensation）的全局关系聚类问题，并在包含束流本底、探测器噪声和实测老化效应的全模拟中测试，结果表明：探测器退化可视为击中的领域偏移而非需要全新重建策略；重训练后，GNN方法将绝对径迹效率损失限制在14%（基线算法为28%），同时保持96%的径迹纯度（基线为90%），证明了GNN算法对不规则击中模式和扩展非活跃区域的稳健性。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 将径迹寻找问题建模为基于目标凝聚（object condensation）的全局关系聚类问题，使用统一的图神经网络（GNN）框架进行求解；在包含束流本底、探测器噪声和实测老化效应的全探测器模拟中评估性能，并与Belle II基线重建算法对比。

Result: 在探测器退化条件下，GNN方法将均匀位移μ子的绝对径迹效率损失限制在14%（基线为28%），同时保持96%的径迹纯度（基线为90%）。

Conclusion: 统一的GNN径迹重建算法对不规则击中模式和扩展的非活跃区域具有更强的鲁棒性，能够在长期探测器老化条件下保持稳定的径迹重建性能，且探测器退化可视为击中模式的领域偏移，通过重训练即可适应。

Abstract: Large backgrounds that can lead to hardware failures and the degradation of detector gain impact the track finding in the Belle II central drift chamber. These conditions lead to spatially non-uniform and time-dependent inefficiencies, which results in inactive regions and missing hits which challenges conventional tracking algorithms and necessitate the development of new track finding algorithms. In this work, we evaluate the performance of our previously developed unified graph neural network (GNN) based track-finding algorithm under realistic long-term detector ageing conditions. Track finding is formulated as a global relational clustering problem using object condensation, which enables the reconstruction of an unknown and variable number of tracks per event. Using a realistic full detector simulation incorporating beam-induced backgrounds, detector noise, and measured detector ageing effects, we evaluate the tracking performance and compare it to the current Belle II baseline reconstruction. We show that detector degradation can be treated as a domain shift in the observed hit patterns, rather than requiring a fundamentally new reconstruction strategy. After retraining on degraded detector conditions, the GNN-based approach limits the absolute track efficiency loss for uniformly displaced muons to 14%, compared to 28% for the baseline tracking, while maintaining a track purity of 96%. Under the same conditions, the baseline reconstruction achieves only 90% track purity. These results demonstrate that the unified GNN-based reconstruction provides increased robustness to irregular hit patterns and extended inactive regions, enabling stable tracking performance under long-term detector ageing at Belle II.

</details>

Motivation: Ibn型超新星因其光谱与富氦、贫氢的星周介质相互作用，成为研究恒星晚期质量损失和前身星剥离过程的重要探针，但其物理机制和起源仍需深入理解。

Result: 光谱在极大光后各阶段均以中等宽度发射线为主；最佳模型需X射线辐照功率约10^8 L☉，冷密壳层半径约(1.5–2)×10^15 cm，速度约5×10^7 cm/s，相互作用功率约数个10^42 erg/s；光学波段模型从早期高功率小半径演变为晚期低功率大半径。

Conclusion: SN 2023tsz的观测与模型结果支持至少部分Ibn型超新星起源于受双星相互作用的较低质量氦星，其最终演化由双星过程塑造。

Abstract: Type Ibn supernovae are stripped-envelope explosions whose spectra indicate interaction with dense, helium-rich and hydrogen-poor circumstellar material (CSM), making them important probes of late-stage mass loss and progenitor stripping. We present extensive ultraviolet-to-near-infrared spectrophotometry of the Type Ibn SN 2023tsz, including two epochs of HST/STIS ultraviolet (UV) spectroscopy and ground-based optical and near-infrared follow-up observations. The spectra are dominated by intermediate-width emission lines at all phases after maximum light, suggesting that much of the luminosity originates in a cold dense shell (CDS) formed by interaction between the ejecta and CSM. We compare the observations to one-dimensional non-local-thermodynamic-equilibrium radiative-transfer models of a helium-star explosion with a mass of $4 M_{\odot}$ at the onset of helium burning. The models reproduce the strong optical and near-infrared He I lines and require an added X-ray irradiation field to match the highly ionized UV features. The spectra are best reproduced by models with an X-ray irradiation power of $L_X \approx 10^8 L_{\odot}$, with the preferred models favoring CDS radii of order $(1.5$--$2) \times 10^{15}$ cm, velocities of $\sim 5 \times 10^7$ cm s$^{-1}$, and interaction powers of a few times $10^{42}$ erg s$^{-1}$. In the optical, the preferred models shift from higher interaction power and smaller radii at early times to lower power and larger radii at later times. These results add to the growing evidence that at least some SNe Ibn arise from lower-mass helium stars whose final evolution is shaped by binary interaction.

</details>


### [98] [Observations of X-ray quasi-periodic eruptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15359)
*Thomas Wevers,Joheen Chakraborty,Erwan Quintin,Michal Zajaček,Margherita Giustini*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 准周期爆发（QPEs）是一类新型重复性核瞬变现象，目前仅能在X射线波段观测到。自2019年首次发现以来，样本已扩展至13个源。本文全面综述了其观测特征，包括X射线光谱、时变特性、长期演化、宁静态发射（由热主导的致密吸积盘描述），以及宿主星系属性。同时讨论了与潮汐瓦解事件（TDEs）的强关联性，并总结了理论模型的观测约束与未来观测挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准周期爆发（QPEs）作为一种新发现的核瞬变现象，其物理机制尚不明确，且现有观测样本有限，亟需系统整理已有观测特征以指导理论和观测研究。

Method: 对现有13个QPE源的X射线光谱、时变特性、长期演化、宁静态发射及宿主星系属性进行系统性综述，分析种群趋势和选择效应，并对比潮汐瓦解事件（TDEs）的关联证据。

Result: QPEs显示出大幅、准规则的爆发模式，宁静态发射由热主导的致密吸积盘描述；样本存在选择偏差；观测证据强烈支持QPEs与TDEs存在关联。

Conclusion: QPEs是一类与TDEs密切相关的独特核瞬变现象，现有观测对其理论模型构成了关键约束，未来观测需解决样本偏差并拓展多波段覆盖以推动理解。

Abstract: Quasi-periodic eruptions (QPEs) are a novel class of repeating nuclear transients, discovered exclusively in the X-ray band to date. Since their initial discovery in 2019, the QPE sample has grown to 13 sources, exhibiting large amplitude, quasi-regular eruptive variability patterns that are distinct from previously known modes of massive black hole variability. In this chapter, we provide a comprehensive overview of their observational characteristics. We review the X-ray spectral and timing properties of QPE eruptions, their long-term evolution, as well as the underlying quiescent emission, which is well described by thermally dominated, compact accretion disks. We discuss population-level emerging trends and selection biases, and present an updated census of their host galaxy properties. We also highlight the growing body of evidence pointing to strong connections between QPEs and tidal disruption events. Finally, we briefly summarize the key observational constraints on proposed QPE model interpretations, before looking ahead to the observational challenges and opportunities that will shape future progress in understanding this emerging population of nuclear transients.

</details>


### [99] [Multimessenger prospects of quasi-periodic eruptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15360)
*Vojtěch Witzany,Alessia Franchini,Matteo Bonetti,Luca Broggi*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文探讨了准周期爆发（QPEs）与极端质量比旋进（EMRIs）的联合探测可能性，分析表明大多数已知QPE对应的引力波信号频率不在LISA灵敏度频段内，且存在选择偏差，未来应寻找短周期QPE以实现联合探测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究QPE与LISA对EMRI的联合探测潜力，以提供电磁对应体定位、实现标准汽笛测量哈勃常数并约束形成机制。

Method: 分析已知QPE的轨道周期与对应的引力波频率，评估其与LISA灵敏度频段的重叠情况，并讨论探测选择偏差。

Result: 大多数已知QPE的EMRI轨道周期过长，导致引力波信号频率低于LISA频段；探测选择偏差进一步限制了联合探测的可能性。

Conclusion: 目前QPE与LISA的联合探测面临挑战，未来需扩大QPE样本并寻找短周期"黄金"QPE以实现协同观测。

Abstract: Quasi-Periodic Eruptions (QPEs) are recurring soft X-ray transients that may be generated by inspirals of stellar-mass objects spiraling into supermassive black holes, known as extreme mass ratio inspirals (EMRIs). Independently, EMRIs and the gravitational-wave signals they generate are one of the key targets for the Laser Interferometer Space Antenna (LISA). What is the potential of a coincident detection of EMRIs both as a QPE and by LISA? Electromagnetic counterparts to LISA events would provide sky localization, enable standard siren measurements of the Hubble constant and constrain formation mechanisms of the corresponding inspirals. Combined observations would link the accurate measurements of black hole masses and spins to their galactic nuclear environments and would thus enable lasting synergies with various observations across the electromagnetic spectrum. However, most of the currently known QPEs imply EMRI orbital periods that place the frequencies of the corresponding gravitational-wave signal out of the LISA sensitivity band. Additionally, the selection biases of QPE detections and the LISA instrument may preclude a coincident detection. Future searches should focus on expanding the QPE catalogs and ultimately hunt for ``golden'' short-period QPEs that correspond to EMRIs that fall within the LISA band.

</details>


### [100] [Large-scale emission from gamma-ray binaries: the case of LS 5039](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15402)
*J. R. Martinez,V. Bosch-Ramon*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文通过建模仿真，研究了伽马射线双星LS 5039的大尺度辐射机制，发现其延展X射线辐射最佳解释为同步辐射，并预测了可探测的射电辐射及逃逸质子对银河宇宙线的贡献（能量0.1-1 PeV，注入光度可达~10^36 erg/s）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LS 5039存在延展的秒差距尺度X射线辐射，其起源可能与大规模相互作用结构有关，但目前理解尚不充分，需要系统建模以解释和预测其大尺度多波段辐射特征。

Method: 模拟了五个不同演化阶段场景（三个气泡场景采用单区模型，两个弓形激波场景采用多区域方法），在混合风终止激波处假设粒子加速，并研究了逃逸粒子的辐射贡献。

Result: 延展X射线最佳解释为同步辐射；部分场景预测可探测射电辐射；强弓形激波场景中逃逸粒子可能对伽马射线有微小稳态贡献；逃逸质子（0.1-1 PeV）在乐观注入光度下可向银河宇宙线注入~10^36 erg/s。

Conclusion: 伽马射线双星能在大规模尺度上有效加速粒子，产生宽带辐射并贡献0.1-1 PeV宇宙线；结果可为未来多波段观测提供指导，以约束LS 5039的大尺度相互作用、年龄和诞生地。

Abstract: Abridged abstract: Context: Gamma-ray binaries hosting a non-accreting neutron star and a massive star exhibit multi-wavelength emission on different spatial scales. The interaction between their winds produces an outflow that can inflate a bubble or form a bow shock as it interacts with the surrounding medium. LS 5039 shows extended (1 pc-scale) X-ray emission that may arise from one of these large-scale structures. Aims: We explain and predict the large-scale emission from LS 5039. Methods: We modelled the thermal and non-thermal emission from five scenarios, representing different evolutionary phases, assuming particle acceleration at the mixed-wind termination shock: three bubble scenarios, treated with a one-zone model, and two bow-shock scenarios, studied with a multi-zone approach. We also investigated the radiation from escaping particles. Results: The extended X-rays are best explained as synchrotron radiation. Some scenarios predict detectable radio emission, while escaping particles may provide a minor steady contribution to the gamma rays in the powerful bow-shock scenario. Escaping protons with energies of 0.1-1 PeV could also inject up to $\sim10^{36}$ erg s$^{-1}$ into Galactic cosmic rays for optimistic injection luminosities. Conclusions: Gamma-ray binaries can efficiently accelerate particles on large scales, producing broadband emission and 0.1-1 PeV cosmic rays. Our results can guide future multi-wavelength observations to constrain the large-scale interaction, age, and birthplace of LS 5039.

</details>


### [101] [Uncovering Hierarchical Sub-Population of Binary Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15431)
*Muhammad Zeeshan,Richard O'Shaughnessy*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个原生多成分混合模型来分析259个双黑洞引力波事件的种群特征，发现除最低质量成分外，所有成分的自旋取向均与各向同性一致，且质量峰值呈现近似层次化分布，但未观察到裸层次化形成所预期的自旋-质量相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着引力波探测灵敏度提高，双黑洞样本量已达259个，足以揭示种群亚结构。已有证据表明存在多个形成通道（包括层次化形成），需要更精细的模型来解析不同子种群的特征。

Method: 构建原生多成分混合模型，每个成分独立恢复率、质量、自旋及自旋错向模型，所有成分共享红移分布。采用幂律加五个依次更高质量的高斯成分，并仔细调参避免参数简并。

Result: 总体并合率与质量及红移趋势与先前结果一致。低质量区域呈现优先对齐和低自旋，高质量区域呈现高自旋和各向同性自旋。但与先前研究关键分歧在于：除最低质量成分外，所有成分自旋均与各向同性一致。质量峰值呈近似层次化谱，但未观察到裸层次化形成预期的自旋-质量相关性。

Conclusion: 多成分模型揭示了比单一模型更丰富的种群结构信息，但自旋各向同性的结果挑战了先前关于层次化形成通道的强自旋对齐预期，表明双黑洞形成机制可能比现有层次化模型更复杂。

Abstract: Enabled by improved instruments with increasing sensitivity, the ongoing gravitational wave census now contains 259 binary black holes, numerous enough to unveil trends, substructure, and subpopulations which may provide key clues to their underlying formation mechanisms. In this work, motivated by evidence for multiple formation channels including hierarchical formation, we build a natively multi component mixture model for the binary black hole population, in which each component has an independently recovered rate, mass, spin, and spin misalignment model. (The components share a common redshift distribution.) Using a model carefully tuned to avoid parameter degeneracies, a powerlaw model plus five successively higher mass gaussians, we recover overall merger rates versus mass and trends versus redshift which are consistent with previously published results. Too, we recover previously identified overall trends versus spin: preferential alignment and low spin at low mass; large spin and isotropic spins at high mass. Critically, however, our multi-component model disagrees with previously published results, finding all components except the lowest mass are consistent with isotropy. Too, our multi-component model has a roughly hierarchical spectrum of gaussian mass peaks, but without the expected correlations between spin and mass expected from naked hierarchical formation

</details>

<details>

Motivation: BL Lacertae天体此前观测到的VHE耀发持续时间仅为数分钟至数天，而本次Fermi-LAT探测到持续升高的GeV活动，触发多波段联合观测，旨在研究该源在更长时间尺度上的甚高能辐射行为及其辐射机制。

Method: 利用VERITAS在2022年9月1日至12月1日期间共约9.8小时的观测数据，结合Fermi-LAT、Swift、NuSTAR以及光学和射电观测，进行多波段能谱建模，比较单区SSC模型与SSC+EC模型的拟合效果。

Result: VERITAS以约28σ的显著性探测到该源，且VHE耀发持续约40天，远长于此前已知的分钟至天量级耀发。宽带能谱建模表明SSC+EC模型优于纯SSC模型。

Conclusion: BL Lacertae能产生持续月量级的VHE耀发，且其高能辐射需要引入外部光子场的逆康普顿散射成分来解释，表明该源存在除同步自康普顿之外的外部辐射成分贡献。

Abstract: We report the first observations of a long-duration very-high-energy (VHE; $E > 100$ GeV) flare from BL Lacertae (VER J2202+422), taken with the Very Energetic Radiation Imaging Telescope Array System (VERITAS). On October 15, 2022, the Fermi-Large Area Telescope (LAT) detected elevated GeV activity originating from this blazar. This triggered a multiwavelength campaign, which includes observations from VERITAS, Swift, NuSTAR, and select optical and radio observatories. VERITAS observed the source for a total of $\sim 9.8$ hours between September 1, 2022 and December 1, 2022. An analysis of these data yields a $\sim 28 σ$ detection of the source. While previously observed VHE flares from BL Lacertae have lasted on time-scales of minutes to days, VERITAS continued to detect flaring activity from the source for over a month ($\sim 40$ days) after the original flaring activity was detected with Fermi-LAT. Broadband spectral modeling shows that a synchrotron self-Compton (SSC) model with an external inverse-Compton (EC) component is preferred over a one-zone SSC model.

</details>


### [103] [Triple radio flares from tidal disruption events: jet-wind collisions and the discovery of a third radio flare from AT2020vwl](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15438)
*Andrew Mummery,Adelle Goodwin,Colin Christy,Kate Alexander,Noah Franz*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文提出潮汐瓦解事件（TDE）中观测到的两类射电耀发（瞬发型与延迟型）可由超爱丁顿风与喷流分别解释，并预测同时展现两类耀发的源会出现第三次耀发——高速喷流追上慢速风产生碰撞。作者在TDE AT2020vwl的长期射电监测中发现了第三次耀发，与模型预测的时间一致，并对另外两个TDE（ASASSN-15oi和AT2024tvd）的长期射电演化做出预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释TDE中观测到的两类不同时标的射电耀发（瞬发与延迟）的物理起源，并验证“风-喷流”框架的预言：同时展现两类耀发的源应出现第三次耀发。

Method: 基于“风-喷流”理论框架进行正向建模，预测喷流与风碰撞的时标与观测特征；对TDE AT2020vwl进行长期射电监测，将观测数据与模型预测对比验证。

Result: 在TDE AT2020vwl中发现了第三次射电耀发，其发生时间与风-喷流碰撞模型预测一致；对ASASSN-15oi预测可能观测到弱耀发或光变曲线变平，对AT2024tvd预测即将发生第三次耀发。

Conclusion: TDE中的双射电耀发现象可由超爱丁顿风与喷流在不同吸积阶段依次产生来统一解释，第三次耀发作为该框架的关键可观测预言，在AT2020vwl中得到验证，支持了该理论模型的正确性。

Abstract: Radio flares from tidal disruption events (TDEs) are observed on two distinct timescales: prompt (around the time of optical peak) and delayed (hundreds to thousands of days after optical peak). A recent framework put forward to explain this diversity suggests that super-Eddington winds produce prompt flares while jets launched during a disk state transition at low accretion rates produce delayed flares. A unique observational prediction of this framework is that individual sources showing both types of flares may be expected to show a third flare. This third flare will occur when the (faster) jet catches up with the (slower) wind. The observational appearance of the third flare depends on the system parameters. As the two shock fronts collide the (brighter) jet encounters a change in density, modifying its synchrotron-self-absorption frequency and observed flux. Here we discuss the observational appearance of such a scenario, and then show, in new long-term monitoring radio observations of the TDE AT2020vwl, the discovery of a third radio flare. This flare is coincident with the time at which forward modeling of the first two flares in a wind-jet framework predicts a collision between the two shock fronts. We make predictions for the long-term radio evolution of two other TDEs showing double radio flares: ASASSN-15oi (which may show a weak flare or flattening of the lightcurve) and AT2024tvd (which is predicted to imminently undergo a third flare).

</details>


### [104] [Assessing the waveform systematics from parameter estimation to population inference with eccentricity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15453)
*Muhammad Zeeshan,Richard O'Shaughnessy,Natalie Malagon,Katelyn J. Wagner*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文分析GWTC-4目录中153个双黑洞、2个双中子星和7个中子星-黑洞双星系统，比较两种偏心波形模型（SEOBNRv5EHM和TEOBResumS-DALI）与准圆形波形分析在源参数估计和群体推断上的差异，发现偏心模型在大多数事件上结果一致，但部分事件存在微小系统偏差，这些偏差在分层群体推断中累积，显著影响红移演化和有效自旋分布，且随着目录扩大，波形系统误差重要性增加。同时引入合成数据框架用于测试偏心群体性质的可恢复性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偏心率为动态组装的致密双星系统提供了重要的起源诊断信息，但不同偏心波形模型间的差异可能引入系统效应并传播到天体物理群体推断中，需要系统评估这种影响。

Method: 使用两种偏心波形模型（SEOBNRv5EHM和TEOBResumS-DALI）对GWTC-4目录中的153个BBH、2个BNS和7个NSBH进行源参数和群体级别推断，并与准圆形波形分析进行对比，同时构建合成数据框架进行注入测试。

Result: 两种偏心模型在大多数事件上给出基本一致的源参数估计，但部分事件存在微小且连贯的差异；这些系统性偏移在分层群体推断中累积，导致群体性质（尤其是红移演化和有效自旋分布）的推断差异；事件级偏差随目录规模按√N增长。

Conclusion: 随着引力波目录规模扩大，波形系统误差对群体推断的影响日益重要，需开发合成数据框架来检验偏心群体性质的可恢复性。

Abstract: While masses and spins are routinely used to constrain compact binary formation channels, eccentricity provides an additional and potentially powerful diagnostic of binary origin, particularly for dynamically assembled systems. Recent advances in eccentric waveform modeling now make it possible to search for eccentric signatures in gravitational wave data; however, differences between waveform models can introduce systematic effects that may propagate into astrophysical population inference. In this work, we analyze 153 binary black holes, 2 binary neutron stars and 7 neutron star black hole binaries from the GWTC-4 catalog. We compare the source and population level inferences obtained with two eccentric waveform models, SEOBNRv5EHM and TEOBResumS-DALI, as well as with quasi circular waveform analyses. We find that the two eccentric models give broadly consistent source parameter estimates for most events, but some events exhibit subtle and coherent differences. These small, systematic offsets can accumulate in hierarchical population inference, leading to differences in inferred population properties, most notably in the redshift evolution and effective spin distribution. Because coherent event level biases can grow approximately as $\sqrt{N}$ for a catalog of N events, waveform systematics become increasingly important as gravitational wave catalogs expand. We also introduce a synthetic data framework that generates eccentric populations and corresponding RIFT posterior samples, enabling injection studies that test the recoverability of eccentric population properties.

</details>


### [105] [Implications of the UV/optical Plateau of AT2018cow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15464)
*Wenbin Lu,Anthony L. Piro*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文重新解释了LFBOT原型事件AT2018cow的晚期UV/光学辐射来源。不同于传统粘滞供能盘模型（要求>200倍太阳质量的黑洞），作者提出辐射来自超爱丁顿风的色球层以及薄外盘的辐照再处理。模型拟合显示从1.4倍太阳质量中子星到100倍太阳质量黑洞都能解释观测，且可通过NIR-MIR自由-自由发射或重组线发射来区分吸积体类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AT2018cow作为典型的明亮快速蓝色光学暂现源（LFBOT），其晚期（爆炸后至少5年）UV/光学光变曲线呈现缓慢衰减的平台，SED表现为黑体谱（温度≥2e4 K，发射半径~40 Rsun）。传统解释认为来自致密天体周围的几何薄吸积盘，但粘滞供能盘模型需要异常大质量的黑洞（≥200倍太阳质量），且产生的X射线辐射与XMM-Newton观测约束存在矛盾。因此需要提出新的物理模型来解释该辐射成分。

Method: 提出超爱丁顿风模型：来自内盘的超爱丁顿风色球层产生UV/光学辐射，同时几何薄外盘对辐射进行辐照和再处理。利用t~4年的HST四波段测光数据进行拟合，并考虑不同吸积体质量（从中子星到黑洞）和盘组成。通过计算NIR-MIR波段（1-10微米）的自由-自由发射及重组线发射（HeII1640或Hα）来寻找区分吸积体类型的观测特征。

Result: 1）所有从1.4倍太阳质量中子星到约100倍太阳质量黑洞的吸积体都能同样好地拟合观测数据，因为风色球颜色温度对吸积体质量依赖性很弱；2）在NIR-MIR波段，不同质量吸积体的自由-自由发射差异可达一个量级，JWST可以区分；3）盘风的电离重组线发射（HeII1640或Hα）也可作为区分吸积体质量和盘组成的特征；4）推断的盘参数与微潮汐瓦解事件（伴星潮汐瓦解或合并）情景一致。

Conclusion: AT2018cow的晚期UV/光学辐射更可能来自超爱丁顿风加薄外盘辐照的联合机制，而非传统粘滞供能盘。该模型回避了传统模型所需超大质量黑洞和X射线矛盾的问题，但也揭示了吸积体质量的简并性。未来JWST的NIR-MIR观测和重组线观测有望打破这种简并，从而确定吸积体性质，并支持其源自微潮汐瓦解事件的情景。

Abstract: AT2018cow, the prototypical luminous fast blue optical transient (LFBOT), shows a slowly fading plateau in the UV/optical light lasting at least 5 years after the explosion. The plateau SED is blackbody-like with temperature $\gtrsim$ 2e4 K and an emission radius ~40 Rsun, which has been attributed to a geometrically thin accretion disk around a compact object. Viscously powered disk models, however, require an unusually massive black hole $\gtrsim$ 200 Msun (for viscosity parameter $α$ > 0.01). Moreover, accretion onto such a massive black hole produces X-ray emission that is likely in tension with the XMM-Newton constraint, unless the X-ray is highly anisotropic or absorbed. We propose instead that the UV/optical emission arises from the photosphere of a super-Eddington wind launched from the inner disk, together with irradiation and reprocessing by the geometrically thin outer disk. Fitting the model to four-band HST photometry at t ~ 4 yr, we find that all accretors from 1.4 Msun neutron star to ~100 Msun black hole produce similarly good fits. This degeneracy arises because the wind photospheric color temperature depends only weakly on the accretor mass. We also find that, at NIR-MIR wavelengths (1 to 10 um), wind free-free emission differs by an order of magnitude across the allowed mass range, providing a potential discriminant accessible to JWST. Another potential signature to differentiate the accretor mass and disk composition is the recombination line emission (HeII1640 or H$α$) from the disk wind. The inferred disk parameters from our model are consistent with a micro-tidal disruption event, either tidal disruption or merger with a companion star, in which an initially geometrically thick disk transitions to a thin phase within the first year and subsequently the thin outer disk evolves on the observed ~10 yr viscous timescale.

</details>


### [106] [High-Density Sound Speed and Post-Merger Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15588)
*John Stroud,David Radice,Sanjay Reddy*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文通过引入简化参数化方案，系统研究了冷中子星物质声速的密度依赖性对双中子星并合可观测信号的影响，发现并合后引力波频率与声速导数之间存在近似EOS无关的关系，为利用多信使观测约束高密度核物质状态方程提供了新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冷中子星物质中声速的密度依赖性（即高密度区状态方程）尚未被很好约束，而双中子星并合模拟虽然越来越多地包含微观物理细节，但中子星核心声速对可观测信号的直接影响尚未被系统探索。

Method: 1) 引入简化参数化方案，通过声速对重子化学势的导数来可控地改变超核密度下的声速，同时抑制微观物理细节；2) 使用WhiskyTHC代码进行全相对论性双中子星并合模拟；3) 从CoRe数据库分析使用更现实状态方程的引力波信号。

Result: 1) 发现声速斜率参数与并合后遗骸性质及引力波频率等关键结果之间存在相关性；2) 揭示出声速导数与峰值并合后引力波频率之间存在近似EOS无关的关系（但并非真正准普适），表明并合后引力波保留了关于最高密度区状态方程的可测量信息；3) 剩余EOS依赖性凸显了隔离高密度物理的难度。

Conclusion: 多信使可观测信号对中子星核心最高密度物质行为敏感；虽然发现的关系不能视为真正准普适，但为解释未来多信使观测提供了方向，并推动了发展针对性参数化框架的必要性。

Abstract: The density dependence of the speed of sound in cold neutron star matter remains poorly constrained and is central to determining the high-density equation of state (EOS). While binary neutron star (BNS) merger simulations increasingly incorporate detailed microphysics, the direct impact of the sound-speed in the neutron star core on observable signatures has not been systematically explored. We address this by introducing a simplified parametrization that suppresses microphysics while allowing controlled variation of the sound speed at supranuclear densities through its derivative with respect to the baryon chemical potential. Using the WhiskyTHC code, we perform a suite of fully relativistic BNS merger simulations and identify correlations between the sound-speed slope parameter and key merger outcomes, including remnant properties and post-merger GW frequencies. These results demonstrate that multimessenger observables are sensitive to the behavior of matter at the highest densities reached in neutron star cores. We further analyze gravitational-wave signals from the CoRe database of binary neutron star merger simulations employing more realistic equations of state. Our analysis reveals approximately EOS-independent relations between the derivative of the sound speed and the peak post-merger gravitational-wave frequency. Although these relations cannot be considered truly quasi-universal, they nonetheless indicate that post-merger gravitational waves retain measurable information about the EOS at he highest densities. At the same time, the remaining EOS dependence highlights the difficulty of isolating the underlying high-density physics and motivates the development of targeted parameterized frameworks for interpreting future multimessenger observations.

</details>


### [107] [Stable X-ray reverberation lags in the black hole X-ray binary Swift J1727.8-1613](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15729)
*Wei Yu,Sinan Allak,Zi-Xu Yang,Xiao Fan,Andrea Santangelo,Tian-hao Xie*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文利用NICER观测数据研究了黑洞X射线双星Swift J1727.8-1613在2023年爆发期间的X射线回波时延演化，发现软时延幅度从低硬态到硬中间态迅速增加，随后在硬中间态保持约10 ms的稳定值，表明吸积内流几何在硬中间态相对稳定，而时延幅度从低硬态到硬中间态的表观演化主要归因于硬时延的减弱而非光行时尺度的实际变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究黑洞X射线双星Swift J1727.8-1613在2023年爆发期间，X射线回波时延随光谱态的演化，以揭示不同光谱态下内吸积流几何结构的变化。

Method: 分析NICER在低硬态和硬中间态的观测数据，采用傅里叶技术计算时延，构建时延-频率和时延-能谱，并在回波信号主导且稳定的频率范围内提取软时延幅度，以最小化硬时延和相位包裹效应的污染。

Result: 软时延幅度从低硬态到硬中间态早期迅速增加，随后在硬中间态保持约10 ms的稳定值；在回波主导的频率范围内，时延对傅里叶频率依赖较弱；低频时延从硬时延主导转变为软时延主导，其幅度与高频测量值相当。

Conclusion: 回波时延在硬中间态变化很小，表明该态下内吸积几何相对稳定；从低硬态到硬中间态的表观时延演化主要由硬时延的减弱解释，而非内禀光行时尺度的显著变化。Swift J1727.8-1613提供了一个在宽频率范围内减少污染研究回波信号的案例，为理解黑洞X射线双星吸积几何演化提供了新见解。

Abstract: Aims. We investigate the evolution of X-ray reverberation lags in the black hole X-ray binary Swift J1727.8-1613 during its 2023 outburst, with the aim of probing the inner accretion flow geometry across spectral states. Methods. We analyzed NICER observations covering the low-hard state (LHS) and hard-intermediate state (HIMS). The time lags were computed using Fourier-based techniques, and we constructed lag-frequency and lag-energy spectra. To obtain a robust estimate of the soft lag amplitude, we focused on a frequency range in which the reverberation signal dominates and remains stable, thereby minimizing contamination from hard lags and phase-wrapping effects. Results. The soft lag amplitude increases rapidly from the LHS to the early HIMS and then stays near 10 ms throughout the HIMS. In the frequency range in which reverberation lags prevail, the lag shows little dependence on Fourier frequency. On the other hand, the observed low-frequency lags change from hard-lag dominated to soft-lag dominated, with amplitudes comparable to those measured at higher frequencies. Conclusions. These results suggest that the reverberation lag varies little during the HIMS, consistent with a relatively stable inner accretion geometry during this state. The apparent evolution of the lag amplitude from the LHS to the HIMS can be largely explained by the diminishing effect of hard lags and does not necessarily require significant changes in the intrinsic light-travel timescale. Swift J1727.8-1613 therefore provides a case in which the reverberation signal can be studied with reduced contamination over a broad frequency range, offering new insight into the evolution of the accretion geometry in black hole X-ray binaries.

</details>


### [108] [A cosmic-ray loaded nascent outflow driven by a massive star cluster](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15797)
*Marianne Lemoine-Goumard,Lucia Härer,Lars Mohrmann,Romain Bernet,Jim Hinton,Giada Peron,Brian Reville,Luigi Tibaldo,Thibault Vieu*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 在韦斯特隆德1号（Westerlund 1）大质量星团中发现了由宇宙射线驱动的初生外流，伽马射线发射与氢原子空腔吻合，表明相对论性电子正在从银盘逃逸，其能量密度比星际介质高一个数量级以上，暗示宇宙射线加载的外流可能是年轻大质量星团的普遍特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宇宙射线被认为能够驱动星暴星系的外流并深刻影响星系演化，但直接观测证据一直缺失；同时，大质量星团在宇宙射线加速中的作用日益受到关注。

Method: 利用伽马射线（GeV和TeV波段）观测数据，结合原子氢（HI）空腔结构分析，对韦斯特隆德1号星团周围的宇宙射线发射和空间分布进行研究。

Result: 探测到与大质量星团Westerlund 1相关的初生外流：GeV伽马射线发射与HI空腔位置重合，标志着相对论性电子正在从银盘逃逸；该发射在空间和能谱上与TeV辐射平滑连接；共加速的质子和原子核的能量密度比一般星际介质高至少一个数量级。

Conclusion: 宇宙射线加载的外流可能是年轻大质量星团的普遍特征，对宇宙射线向银河系晕的输运具有重要意义。

Abstract: Cosmic rays are widely held to drive outflows from star-forming galaxies and profoundly influence galaxy evolution. Direct evidence for cosmic-ray carrying outflows is however lacking. At the same time there is increasing awareness of the importance of massive star clusters in the acceleration of cosmic rays in galaxies. Here we report on the discovery of a nascent outflow driven by the massive star cluster Westerlund 1. Giga-electronvolt gamma-ray emission coincident with a cavity visible in atomic hydrogen traces the emergence of a population of relativistic electrons out of the Galactic Disc. The emission is offset from tera-electronvolt gamma-ray radiation surrounding the cluster, but connects to it smoothly spectrally and spatially. The implied energy density of co-accelerated protons and nuclei, assuming standard non-thermal electron/proton injection efficiencies, is at least an order of magnitude higher than that in the general interstellar medium. These particles therefore have the potential to dynamically influence the outflow. This discovery suggests that cosmic-ray loaded outflows may be a common feature of young massive star clusters, with implications for the transport of cosmic rays into the halo of the Galaxy.

</details>


### [109] [Non-thermal emission from the vicinity of the magnetar CXOU J171405.7-381031](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15955)
*Manoel F. Sousa,Rita. C. Anjos*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文对磁星CXOU J171405.7-381031及其周围区域进行多波段分析，通过轻子模型和轻子-强子模型解释其宽带能谱，并利用MCMC技术约束粒子分布。轻子-强子模型在高能段表现更优，但所需质子能量较高。磁星风云模型因形态不符被排除。模拟显示CTAO观测可检验强子发射机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁星作为具有超强磁场的中子星，是潜在的高能粒子加速源，但对其粒子加速机制和辐射过程的理解尚不充分。本研究旨在通过多波段观测和建模，揭示磁星CXOU J171405.7-381031及其周围环境（超新星遗迹CTB 37B）的粒子加速与辐射机制。

Method: 采用多波段数据分析，构建从射电到TeV能量的宽带能谱分布（SED）；分别建立纯轻子（leptonic）和轻子-强子（lepto-hadronic）两种辐射模型；利用马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（MCMC）方法约束粒子分布参数；模拟未来CTAO观测能力。

Result: 两种模型均能较好拟合伽马射线数据。纯轻子模型整体符合能谱形状，但略低估H.E.S.S.测量的最高能段流量；轻子-强子模型在~10 TeV以上提供更优描述，GeV波段由逆康普顿散射主导，最高能段由中性π介子衰变贡献。所需质子能量~10^51 erg，若与稠密介质相互作用可大幅降低。磁星风云模型虽能拟合能谱，但与观测形态严重不符。模拟表明CTAO 50小时曝光可约束质子截断能量。

Conclusion: 磁星CXOU J171405.7-381031周围的TeV辐射可能源自超新星遗迹中轻子或强子过程；强子成分在极高能段起关键作用，而磁星风云模型被排除。未来CTAO观测将能对强子发射模型做出决定性检验。

Abstract: Magnetars are neutron stars with ultra-strong magnetic fields ($B \sim 10^{14}$-$10^{15}$ G) and are promising candidates for high-energy particle acceleration. We present a multiwavelength analysis of the region surrounding CXOU J171405.7-381031, a magnetar associated with the supernova remnant (SNR) CTB 37B. The broadband spectral energy distribution spanning radio to TeV energies is modeled using leptonic and lepto-hadronic scenarios, with particle populations constrained using Markov Chain Monte Carlo techniques. Within an SNR framework, both scenarios provide acceptable descriptions of the gamma-ray data. The purely leptonic model reproduces the overall spectral shape but slightly underestimates the highest-energy flux measured by H.E.S.S., whereas a lepto-hadronic interpretation offers an improved description above $\sim 10$ TeV, with inverse-Compton scattering dominating the GeV emission and neutral-pion decay contributing at the highest energies. The required proton energy ($W_{\rm p} \gtrsim 10^{51}$ erg) can be substantially reduced if the remnant interacts with a dense ambient medium. A magnetar wind nebula scenario can reproduce the broadband spectrum but is strongly disfavored by the observed source morphology. Simulated Cherenkov Telescope Array Observatory (CTAO) observations indicate that exposures of $\sim 50$ h will constrain the proton cut-off energy, enabling a decisive test of hadronic emission in this region.

</details>


### [110] [Discovery of $γ$-Ray Pulsations from the Extreme-Spin-Down Millisecond Pulsar PSR J0435+3233](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16119)
*Mengqing Zhang,Shengbin Pei,Pengfei Zhang*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文利用费米望远镜约17年的观测数据，确认了PSR~J0435+3233（一颗具有极大自转减慢率和自转能损失率的毫秒脉冲星）的γ射线对应体，探测到其γ射线脉冲信号（置信度~6.8σ），发现其脉冲辐射集中在旋转相位φ~0.44-0.69，并测量到极低的γ射线光度（6.26×10^32 erg/s）和效率（~1.1×10^{-5}），为研究极端自转特性毫秒脉冲星的粒子加速、辐射束和观测几何提供了新探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新发现的毫秒脉冲星PSR~J0435+3233具有异常巨大的自转减慢率（\dot{P}=4.9×10^{-17} s s^{-1}）和高自转能损失率（\dot{E}=5.89×10^{37} erg s^{-1}），其极端特性促使作者利用长期费米观测数据研究其γ射线辐射性质。

Method: 利用Fermi-LAT约17年的观测数据，结合射电计时历表，通过γ射线脉冲相位分析（置信度~6.8σ）和相位分辨分析，识别γ射线对应体并测量其辐射特性。

Result: 确认4FGL J0435.5+3232为γ射线对应体；探测到γ射线脉冲信号（~6.8σ）；脉冲辐射集中在φ~0.44-0.69相位区间；γ射线光度L_γ=6.26×10^32 erg/s（距离1.2 kpc，各向同性假设）；γ射线效率η_γ~1.1×10^{-5}，极低。

Conclusion: 确认PSR~J0435+3233为γ射线毫秒脉冲星；其高自转能损失率与极低γ射线效率的显著组合，为研究毫秒脉冲星磁层中粒子加速、辐射束和观测几何提供了新的探针。

Abstract: Motivated by the recent discovery of PSR~J0435+3233, a millisecond pulsar with an exceptionally large period derivative of $\dot{P}=4.9\times10^{-17}\ {\rm s\,s^{-1}}$ and a high spin-down luminosity of $\dot{E}=5.89\times10^{37}\ {\rm erg\,s^{-1}}$, we analyze $\sim$17~yr of observations obtained with the \textit{Fermi} Large Area Telescope for the pulsar. We identify the cataloged source 4FGL~J0435.5+3232, located only $0.01^{\circ}$ from the radio timing position, as the $γ$-ray counterpart of PSR~J0435+3233. Using $γ$-ray events collected within the validity interval of the radio timing ephemeris, we detect its $γ$-ray pulsations at a $\sim6.8σ$ confidence level. In this interval, the phase-resolved analysis shows that the pulsed emission is concentrated predominantly within the rotational-phase of $φ\sim0.44$--$0.69$. We also derive its $γ$-ray luminosity of $L_γ=6.26\times10^{32}\ {\rm erg\,s^{-1}}$, assuming a distance of 1.2~kpc and isotropic emission. This luminosity corresponds to an apparent $γ$-ray efficiency of $η_γ\sim1.1\times10^{-5}$, revealing an exceptionally low $γ$-ray output despite the pulsar's young-pulsar-like rotational-energy budget. Our detection establishes PSR~J0435+3233 as a $γ$-ray MSP, and the striking combination of its high spin-down power and low apparent $γ$-ray efficiency provides a new probe of particle acceleration, radiation beaming, and viewing geometry in the magnetospheres of millisecond pulsars with extreme rotational properties.

</details>


### [111] [Impact of eccentricity and higher-modes on neutron star-black hole parameter estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16135)
*Snehal Tibrewal,Aasim Jan,Aaron Zimmerman,Hsin-Yu Chen*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了中子星-黑洞双星系统中偏心率的参数估计影响，发现高偏心率（e=0.25）可将偏心率测量精度提升至$4\times10^{-4}$，并将有效自旋和质量比的约束分别改善约13倍和20倍，但对光度距离和天空定位等外在参数无显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中子星-黑洞双星的引力波信号包含偏心特征，可改善参数估计并提供双星形成机制线索。然而偏心率主要在旋近阶段留下印记，在黑洞双星中通常难以约束，而中子星-黑洞系统信号频带内持续时间更长，有望实现更精确的偏心率测量。

Method: 使用最先进的偏心波形模型SEOBNRv5EHM进行注入模拟，对GW200105_162426这类系统开展系统性的参数估计研究，分析不同偏心率对参数推断的影响。

Result: 偏心率测量精度随偏心率增大而提升，$e=0.25$时$1σ$不确定度低至$4\times10^{-4}$；有效自旋和质量比的约束相比准圆形情况分别改善约13倍和20倍；光度距离和天空定位等外在参数无明显改善。

Conclusion: 高偏心率显著提升中子星-黑洞双星系统的内在参数约束精度，但对外在参数改善有限，表明偏心率在这方面提供的信息与高阶模信息存在简并或可忽略。

Abstract: Detections of gravitational waves from neutron star-black hole systems provide avenues for studying extreme matter, constraining binary formation channels, and testing the nature of compact objects in strong gravity. Eccentric signatures in the signal further enhance this potential by improving parameter estimation and offering clues about binary formation. Because eccentricity is primarily imprinted during the inspiral phase, it is often weakly constrained or missed entirely in binary black hole observations; in contrast, neutron star-black hole systems produce longer in-band signals, enabling more precise measurements of eccentricity and leaving a distinct imprint on parameter inference. In this work, we present a systematic parameter-estimation study exploring the impact of eccentricity on inference using injections simulated with the state-of-the-art eccentric waveform model SEOBNRv5EHM. We find that for systems like GW200105_162426, the measurement precision of eccentricity and correlated parameters improves as eccentricity increases, yielding tighter constraints at larger eccentricities. For the highest eccentricity considered in this study, $e=0.25$, we recover eccentricity with $1σ$ uncertainty as low as $4\times10^{-4}$. In addition, the constraints on effective spin $χ_\mathrm{eff}$ and mass ratio $q$ improve relative to the quasi-circular case by factors of $\sim13$ and $\sim20$, respectively. On the other hand, we find no significant improvement in extrinsic parameters such as luminosity distance and sky localization, suggesting that for systems like GW200105_162426, the additional information provided by eccentricity in this sector is either negligible or degenerate with the information provided by higher-order modes.

</details>


### [112] [Eccentricity as a Magnifying Glass: Precision Population Inference Enabled by Eccentric Neutron Star--Black Hole Mergers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16136)
*Alberto Salvarese,Hsin-Yu Chen,Aaron Zimmerman,Snehal Tibrewal*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了偏心轨道如何增强对中子星-黑洞（NSBH）并合系统参数的测量精度，从而改善对其形成通道的推断能力，发现偏心系统能够更清晰地测量有效自旋参数、中子星质量分布和黑洞质量分布，并在LVK第五次观测运行结束时能够检验动力学形成通道的偏心分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 致密双星系统的形成历史是天体物理学中尚未解决的关键问题之一。理论通常认为孤立双星演化产生的NSBH系统轨道近乎圆形，但GW200105事件的可测量偏心性表明可能存在其他形成通道，因此需要探究偏心增强的参数测量对NSBH形成通道推断的影响。

Method: 通过比较偏心并合与圆并合对NSBH系统本征参数（质量、自旋）的测量精度差异，分析偏心增强测量如何影响有效自旋参数χ_eff的识别、中子星质量分布约束以及金属丰度依赖的黑洞质量分布恢复，并评估在LVK O5阶段检验动力学形成通道偏心分布的能力。

Result: 偏心系统能更清晰测量χ_eff，使自旋-轨道未对准事件在O5阶段每约2.5次偏心NSBH探测即可识别一次；改进的中子星质量测量能更好约束其质量分布及上下限；偏心增强测量改善了金属丰度依赖的黑洞质量分布的恢复；动力学形成通道的种群级偏心分布可在O5结束时得到检验。

Conclusion: 偏心NSBH并合事件通过增强参数测量精度，能够显著提升对NSBH形成通道的区分能力，为检验不同形成机制提供了新的观测途径。

Abstract: The formation history of compact binary systems remains one of the key open questions in astrophysics. Theoretical studies generally favor isolated binary evolution for neutron star-black hole (NSBH) systems, which tends to produce nearly circular orbits. However, recent analyses of the gravitational-wave event GW200105 indicate that its source has measurable eccentricity, suggesting that alternative formation channels may also contribute. It has been shown that the intrinsic parameters of eccentric NSBH mergers, such as the component masses and spins, are much better measured than circular mergers with LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA (LVK) observatories. We explore how such eccentricity-enhanced parameter measurements can affect the inference of NSBH formation channels. We find that sharper measurements of the effective spin parameter $χ_{\rm eff}$ increase the fraction of systems for which negative values can be confidently identified, allowing for the clear measurement of a spin-orbit misaligned event every $\sim 2.5$ eccentric NSBH detections for an isotropically distributed population in the fifth LVK observing run (O5). Improved NS mass measurements provide better constraining power for NS mass distributions, potentially revealing structure and tightening the bounds that can be drawn on their upper and lower masses. Similarly, the recovery of a metallicity-dependent BH mass distribution is improved by eccentricity-enhanced measurements. Finally, we show that the proposed population-level eccentricity distribution for dynamical-formation channels can be tested by the end of O5.

</details>


### [113] [X-ray polarization of Z-type neutron star low-mass X-ray binaries -- II. Spectropolarimetric analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16140)
*Andrea Gnarini,Francesco Ursini,Giorgio Matt,Stefano Bianchi,Fiamma Capitanio,Massimo Cocchi,Sergio Fabiani,Ruben Farinelli,Philip Kaaret,Lorenzo Marra,Antonella Tarana*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文利用IXPE、NICER和NuSTAR数据，对Z型中子星低质量X射线双星在不同分支上的能谱偏振特性进行了详细分析。结果表明，康普顿化过程是X射线发射和偏振的主要贡献者：从水平分支到正常分支，2-8 keV波段的偏振度从约6%降至3-4%，而耀发分支由于曝光时间短，偏振度约束较弱。盘偏振通常低于3%，但高于纯电子散射主导的平面平行大气的理论预测。此外，盘的偏振角与康普顿化成分的偏振角显著不垂直。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前对Z型中子星低质量X射线双星（NS-LMXBs）的X射线偏振研究较为有限，特别是对其在不同光谱分支上的偏振特性缺乏详细的能谱偏振分析。本文旨在填补这一空白，通过联合IXPE、NICER和NuSTAR数据，对每个源在每个分支上进行详细的能谱偏振分析，以揭示偏振特征与光谱状态之间的联系。

Method: 对多个Z型NS-LMXBs源，利用IXPE、NICER和NuSTAR的联合观测数据，在每个分支（水平分支HB、正常分支NB、耀发分支FB）上进行详细的能谱偏振分析。使用热吸积盘辐射加较硬的康普顿化成分的连续谱模型，并包含相对论展宽的铁线等反射特征。通过模型拟合获得各成分的偏振度和偏振角。

Result: 1）所有源的连续X射线发射可由热吸积盘辐射加康普顿化成分很好地描述，除GX 5-1外均观测到反射特征（特别是相对论展宽的铁线）；2）康普顿化是X射线发射和偏振的主要贡献者：从HB到NB，2-8 keV的PD从约6%降至3-4%，FB上PD约束较弱；3）盘偏振通常低于3%，但高于纯电子散射主导平面平行大气模型的预测；4）盘的偏振角与康普顿化成分的偏振角显著不垂直；5）未发现偏振信号与倾角或反射光子贡献之间的相关性。

Conclusion: Z型NS-LMXBs在水平分支上表现出迄今观测到的NS-LMXBs中最高的X射线偏振度（约6%）。观测到的偏振度显著高于典型的扩散层或边界层模型的理论预期，盘的偏振虽低但也高于纯电子散射模型的预测，表明现有理论模型需要修订。盘与康普顿化成分的偏振角显著不对齐，暗示两者几何结构不同。

Abstract: IXPE has provided for the first time detailed energy- and time-resolved X-ray polarimetry of Z-type neutron star low-mass X-ray binaries (NS-LMXBs) as they move along their color-color diagrams (CCDs). These sources can reach the highest polarization observed for NS-LXMBs in the 2-8 keV range when they move along the horizontal branch. In a previous paper, we characterized the spectral state of a sample of Z-sources using the CCD and estimated the polarization with model-independent analysis. Here, we present detailed spectropolarimetric analysis for each source on each branch using data from IXPE, NICER, and NuSTAR. The continuum X-ray emission of all the sources is well described with a combination of thermal accretion disk emission plus a harder Comptonized component. In addition, reflection features, in particular the relativistically broadened Fe line, are observed for our sources, except GX 5-1. For most of the sources and branches, the main contribution to the X-ray emission and polarization is due to Comptonization: moving from the horizontal branch (HB) to the normal branch (NB), the polarization degree (PD) in the 2-8 keV band varies from about 6% to 3-4%, while the PD is loosely constrained in the flaring branch (FB), due to the shorter exposures. These PD values are significantly higher than theoretical expectations for typical spreading or boundary layer configurations. The polarization of the disk is generally lower (below 3%) but still higher than predictions for an electron scattering-dominated, plane-parallel atmosphere above the disk observed at the corresponding inclination. Moreover, the polarization angle (PA) of the disk seems to be significantly misaligned and not perpendicular to that of Comptonization. We find no correlation between the polarization signal and the inclination, nor with the contribution of reflected photons throughout the Z-track.

</details>

### [114] [Metric tensors and two-forms in information geometry from the GNS construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15800)
*M. Castrillón López,F. M. Ciaglia,L. González-Bravo,A. Ibort*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于GNS表示的几何张量构造方法，适用于C*-代数上的光滑参数统计模型。该方法无需依赖状态流形的光滑结构，而是通过构建非局部平凡的Hilbert纤维丛，在状态空间上定义出Fisher-Rao度量（交换主导情形）、Fubini-Study几何（纯态）和SLD度量（忠实量子态）。此外，文中导出了一个二形式Ω，它与SLD表示子的期望对易子（即平均Uhlmann曲率）成正比，并通过忠实量子比特和位移热态示例证明了Ω不一定闭合。对于纤维丛正则模型，其实对偶GNS丛上的纤维辛形式可（依赖于联络地）闭延拓到全空间，而Ω在参数流形上的闭合性由典范实对偶GNS提升的协变外导数控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: C*-代数上的态空间一般不是光滑流形，因此无法直接通过从环境态流形拉回张量来定义几何结构。已有方法（如基于SLD度量的量子信息几何）多局限于有限维或特定量子态族，缺乏统一且严格的泛函分析框架。本文旨在发展一种不依赖于态流形光滑性的、基于GNS表示的几何张量构造方法，为量子统计模型提供统一的微分几何工具。

Method: 1) 将GNS Hilbert空间及其对偶组织成状态空间上的非局部平凡Hilbert纤维丛；2) 对满足相容性条件（期望值的导数可表示为实化GNS纤维上的连续泛函）的模型，为每个切向量定义典范对偶GNS表示子；3) 沿典范提升拉回对偶GNS Hermitian内积，在复化切丛上定义Hermitian张量K，其实部和虚部分别给出弱Riemann度量G和2-形式Ω；4) 在有限维参数流形上，度量自然成为强Riemann度量。

Result: 1) 该构造在交换主导情形恢复Fisher-Rao度量；2) 对纯态恢复Fubini-Study几何（含归一化和符号约定）；3) 对忠实量子态恢复SLD度量；4) 在有限维忠实模型中，Ω正比于SLD表示子的期望对易子（即平均Uhlmann曲率）；5) 通过忠实量子比特和位移热态示例，证明Ω一般不是闭的；6) 对纤维丛正则模型，对偶GNS丛上的纤维辛形式允许联络依赖的闭延拓，Ω的闭合性由典范实对偶GNS提升的协变外导数控制。

Conclusion: 本文建立的GNS-based几何框架为C*-代数上参数统计模型提供了统一的微分几何语言，统一了经典Fisher-Rao度量、纯态Fubini-Study度量和量子SLD度量，并揭示了二形式Ω与Uhlmann曲率的关系。该框架不依赖于态流形的光滑性，适用于更广泛的量子统计模型。关于Ω非闭合性和闭延拓条件的讨论，为后续研究量子统计流形上的辛几何与信息几何奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: We develop a GNS-based construction of geometric tensors on smooth parametric statistical models over $C^*$-algebras. Since the state space of a $C^*$-algebra is generally not a smooth manifold, the construction does not rely on pulling back tensors from an ambient state manifold. Instead, the GNS Hilbert spaces and their duals are organized into non-locally-trivial Hilbert fibrations over the state space. For models satisfying a compatibility condition expressing derivatives of expectation values as continuous functionals on the realified GNS fibers, each tangent vector admits a canonical dual GNS representative. Pulling back the dual GNS Hermitian product along the corresponding canonical lift produces a Hermitian tensor $K$ on the complexified tangent bundle of the model, whose real and imaginary parts define, under suitable regularity assumptions, a smooth weak Riemannian metric tensor $G$ and a smooth two-form $Ω$. In finite-dimensional parameter manifolds the metric is, of course, strong.
  The construction recovers the Fisher--Rao metric in the commutative dominated case, the Fubini--Study geometry for pure states up to the normalization and sign convention imposed by the dual GNS pairing, and the SLD metric for faithful quantum states. In finite-dimensional faithful models, the two-form $Ω$ is proportional, up to convention, to the expected commutator of the SLD representatives, equivalently to the mean Uhlmann curvature. We show through faithful qubits and displaced thermal states that $Ω$ need not be closed. For bundle-regular models, the associated fiberwise symplectic form on the real dual GNS bundle admits connection-dependent closed extensions to the total space, while closedness of $Ω$ on the parameter manifold is controlled by the covariant exterior derivative of the canonical real dual GNS lift.

</details>


### [115] [Variational modeling adapted to the medium with gradient properties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16005)
*Azdine Nait-Ali,Sami Ben Elhaj Salah*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对非均质层状介质的数值均匀化方法，通过两步均匀化方案（先分解为2D层并利用Hashin-Shtrikman界限估计，再用变分求和法重建3D行为）来捕捉材料属性梯度，并在具有孔隙率梯度的薄板上验证了该方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统尺度过渡方法无法充分捕捉某些材料的梯度特性，因此需要构建一种考虑材料属性梯度的均匀化模型。

Method: 采用两步均匀化方案：首先将3D模型分解为多个2D非均质层，利用微机械模型（如Hashin-Shtrikman界限）估计各层行为；然后使用变分求和法重建3D环境行为。

Result: 该方法被应用于具有孔隙率梯度的薄板均匀化，验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: 提出的两步均匀化方案能够有效处理具有梯度特性的非均质层状介质，为梯度材料的宏观性能预测提供了可行方法。

Abstract: This study aims to develop a numerical homogenization method that can be applied to a heterogeneous stratified medium. Traditional scale transition methods are inadequate in capturing the essential gradient properties of some materials. Therefore, the focus of this work is to construct a homogenized model that considers the material property gradient. To achieve this, a two-step homogenization scheme is proposed. Firstly, the 3D model is decomposed into multiple 2D heterogeneous layers, and the behavior of each layer is estimated using a micro-mechanical model such as the Hashin-Shtrikman bounds. Secondly, a variational sum method is used to rebuild the behavior of the 3D environment. Finally, the method is applied to homogenize a thin plate with a porosity gradient.

</details>


### [116] [Inertial motion of incompressible continua](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16043)
*Francesca Berlinghieri,Giulio G. Giusteri*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文在几何与变分框架下研究了不可压缩连续体的惯性运动，将经典的Arnold-Ebin-Marsden理论从固定域的体积保持微分同胚群扩展至具有可变像的形变位形空间。研究表明保定向形变构成Hilbert流形，体积保持形变构成子流形，其切向量在拉格朗日到欧拉图像对应下映射为无散向量场。动能同时导出拉格朗日作用量和度量，其测地线由惯性运动精确刻画。获得了测地流方程局部时间存在性的普适结果，并给出了显式例子：在可压缩情形下，相同初始数据的测地线可能在有限时间内离开可容许形变流形，而相应的不可压缩测地线则全局存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典的Arnold-Ebin-Marsden理论从固定域的体积保持微分同胚群扩展至具有可变像的形变位形空间，建立可压缩与不可压缩连续体惯性运动的几何框架。

Method: 采用几何与变分方法，证明保定向形变构成Hilbert流形，体积保持形变构成子流形，通过拉格朗日-欧拉对应建立切向量与无散向量场的映射，由动能导出拉格朗日作用量和度量。

Result: 获得了测地流方程局部时间存在性的普适结果。显式例子表明可压缩情形下测地线可能在有限时间内离开可容许形变流形，而不可压缩情形下全局存在。

Conclusion: 为可压缩与不可压缩连续体的惯性运动建立了统一的几何与变分框架，揭示了不可压缩性在保证解全局存在性中的关键作用。

Abstract: We study the inertial motion of incompressible continua within a geometric and variational framework, extending the classical Arnold-Ebin-Marsden theory from the group of volume-preserving diffeomorphisms of a fixed domain to configuration spaces of deformations with variable image. In the latter case, the lack of a group structure requires proving some results that are instead immediate in the classical setting. We show that the orientation-preserving deformations with suitable regularity constitute a Hilbert manifold and that volume-preserving deformations form a submanifold with tangent vectors that are mapped onto divergence-free vector fields by the Lagrangian-to-Eulerian-picture correspondence. In so doing, we also present the geometric structure corresponding to compressible continua. The kinetic energy of the continuum gives rise to both a Lagrangian action, from which the equations of inertial motion are deduced, and a metric, with geodesics that are identified precisely by inertial motions. While the inertial motion can coincide with a physical one only for incompressible perfect fluids, it can be used to provide a natural parametrization of the configuration manifold for generic continua. We obtain a general result of local-in-time existence of solutions for the geodesic flow equation and we present explicit examples showing that, for the same initial data, the geodesic followed by the continuum in the compressible case can leave the manifold of admissible deformations in finite time, while the corresponding incompressible geodesic exists for all times.

</details>

### [117] [Aggregate combat modeling using high-resolution simulation: "the "meeting engagement"scenario as a case study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15312)
*Sumanta K. Das,Pankaj Sati,Rajiv Gupta*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用高分辨率仿真和马尔可夫兰彻斯特过程开发聚合战斗模型的方法，通过离散事件仿真输出估计聚合兰彻斯特模型的损耗率，并比较了多种统计估计方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前聚合战斗模型的开发缺乏系统性的方法论，特别是缺乏利用高分辨率仿真数据来校准聚合模型参数的可靠途径，导致模型精度和可信度不足。

Method: 首先构建遭遇战战术场景的高分辨率离散事件仿真模型，详细呈现其中的数学模型；然后在理论上讨论马尔可夫兰彻斯特过程；接着利用仿真输出数据估计聚合兰彻斯特模型的损耗率；最后比较多种适用于此类估计的统计方法。

Result: 成功展示了从高分辨率仿真到聚合模型参数估计的完整流程，并对不同统计估计方法进行了比较分析。

Conclusion: 验证了所提方法论的有效性，为聚合战斗模型的开发提供了一种基于高分辨率仿真数据驱动的实用途径。

Abstract: This paper illustrates a methodology for developing aggregated combat models using high-resolution simulation and the Markovian Lanchester process. The details of the mathematical models involved in a high-resolution simulation process of a Meeting Engagement tactical scenario are presented, along with a theoretical discussion on the Markovian Lanchester model. The output from the discrete event simulation model is used to estimate the attrition rates for an aggregated Lanchester model. A comparative study of various statistical estimation methods suitable for such estimation is also presented.

</details>


### [118] [Proactive Inpatient Bed Requests for Emergency Department Admissions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15432)
*QIan Cheng,Nilay Tanik Argon,Aniruddhan Ganesaraman,Serhan Ziya*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用患者信息和床位可用性预测来主动提前请求住院床位的框架，以减少急诊科滞留时间和住院时长。研究将问题建模为马尔可夫决策过程，提出了三种数据驱动策略（近似动态规划、强化学习、新闻供应商启发式），模拟实验显示可减少30-70%的滞留时间和6-15%的总住院时长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 急诊科滞留（boarding）是导致急诊拥挤和患者预后不良的主要原因，现有方法往往在入院决定最终确定后才请求床位，缺乏主动性和前瞻性。

Method: 将问题建模为马尔可夫决策过程（MDP），利用每个患者的入院概率和处置时间预测来聚合指导早期床位请求，提出三种数据驱动策略：近似动态规划、强化学习和新闻供应商启发式方法，并使用基于大型急诊科数据的仿真模型进行评估。

Result: 主动聚合床位请求可减少入院患者平均滞留时间30-70%，减少所有急诊患者平均住院时间6-15%，同时仅造成适度的空闲床位时间。新闻供应商启发式在急诊绩效与床位空闲时间间实现最佳权衡，强化学习启发式在需要下游医院流程稳定性时产生更平滑的请求模式。

Conclusion: 急诊科可以利用预测工具进行主动床位请求决策，改善急诊运营并帮助管理者平衡减少急诊延误与床位空闲时间。简单启发式方法与复杂强化学习方法各有优势，应根据绩效指标和实施约束选择合适策略。

Abstract: Emergency department (ED) boarding occurs when admitted patients remain in the ED while awaiting inpatient beds. Boarding is a major driver of ED crowding and has been associated with poor patient outcomes. We propose a framework to help EDs reduce boarding time and length of stay by using information about current patients and bed availability to proactively request inpatient beds before admission decisions are finalized.
  We formulate the problem as a Markov decision process in which predictions of each patient's admission probability and time to disposition are aggregated to guide early inpatient bed requests. This formulation leads to three data-driven policies based on approximate dynamic programming, reinforcement learning, and a newsvendor-type approach. Using a simulation model based on data from a large ED, we evaluate these policies across a wide range of settings. The simulation study shows that proactive aggregate bed requests can reduce average boarding times for admitted patients by 30-70\% and average length of stay for all ED patients by 6-15\%, while creating only modest idle time for prepared inpatient beds. The newsvendor heuristic provides the most attractive tradeoff between ED performance and inpatient bed idle time, whereas the reinforcement learning heuristic produces smoother bed-request patterns when stability in downstream hospital processes is especially important.
  Our work shows how EDs can use prediction tools to make proactive bed-request decisions that improve ED operations while helping managers balance reductions in ED delays against inpatient bed idle time. Our findings also illustrate the value of evaluating both simple myopic heuristics and more sophisticated reinforcement learning-based approaches, since each can offer distinct advantages depending on the performance measures and implementation constraints most important to managers.

</details>


### [119] [LLM Latent Edge Measurement: Point-in-Time Economic Graphs for Quantitative Investing from Corporate Disclosures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15640)
*Fan Yang,Lin Zhang*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于LLM的管道，从上市公司公开披露（10-K/10-Q）中提取加权、有向、时点的企业网络邻接矩阵。对纳斯达克100中42家公司的测试显示，该网络包含149条有向边，经对抗审计确认88%的边可靠，且能捕获GICS分类无法体现的跨部门经济关系（如核电购电协议、GPU云依赖等）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准行业分类系统（如GICS）将每家公司归入单一部门，但经济冲击传播所依赖的关系（如供应商协议、客户集中度、知识产权许可、云服务依赖、购电合同等）常常跨越部门边界，且仅在非结构化文本中披露。现有方法无法系统性地从文本中构建企业间关系网络。

Method: 将企业级邻接矩阵构建形式化为测量问题，提出基于LLM的管道：从10-K/10-Q文件中提取加权、有向、时点的企业网络。采用多智能体融合、逆文档频率过滤和相对阈值化等技术提升网络质量。对纳斯达克100中42家公司的最近期文件进行应用测试。

Result: 生成包含149条有向边的网络；对抗审计确认88%的抽样边（权重≥0.1）可靠，若纳入经济合理但文档记载薄弱的关系则达100%；被反驳的边集中在最低权重部分；网络内部门连接性是部门间的1.9倍；成功捕获了核电购电协议、GPU云依赖等跨部门关系。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的管道能从非结构化披露中有效构建企业关系网络，既与GICS分类一致（部门内连接性更高），又能发现标准分类无法表达的跨部门经济关系。消融实验验证了多智能体融合、IDF过滤和相对阈值化对网络质量均有可测量的贡献。

Abstract: Standard industry classification systems such as GICS assign each firm to a single sector, but the economic relationships through which shocks propagate, such as supplier agreements, customer concentration, intellectual property licensing, cloud service dependencies, and power purchase contracts frequently cross sector boundaries and are often disclosed only in unstructured text. We formulate the construction of a firm-level adjacency matrix as a measurement problem and propose an LLM based pipeline that extracts a weighted, directed, point in time corporate network from public disclosures.
  Applied to the most recent 10 K and 10 K filings of 42 Nasdaq 100 constituents, the proposed pipeline produces a network containing 149 directed edges. An adversarial audit confirms 88% of sampled edges with weights of at least 0.1, increasing to 100% when economically plausible but weakly documented relationships are included. Refuted edges are concentrated entirely in the lowest-weight portion of the network. The resulting network is consistent with GICS where sector classifications are informative, exhibiting a 1.9-fold increase in within-sector connectivity, while also recovering economically meaningful cross-sector relationships that standard classifications cannot represent. Examples include nuclear power-purchase agreements connecting utilities with hyper scale technology firms and GPU-cloud dependencies within the emerging AI infrastructure ecosystem. Ablation studies further demonstrate that multi-agent fusion, inverse-document-frequency filtering, and relative thresholding each make measurable contributions to network quality.

</details>

### [120] [Structural Compression for Phylogenetic Inference under Alignment Instability and Indel-Rich Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15308)
*Zhuoxin Zhang,Jieyu Wang,Fengyao Zhai,Jing Wang,Xiaojun Hu,Dayou Zhang,Lu Fan,Yu Liu*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本文提出Ladderpath，一种基于算法信息论的无比对系统发育推断方法，通过将序列分解为可重用的层级结构单元（ladderons）来计算距离，在比对不稳定或结构变异主导的情况下表现出优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于比对和替代模型的系统发育推断在处理比对不稳定、以及受插入、缺失、重复和其他结构变化主导的进化场景时可靠性下降，亟需一种能捕捉高阶序列结构信息的无比对方法。

Method: 受算法信息论启发，Ladderpath将序列分解为可衍生的可重用单元（ladderons），以层级结构组织，并据此计算成对距离，实现无比对系统发育推断。

Result: 在噬菌体T7已知谱系、cpSSR重复标记、细胞色素c蛋白数据集上，Ladderpath重建的拓扑与已知实验史或基于比对的方法一致；在块易位和插入缺失主导的模拟中保持稳定；在香蕉线粒体和质体基因组上可扩展至全基因组尺度并捕获预期的细胞器历史对比。

Conclusion: Ladderpath作为一种无比对、结构信息驱动的系统发育推断方法，在高阶序列结构携带系统发育信号时，可补充传统分析流程。

Abstract: Phylogenetic inference traditionally relies on aligned characters under substitution models, but this framework becomes less reliable when alignments are unstable or when evolution is dominated by insertions, deletions, repeats, and other structural changes. We adapt Ladderpath as an alignment-free distance approach for phylogenetic inference. Motivated by algorithmic information theory, Ladderpath decomposes sequences into derived, reusable units (``ladderons'', rather than fixed-length $k$-mers) organized hierarchically, from which pairwise distances are computed. The premise is that shared derived sequence structure, including repeated or reused segments that are poorly represented by column-wise substitutions, can retain phylogenetic information. The bacteriophage T7 known lineage, the cpSSR repeat-rich marker, and a cytochrome~$c$ protein dataset confirm that Ladderpath recovers topologies consistent with the known experimental history or with established alignment-based methods. Its advantage emerges under stress: in block-translocation and indel-dominated simulations Ladderpath remains stable while alignment-dependent pipelines deteriorate; on banana mitochondrial and plastome genomes it scales to genome length and captures the expected contrast between organellar histories, all from unaligned input. These results support Ladderpath as an alignment-free, structurally informed method that could complement standard pipelines in cases where higher-order sequence structure carries phylogenetic signal.

</details>


### [121] [A New Method to Predict the Effect of an Intervention in the Host Population to Reduce the Magnitude of an Outbreak of a Vector-Borne Infection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15445)
*Francisco Antônio Bezerra Coutinho,Marcos Amaku,Esper Georges Kallas,Eduardo Massad*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种不依赖稳态假设、与疫情强度无关的新型模型，用于评估媒介传播感染（如登革热）干预措施的效果。该模型以官方报告的年龄分层病例数为输入，利用同一媒介传播的感染病例年龄分布比例与传播强度和地理区域无关的实证发现（至少巴西地区如此），模拟假设的干预措施（如登革热疫苗），从而确定最佳接种策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型多依赖稳态假设来评估干预效果，但登革热等媒介传播疾病每年爆发强度不同，需要一种能适应不同疫情强度、不依赖稳态假设的评估方法。

Method: 提出确定性模型，以官方报告的年龄分层病例数为输入，基于同一媒介传播的感染病例年龄分布比例与传播强度和地理区域无关的实证发现（巴西地区），计算干预措施的效果。

Result: 通过模拟登革热疫苗干预，确定了最佳疫苗接种策略。

Conclusion: 该模型能够独立于疫情强度评估干预效果，为登革热等媒介传播疾病的疫苗接种策略优化提供了理论依据。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a new model to estimate the impact of an intervention on human hosts of a vector-borne infection, such as dengue, which occurs in yearly outbreaks of different magnitudes. The model applies to these outbreaks and, in fact, is independent of their intensity, that is, it does not require the steady-state assumption. The model takes as input the officially reported age-dependent number of cases of a vector-borne infection. It is deterministic and does not account for stochasticity. Our objective is to estimate the impact of the intervention (the efficacy), and we rely on the observed fact that the age distribution of the proportion of cases of the infections transmitted by the same vector is independent of both the intensity of transmission and the geographic area studied, at least for Brazilian regions. This finding is highlighted in the main text and forms the basis of our calculations. A hypothetical intervention is simulated using a dengue vaccine, which allows the determination of the optimal strategy for a vaccination campaign.

</details>


### [122] [Classes of phylogenetic networks that are robust to root placement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15625)
*Thomas Britz,Michael Hendriksen,Kaitlyn O'Reilly*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本文研究无根系统发育网络的鲁棒定向性，即任意选择根都能得到有效有根网络的性质，并给出了严格的结构条件（无汇分量），同时探讨了不同网络类别的鲁棒分类特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准系统发育重建方法常得到无根网络，需要后续生根以推断演化历史，但生根后网络所属结构类别不确定。本文旨在系统研究无根网络在任意生根选择下是否都能产生有效的有根系统发育网络，并刻画这类网络的结构特征。

Method: 通过图论分析，定义"鲁棒定向性"（robustly orientable）和"鲁棒类 $\mathcal C$"（robustly class $\mathcal C$）等概念，利用无根网络中的汇分量（sink components）等结构特征进行充要条件刻画，并针对level-$k$网络、树基网络、树子网络、无栈网络和正规网络等具体类别进行分类分析。

Result: (1) 无根网络是鲁棒定向的当且仅当它不含汇分量；(2) 若无根网络的level不超过2，或是树基网络，则它是鲁棒定向的；(3) 无根网络是鲁棒树子（robustly tree-child）或鲁棒无栈（robustly stack-free）的当且仅当它的level不超过1；(4) 系统发育网络是鲁棒正规（robustly normal）的当且仅当它是一棵系统发育树。

Conclusion: 本文为无根系统发育网络的可生根性提供了严格的理论刻画，明确了哪些无根网络结构能保证任意生根都产生有效的有根网络，为实际系统发育重建中网络的后处理步骤提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Standard phylogenetic reconstruction techniques often yield unrooted phylogenetic networks; these are subsequently rooted to infer evolutionary history. A common problem in this process is to determine the structural classes to which the resulting network will belong. In this paper, we investigate unrooted networks in which the choice of any root results in a valid rooted phylogenetic network, a property we define as {\em robustly orientable}. We then establish a strict structural condition for this class, specifically, that an unrooted network is robustly orientable if and only if it contains no sink components. We also show that if an unrooted network is level-$2$ or less, or if it is tree-based, then it is robustly orientable. Furthermore, we define an unrooted network to be {\em robustly class $\mathcal C$} if the choice of any root results in a network belonging to class $\mathcal C$. We demonstrate that an unrooted network is robustly tree-child or robustly stack-free if and only if it is level-$1$ or less. Finally, we show that a phylogenetic network is robustly normal if and only if it is a phylogenetic tree.

</details>


### [123] [Evolutionary path dependence of semantic complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15836)
*Elliot M. Butterworth,Tim Rogers,Matthew A. Wills*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本文提出将生物复杂度区分为句法复杂度（结构信息量）和语义复杂度（影响适应度的结构特征），通过一个分节功能约束的数学建模，发现两种进化模式：驱动模式（语义与句法复杂度同步上升）和熵模式（句法复杂度在中性进化下漂移），历史偶然性决定了路径依赖的进化结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统生物复杂度研究仅关注结构层面的句法特征（如身体部位数量与分化程度），缺乏对功能意义的考量，无法全面解释复杂度作为进化驱动力或结果的机制。作者因此提出语义复杂度的概念，旨在区分对适应度有实际影响的结构特征与单纯的结构信息量。

Method: 构建一个关于分节（tagmosis）的简单数学模型，引入功能约束、对称性破缺和特化机制，在具有多个最优解的适应度景观中模拟进化过程，追踪句法复杂度与语义复杂度的协同演化动态。

Result: 句法复杂度的演化具有路径依赖性：存在驱动模式（语义与句法复杂度同步上升）和熵模式（句法复杂度在近中性进化下漂移）。早期特化的历史偶然性与多最优适应度景观决定了两模式的持续时间。未能直接达到最高适应度状态的路径会延长驱动模式，最终通过更高的句法复杂度达到相近的适应度水平。

Conclusion: 语义复杂度与句法复杂度的区分有助于更精确地理解生物复杂度的演化机制。路径依赖和历史偶然性在形态复杂度的进化中起关键作用，句法复杂度可以在无需语义增益的情况下累积，这为理解中性进化与适应进化在生物复杂性中的作用提供了新视角。

Abstract: Attempts to quantify biological complexity often consider intrinsic structural properties at a chosen hierarchical level and resolution, such as counts of body parts and their degree of differentiation. These measures are inherently \emph{syntactic}, being concerned with the information needed to specify an arrangement rather than the biological functions performed. Syntactic complexity alone is therefore not sophisticated enough of a measure to fully address the role of complexity as either a driver or consequence of evolution. We propose to study the counterpart, \emph{semantic} complexity: the subset of structural features whose variation has a measurable effect on organismal fitness. We illustrate this distinction in a simple mathematical model of tagmosis with functional constraints, symmetry breaking, and specialisation. We find that the total syntactic complexity evolved as selection drives lineages toward globally optimal fitness is path-dependent, revealing two evolutionary modes: a driven mode, in which semantic and syntactic complexity rise together, and an entropic mode, in which syntactic complexity drifts upward under a near-neutral evolution. Historical contingencies in early specialisation, combined with multi-optima fitness landscapes, govern how long lineages stay in each mode. Those on paths that do not lead directly to the highest-fitness states remain in the driven mode for longer and can eventually reach comparable fitness, but only by evolving morphologies with greater syntactic complexity.

</details>


<div id='math.AT'></div>

# math.AT [[Back]](#toc)

### [124] [Syzygy Computations and the D(2)-Problem for the Metacyclic Group G(p,3)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15822)
*J. D. P. Evans,R. Sanchez Galan*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文研究以p=3d+1为素数且2和3生成乘法群条件下，亚循环群G(p,3)的整群环Λ上的合冲模Ω_r(ℤ)的张量积交互作用，并将其与Wall的D(2)问题联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨亚循环群整群环上合冲模的张量积结构，并揭示其与Wall经典D(2)问题之间的深层联系。

Method: 通过显式分析合冲模在−⊗_ℤ−作用下的交互关系，利用特定素数条件p=3d+1且2,3生成乘法群来简化结构，建立合冲模的稳定类与自由分解之间的关系。

Result: 得到了合冲模在张量积运算下的具体交互行为，并建立了与Wall D(2)问题的关联。

Conclusion: 该研究为理解亚循环群上合冲模的张量积性质提供了显式刻画，并为解决Wall的D(2)问题提供了新的代数视角。

Abstract: Let $p=3d+1$ be prime in which $(\mathbb{Z}/p\mathbb{Z})^\times=\langle 2,\,3\rangle$, and let $Λ=\mathbb{Z}[C_p\rtimes C_3]$ be the integral group ring of the metacyclic group $G(p,\,3)$ of order $3p$. The syzygies $Ω_r(\mathbb{Z})$ are the stable classes of intermediate modules in a free $Λ$-resolution of the trivial module. We discuss explicitly the interaction of the syzygies under $-\otimes_\mathbb{Z}-$ and relate this to Wall's $\mathcal{D}(2)$-problem.

</details>


### [125] [Some homotopy coherent finite group actions on spheres](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15979)
*Manuel Krannich,Oscar Randal-Williams*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文构造了球面上的一些同伦连贯光滑有限群作用实例，这些作用无法由严格作用实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上，有限群在球面上的作用通常考虑严格作用。本文旨在探索是否存在同伦连贯（homotopy coherent）但非严格的光滑有限群作用，以丰富对群作用分类的理解。

Method: 通过具体的构造方法，给出实例展示同伦连贯光滑有限群作用与严格作用之间的差异。

Result: 成功构造出一些球面上的同伦连贯光滑有限群作用，并证明它们不能归结为任何严格作用。

Conclusion: 存在同伦连贯但非严格的光滑有限群作用，这扩展了群作用理论的边界，对理解变换群及其分类具有重要意义。

Abstract: We provide some examples of homotopy coherent smooth finite group actions on spheres which do not arise from strict actions.

</details>
